# Complex event recognition meets hierarchical conjunctive queries

复杂事件识别遇上层次化合取查询

Dante Pinto ${}^{1}$ and Cristian Riveros ${}^{1}$

丹特·平托 ${}^{1}$ 与克里斯蒂安·里韦罗斯 ${}^{1}$

${}^{1}$ Pontificia Universidad Católica de Chile,drpinto1@uc.cl,cristian.riveros@uc.cl

${}^{1}$ 智利天主教大学,drpinto1@uc.cl,cristian.riveros@uc.cl

## Abstract

## 摘要

Hierarchical conjunctive queries (HCQ) are a subclass of conjunctive queries (CQ) with robust algorithmic properties. Among others, Berkholz, Keppeler, and Schweikardt have shown that HCQ is the subclass of CQ (without projection) that admits dynamic query evaluation with constant update time and constant delay enumeration. On a different but related setting stands Complex Event Recognition (CER), a prominent technology for evaluating sequence patterns over streams. Since one can interpret a data stream as an unbounded sequence of inserts in dynamic query evaluation, it is natural to ask to which extent CER can take advantage of HCQ to find a robust class of queries that can be evaluated efficiently.

层次化合取查询(HCQ)是具有强健算法特性的合取查询(CQ)子类。Berkholz、Keppeler和Schweikardt等人研究表明，HCQ是CQ中(无投影情况下)唯一支持恒定更新时间与恒定延迟枚举动态查询评估的子类。复杂事件识别(CER)作为流数据序列模式评估的重要技术，因其可将数据流视为动态查询评估中的无限插入序列，自然引发思考：CER能在多大程度上利用HCQ来建立高效评估的强健查询类？

In this paper, we search to combine HCQ with sequence patterns to find a class of CER queries that can get the best of both worlds. To reach this goal, we propose a class of complex event automata model called Parallelized Complex Event Automata (PCEA) for evaluating CER queries with correlation (i.e., joins) over streams. This model allows us to express sequence patterns and compare values among tuples, but it also allows us to express conjunctions by incorporating a novel form of non-determinism that we call parallelization. We show that for every HCQ (under bag semantics), we can construct an equivalent PCEA. Further, we show that HCQ is the biggest class of acyclic CQ that this automata model can define. Then, PCEA stands as a sweet spot that precisely expresses $\mathrm{{HCQ}}$ (i.e.,among acyclic $\mathrm{{CQ}}$ ) and extends them with sequence patterns. Finally,we show that PCEA also inherits the good algorithmic properties of HCQ by presenting a streaming evaluation algorithm under sliding windows with logarithmic update time and output-linear delay for the class of PCEA with equality predicates.

本文致力于融合HCQ与序列模式，构建兼具两者优势的CER查询类。为此我们提出并行化复杂事件自动机(PCEA)模型，支持流数据关联(即连接)的CER查询评估。该模型不仅能表达序列模式和元组值比较，还通过创新性"并行化"非确定性机制实现合取运算。我们证明每个HCQ(包语义下)都可构造等效PCEA，且HCQ是该自动机模型可定义的最大无环CQ类。因此PCEA精准表达了$\mathrm{{HCQ}}$(即无环$\mathrm{{CQ}}$中的查询类)并扩展了序列模式。最后，针对含等值谓词的PCEA类，我们提出滑动窗口下对数更新时间与输出线性延迟的流评估算法，证明PCEA继承了HCQ的优良算法特性。

## 1 Introduction

## 1 引言

Hierarchical Conjunctive Queries [12] (HCQ) are a subclass of Conjunctive Queries (CQ) with good algorithmic properties for dynamic query evaluation [9, 18]. In this scenario, users want to continuously evaluate a CQ over a database that receives insertion, updates, or deletes of tuples, and to efficiently retrieve the output after each modification. A landmark result by Berkholz, Keppeler, and Schweikardt [5] shows that HCQ are the subfragment among CQ for dynamic query evaluation. Specifically, they show one can evaluate every HCQ with constant update time and constant-delay enumeration. Furthermore, they show that HCQ are the only class of full CQ (i.e., CQ without projection) with such guarantees, namely, under fined-grained complexity assumptions, a full CQ can be evaluated with constant update time and constant delay enumeration if, and only if, the query is hierarchical. Therefore, HCQ stand as the fragment for efficient evaluation under a dynamic scenario (see also [18]).

层次化合取查询[12](HCQ)是具有动态查询评估优势的合取查询(CQ)子类[9,18]。该场景下用户需在持续接收元组增删改的数据库上连续评估CQ，并在每次修改后高效获取输出。Berkholz等人的里程碑成果[5]表明HCQ是动态评估中CQ的唯一理想子类——所有HCQ都支持恒定更新时间与恒定延迟枚举，且在细粒度复杂度假设下，无投影CQ当且仅当呈层次性时才具备该特性。因此HCQ成为动态场景下高效评估的标杆片段[18]。

Data stream processing is another dynamic scenario where we want to evaluate queries continuously but now over an unbounded sequence of tuples (i.e., a data stream). Complex Event Recognition (CER) is one such technology for processing information flow [14, 11. CER systems read high-velocity streams of data, called events, and evaluate expressive patterns for detecting complex events, a subset of relevant events that witness a critical case for a user. A singular aspect of CER compared to other frameworks is that the order of the stream's data matters, reflecting the temporal order of events in reality (see [27]). For this reason, sequencing operators are first citizens on CER query languages, which one combines with other operators, like filtering, disjunction, and correlation (i.e., joins), among others [4].

数据流处理是另一种动态场景，需对无限元组序列(即数据流)持续评估查询。复杂事件识别(CER)作为信息流处理技术[14,11]，通过读取高速事件流并评估表达性模式来检测反映用户关键案例的复杂事件(相关事件子集)。CER区别于其他框架的核心在于其重视数据流顺序(反映现实事件时序[27])，因此序列操作符与过滤、析取、关联(即连接)等操作符共同构成CER查询语言的基石[4]。

Similar to dynamic query evaluation, this work aims to find a class of CER query languages with efficient streaming query evaluation. Our strategy to pursue this goal is simple but effective: we use $\mathrm{{HCQ}}$ as a starting point to guide our search for CER query languages with good algorithmic properties. Since one can interpret a data stream as an unbounded sequence of inserts in dynamic query evaluation, we want to extend $\mathrm{{HCQ}}$ with sequencing while maintaining efficient evaluation. We plan this strategy from an algorithmic point of view. Instead of studying which CER query language fragments have such properties, we look for automata models that can express HCQ. By finding such a model, we can later design our CER query language to express these queries [17.

与动态查询评估相似，本研究旨在寻找支持高效流查询评估的CER查询语言类。我们采取的策略简明有效：以$\mathrm{{HCQ}}$为起点，寻找具有良好算法特性的CER查询语言。鉴于数据流可视为动态评估中的无限插入序列，我们计划在保持高效评估的同时为$\mathrm{{HCQ}}$扩展序列功能。从算法视角出发，我们不直接研究CER语言片段特性，而是寻找能表达HCQ的自动机模型，据此设计对应的CER查询语言[17]。

With this goal and strategy in mind, we start from the proposal of Chain Complex Event Automata (CCEA), an automata model for CER expressing sequencing queries with correlation, but that cannot express simple HCQ [16]. We extend this model with a new sort of non-deterministic power that we call parallelization. This feature allows us to run several parallel executions that start independently and to gather them together when reading new data items. We define the class of Parallelized Complex Event Automata (PCEA), the extension of CCEA with parallelization. As an extension, PCEA can express patterns with sequencing, disjunction, iteration, and correlation but also allows conjunction. In particular,we can show that PCEA can express an acyclic CQ $Q$ if,and only if, $Q$ is hierarchical. Then, PCEA is a sweet spot that precisely expresses HCQ (i.e., among acyclic CQ) and extends them with sequencing and other operations. Moreover, we show that PCEA inherits the good algorithmic properties of HCQ by presenting a streaming evaluation algorithm under sliding windows, reaching our desired goal.

基于这一目标和策略，我们从链式复杂事件自动机(CCEA)的提案出发，这是一种用于CER的自动机模型，能表达带关联性的序列查询，但无法表达简单层次化查询(HCQ)[16]。我们通过引入称为"并行化"的新型非确定性能力扩展该模型。该特性允许启动多个独立执行的并行线程，并在读取新数据项时将其汇聚。我们定义了并行化复杂事件自动机(PCEA)类别，即支持并行化的CCEA扩展版本。作为扩展模型，PCEA不仅能表达含序列、析取、迭代和关联的模式，还支持合取操作。特别地，我们证明PCEA能表达无环合取查询(CQ)$Q$当且仅当$Q$是层次化查询。因此PCEA成为能精确表达HCQ（即无环CQ的子集）并扩展序列化等操作的理想模型。此外，通过提出滑动窗口下的流式评估算法，我们证明PCEA继承了HCQ的良好算法特性，达成了预期目标。

Contributions. The technical contributions and outline of the paper are the following.

贡献。本文的技术贡献与框架如下。

In Section 2, we provide some basic definitions plus recalling the definition of CCEA.

第2节提供基础定义并回顾CCEA的定义。

In Section 3, we introduce the concept of parallelization for standard non-deterministic NFA, called PFA, and study their properties. We show that PFA can be determinized in exponential time (similar to NFA) (Proposition 3.2). We then apply this notion to CER and define the class of PCEA, showing that it is strictly more expressive than CCEA (Proposition 3.4).

第3节引入标准非确定性有限自动机(NFA)的并行化概念（称为PFA），研究其性质。我们证明PFA可在指数时间内确定化（类似于NFA）（命题3.2）。随后将该概念应用于CER领域，定义PCEA类别，证明其表达能力严格强于CCEA（命题3.4）。

Section 4 compares PCEA with HCQ under bag semantics. Given that PCEA runs over streams and HCQ over relational databases, we must revisit the semantics of HCQ and formalize in which sense an HCQ and a PCEA define the same query. We show that under such comparison,every HCQ $Q$ under bag semantics can be expressed by a PCEA with equality predicates of exponential size in $\left| Q\right|$ and of quadratic size if $Q$ does not have self joins (Theorem 4.1). Furthermore,if $Q$ is acyclic but not hierarchical,then $Q$ cannot be defined by any PCEA (Theorem 4.2).

第4节在包语义下比较PCEA与HCQ。鉴于PCEA运行于数据流而HCQ基于关系数据库，我们重新审视HCQ语义，形式化定义HCQ与PCEA表达相同查询的判定标准。研究表明：在此比较框架下，包语义下的每个HCQ$Q$都可由带等值谓词的PCEA表达，其规模在$\left| Q\right|$上呈指数级；若$Q$无自连接则降为二次方（定理4.1）。进一步证明：若$Q$是无环非层次化查询，则任何PCEA都无法定义它（定理4.2）。

In Section 5, we study the evaluation of PCEA in a streaming scenario. Specifically, we present a streaming evaluation algorithm under a sliding window with logarithmic update time and output-linear delay for the class of unambiguous PCEA with equality predicates (Theorem 5.1).

第5节研究PCEA在流式场景中的评估。具体而言，我们提出针对带等值谓词的无歧义PCEA类的流式评估算法，在滑动窗口下实现对数级更新时间与输出线性延迟（定理5.1）。

Related work. Dynamic query evaluation of HCQ and acyclic CQ has been studied in [5, 18, 28, 20]. This research line did not study HCQ or acyclic CQ in the presence of order predicates. [29, 26] studied CQ under comparisons (i.e., $\theta$ -joins) but in a static setting (i.e.,no updates). The closest work is [19], which studied dynamic query evaluation of CQ with comparisons; however, this work did not study well-behaved classes of HCQ with comparisons, and, further, their algorithms have update time linear in the data.

相关工作。HCQ和无环CQ的动态查询评估已在[5,18,28,20]中研究。该方向未探讨含顺序谓词的HCQ或无环CQ。[29,26]研究了带比较运算的CQ（即$\theta$-连接），但属于静态环境（无更新）。最接近的是[19]，研究带比较运算的CQ动态评估，但未涉及行为良好的带比较HCQ类别，且其算法更新时间与数据规模呈线性关系。

Complex event recognition and, more generally, data stream processing have studied the evaluation of joins over streams (see, e.g., 31, 30, 21). To the best of our knowledge, no work in this research line optimizes queries focused on $\mathrm{{HCQ}}$ or provides guarantees regarding update time or enumeration delay in this setting. We base our work on [16], which we will discuss extensively.

复杂事件识别及更广义的数据流处理领域已研究流式连接运算（参见[31,30,21]）。据我们所知，该方向尚无工作针对$\mathrm{{HCQ}}$优化查询，或在此场景下提供更新时间与枚举延迟的保证。我们的研究基于[16]的成果，后文将详细讨论。

## 2 Preliminaries

## 2 预备知识

Strings and NFA. A string is a sequence of elements $\bar{s} = {a}_{0}\ldots {a}_{n - 1}$ . For presentation purposes,we make no distinction between a sequence or a string and,thus,we also write $\bar{s} = {a}_{0},\ldots ,{a}_{n - 1}$ for denoting a string. We will denote strings using a bar and its $i$ -th element by $\bar{s}\left\lbrack  i\right\rbrack   = {a}_{i}$ . We use $\left| \bar{s}\right|  = n$ for the length of $\bar{s}$ and $\{ \bar{s}\}  = \left\{  {{a}_{0},\ldots ,{a}_{n - 1}}\right\}$ to consider $\bar{s}$ as a set. Given two strings $\bar{s}$ and ${\bar{s}}^{\prime }$ ,we write $\bar{s}{\bar{s}}^{\prime }$ for the concatenation of $\bar{s}$ followed by ${\bar{s}}^{\prime }$ . Further,we say that ${\bar{s}}^{\prime }$ is a prefix of $\bar{s}$ ,written as $\overline{{s}^{\prime }}{ \leq  }_{p}\bar{s}$ ,if $\left| {\bar{s}}^{\prime }\right|  \leq  \left| \bar{s}\right|$ and ${\bar{s}}^{\prime }\left\lbrack  i\right\rbrack   = \bar{s}\left\lbrack  i\right\rbrack$ for all $i < \left| {\bar{s}}^{\prime }\right|$ . Given a non-empty set $\sum$ we denote by ${\sum }^{ * }$ the set of all strings from elements in $\sum$ ,where $\epsilon  \in  {\sum }^{ * }$ denotes the 0 -length string. For a function $f : \sum  \rightarrow  \Omega$ and $\bar{s} \in  {\sum }^{ * }$ ,we write $f\left( \bar{s}\right)  = f\left( {a}_{0}\right) \ldots f\left( {a}_{n - 1}\right)$ to denote the point-wise application of $f$ over $\bar{s}$ .

字符串与非确定有限自动机(NFA)。字符串是由元素$\bar{s} = {a}_{0}\ldots {a}_{n - 1}$构成的序列。为表述统一，我们不对序列与字符串作区分，因此也用$\bar{s} = {a}_{0},\ldots ,{a}_{n - 1}$表示字符串。我们将用带横线的符号表示字符串，其$i$第个元素记作$\bar{s}\left\lbrack  i\right\rbrack   = {a}_{i}$。$\left| \bar{s}\right|  = n$表示$\bar{s}$的长度，$\{ \bar{s}\}  = \left\{  {{a}_{0},\ldots ,{a}_{n - 1}}\right\}$表示将$\bar{s}$视为集合。给定两个字符串$\bar{s}$与${\bar{s}}^{\prime }$，$\bar{s}{\bar{s}}^{\prime }$表示$\bar{s}$与${\bar{s}}^{\prime }$的连接运算。若$\left| {\bar{s}}^{\prime }\right|  \leq  \left| \bar{s}\right|$且对所有$i < \left| {\bar{s}}^{\prime }\right|$满足${\bar{s}}^{\prime }\left\lbrack  i\right\rbrack   = \bar{s}\left\lbrack  i\right\rbrack$，则称${\bar{s}}^{\prime }$是$\bar{s}$的前缀，记作$\overline{{s}^{\prime }}{ \leq  }_{p}\bar{s}$。对于非空集合$\sum$，${\sum }^{ * }$表示由$\sum$中元素构成的所有字符串集合，其中$\epsilon  \in  {\sum }^{ * }$表示零长度字符串。对于函数$f : \sum  \rightarrow  \Omega$和$\bar{s} \in  {\sum }^{ * }$，$f\left( \bar{s}\right)  = f\left( {a}_{0}\right) \ldots f\left( {a}_{n - 1}\right)$表示$f$在$\bar{s}$上的逐点应用。

A Non-deterministic Finite Automaton (NFA) is a tuple $\mathcal{A} = \left( {Q,\sum ,\Delta ,I,F}\right)$ such that $Q$ is a finite set of states, $\sum$ is a finite alphabet, $\Delta  \subseteq  Q \times  \sum  \times  Q$ is the transition relation,and $I$ and $F$ are the set of initial and final states,respectively. A run of $\mathcal{A}$ over a string $\bar{s} = {a}_{0}\ldots {a}_{n - 1} \in  {\sum }^{ * }$ is a non-empty sequence ${p}_{0}\ldots {p}_{n}$ such that ${p}_{0} \in  I$ ,and $\left( {{p}_{i},{a}_{i},{p}_{i + 1}}\right)  \in  \Delta$ for every $i < n$ . We say that $\mathcal{A}$ accepts a string $\bar{s} \in  {\sum }^{ * }$ iff there exists such a run of $\mathcal{A}$ over $\bar{s}$ such that ${p}_{n} \in  F$ . We define the language $\mathcal{L}\left( \mathcal{A}\right)  \subseteq  {\sum }^{ * }$ of all strings accepted by $\mathcal{A}$ . Finally we say that $\mathcal{A}$ is a Deterministic Finite Automaton (DFA) iff $\Delta$ is given as a partial function $\Delta  : Q \times  \sum  \rightarrow  Q$ and $\left| I\right|  = 1$ .

非确定有限自动机(NFA)是一个元组$\mathcal{A} = \left( {Q,\sum ,\Delta ,I,F}\right)$，其中$Q$是有限状态集，$\sum$是有限字母表，$\Delta  \subseteq  Q \times  \sum  \times  Q$是转移关系，$I$与$F$分别表示初始状态集和终止状态集。NFA$\mathcal{A}$在字符串$\bar{s} = {a}_{0}\ldots {a}_{n - 1} \in  {\sum }^{ * }$上的运行是指非空序列${p}_{0}\ldots {p}_{n}$满足${p}_{0} \in  I$，且对每个$i < n$有$\left( {{p}_{i},{a}_{i},{p}_{i + 1}}\right)  \in  \Delta$。当存在$\mathcal{A}$在$\bar{s}$上的运行使得${p}_{n} \in  F$时，称$\mathcal{A}$接受字符串$\bar{s} \in  {\sum }^{ * }$。定义$\mathcal{A}$接受的所有字符串构成的语言为$\mathcal{L}\left( \mathcal{A}\right)  \subseteq  {\sum }^{ * }$。若$\Delta$是部分函数$\Delta  : Q \times  \sum  \rightarrow  Q$且$\left| I\right|  = 1$，则称$\mathcal{A}$为确定有限自动机(DFA)。

Schemas,tuples,and streams. Fix a set $\mathbf{D}$ of data values. A relational schema $\sigma$ (or just schema) is a pair(T,arity)where $\mathbf{T}$ are the relation names and arity : $\mathbf{T} \rightarrow  \mathbb{N}$ maps each name to a number,that is,its arity. An $R$ -tuple of $\sigma$ (or just a tuple) is an object $R\left( {{a}_{0},\ldots ,{a}_{k - 1}}\right)$ such that $R \in  \mathbf{T}$ ,each ${a}_{i} \in  \mathbf{D}$ , and $k = \operatorname{arity}\left( R\right)$ . We will write $R\left( \bar{a}\right)$ to denote a tuple with values $\bar{a}$ . We denote by Tuples $\lceil \sigma \rceil$ the set of all $R$ -tuples of all $R \in  \mathbf{T}$ . We define the size of a tuple $R\left( \bar{a}\right)$ as $\left| {R\left( \bar{a}\right) }\right|  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 0}}^{{k - 1}}\left| {\bar{a}\left\lbrack  i\right\rbrack  }\right|$ with $k = \operatorname{arity}\left( R\right)$ where $\left| {\bar{a}\left\lbrack  i\right\rbrack  }\right|$ is the size of the data value $\bar{a}\left\lbrack  i\right\rbrack   \in  \mathbf{D}$ ,which depends on the domain.

模式(Schemas)、元组(tuples)与流(streams)。给定一组数据值集合$\mathbf{D}$。关系模式$\sigma$（简称模式）是由(T,arity)构成的二元组，其中$\mathbf{T}$表示关系名，而arity:$\mathbf{T} \rightarrow  \mathbb{N}$将每个名称映射到一个数值（即其元数）。$\sigma$的$R$-元组（简称元组）是满足$R \in  \mathbf{T}$的对象$R\left( {{a}_{0},\ldots ,{a}_{k - 1}}\right)$，其中每个${a}_{i} \in  \mathbf{D}$且$k = \operatorname{arity}\left( R\right)$。我们用$R\left( \bar{a}\right)$表示值为$\bar{a}$的元组，Tuples$\lceil \sigma \rceil$表示所有$R \in  \mathbf{T}$的$R$-元组集合。定义元组$R\left( \bar{a}\right)$的大小为$\left| {R\left( \bar{a}\right) }\right|  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 0}}^{{k - 1}}\left| {\bar{a}\left\lbrack  i\right\rbrack  }\right|$，其中$k = \operatorname{arity}\left( R\right)$且$\left| {\bar{a}\left\lbrack  i\right\rbrack  }\right|$表示数据值$\bar{a}\left\lbrack  i\right\rbrack   \in  \mathbf{D}$的大小（具体取决于定义域）。

A stream $\mathcal{S}$ over $\sigma$ is an infinite sequence of tuples $\mathcal{S} = {t}_{0}{t}_{1}{t}_{2}\ldots$ such that ${t}_{i} \in  \operatorname{Tuples}\left\lbrack  \sigma \right\rbrack$ for every $i \geq  0$ . For a running example,consider the schema ${\sigma }_{0}$ with relation names $\mathbf{T} = \{ R,S,T\}$ ,arity $\left( R\right)  = \operatorname{arity}\left( S\right)  = 2$ and arity $\left( T\right)  = 1$ . A stream ${\mathcal{S}}_{0}$ over ${\sigma }_{0}$ could be the following:

在$\sigma$上的流(stream)$\mathcal{S}$是元组序列$\mathcal{S} = {t}_{0}{t}_{1}{t}_{2}\ldots$的无限序列，满足对每个$i \geq  0$都有${t}_{i} \in  \operatorname{Tuples}\left\lbrack  \sigma \right\rbrack$。举例说明：考虑具有关系名$\mathbf{T} = \{ R,S,T\}$、元数$\left( R\right)  = \operatorname{arity}\left( S\right)  = 2$和$\left( T\right)  = 1$的模式${\sigma }_{0}$，其上的流${\mathcal{S}}_{0}$可能如下所示：

where we add an index (i.e.,the position) below each tuple (for simplification,we use $\mathbf{D} = \mathbb{N}$ ).

（为简化表示，我们在每个元组下方添加索引（即位置编号），使用$\mathbf{D} = \mathbb{N}$作为占位符）

Predicates. For a fix $k$ ,a $k$ -predicate $P$ is a subset of Tuples ${\left\lbrack  \sigma \right\rbrack  }^{k}$ . Further,we say that $\bar{t} = \left( {{t}_{1},\ldots ,{t}_{k}}\right)$ satisfies $P$ iff $\bar{t} \in  P$ . We say that $P$ is unary if $k = 1$ and binary if $k = 2$ . In the following,we denote any class of unary or binary predicates by $\mathbf{U}$ or $\mathbf{B}$ ,respectively.

谓词(Predicates)。对于固定$k$，$k$-谓词$P$是Tuples${\left\lbrack  \sigma \right\rbrack  }^{k}$的子集。当$\bar{t} \in  P$时，称$\bar{t} = \left( {{t}_{1},\ldots ,{t}_{k}}\right)$满足$P$。若$k = 1$则称$P$为一元谓词，若$k = 2$则为二元谓词。下文分别用$\mathbf{U}$和$\mathbf{B}$表示任意一元或二元谓词类。

Although we define our automata models for any class of unary and binary predicates, the following two predicate classes will be relevant for algorithmic purposes (see Section 4 and 5). Let $\sigma$ be a schema. We denote by ${\mathbf{U}}_{\text{lin }}$ the class of all unary predicates $U$ such that,for every $t \in  \operatorname{Tuples}\left\lbrack  \sigma \right\rbrack$ ,one can decide in linear time over $\left| t\right|$ whether $t$ satisfies $U$ or not. In addition,we denote by ${\mathbf{B}}_{\mathrm{{eq}}}$ the class of all equality predicates defined as follow: a binary predicate $B$ is an equality predicate iff there exist partial functions $\bar{B}$ and $\overrightarrow{B}$ over Tuples $\left\lbrack  \sigma \right\rbrack$ such that,for every ${t}_{1},{t}_{2} \in$ Tuples $\left\lbrack  \sigma \right\rbrack  ,\left( {{t}_{1},{t}_{2}}\right)  \in  B$ iff $\bar{B}\left( {t}_{1}\right)$ and $\overrightarrow{B}\left( {t}_{2}\right)$ are defined and $\bar{B}\left( {t}_{1}\right)  = \bar{B}\left( {t}_{2}\right)$ . Further,we require that one can compute $\bar{B}\left( {t}_{1}\right)$ and $\bar{B}\left( {t}_{2}\right)$ in linear time over $\left| {t}_{1}\right|$ and $\left| {t}_{2}\right|$ ,respectively. For example,recall our schema ${\sigma }_{0}$ and consider the binary predicate $\left( {{Tx},{Sxy}}\right)  = \{ \left( {T\left( a\right) ,S\left( {a,b}\right) }\right)  \mid  a,b \in  \mathbf{D}\}$ . Then by using the functions $\bar{B}\left( {T\left( a\right) }\right)  = a$ and $\overrightarrow{B}\left( {S\left( {a,b}\right) }\right)  = a$ , one can check that(Tx,Sxy)is an equality predicate.

尽管我们为任意一元和二元谓词类定义了自动机模型，但以下两个谓词类对算法研究具有重要意义（参见第4和第5节）。设$\sigma$为模式，用${\mathbf{U}}_{\text{lin }}$表示所有满足条件的一元谓词$U$类：对于每个$t \in  \operatorname{Tuples}\left\lbrack  \sigma \right\rbrack$，可在$\left| t\right|$的线性时间内判定$t$是否满足$U$。此外，用${\mathbf{B}}_{\mathrm{{eq}}}$表示按如下方式定义的所有等价谓词类：二元谓词$B$是等价谓词当且仅当存在元组$\left\lbrack  \sigma \right\rbrack$上的部分函数$\bar{B}$和$\overrightarrow{B}$，使得对于任意${t}_{1},{t}_{2} \in$元组$\left\lbrack  \sigma \right\rbrack  ,\left( {{t}_{1},{t}_{2}}\right)  \in  B$，当且仅当$\bar{B}\left( {t}_{1}\right)$与$\overrightarrow{B}\left( {t}_{2}\right)$均有定义且$\bar{B}\left( {t}_{1}\right)  = \bar{B}\left( {t}_{2}\right)$。进一步要求能分别在$\left| {t}_{1}\right|$和$\left| {t}_{2}\right|$的线性时间内计算出$\bar{B}\left( {t}_{1}\right)$和$\bar{B}\left( {t}_{2}\right)$。例如，回顾模式${\sigma }_{0}$并考虑二元谓词$\left( {{Tx},{Sxy}}\right)  = \{ \left( {T\left( a\right) ,S\left( {a,b}\right) }\right)  \mid  a,b \in  \mathbf{D}\}$，通过函数$\bar{B}\left( {T\left( a\right) }\right)  = a$和$\overrightarrow{B}\left( {S\left( {a,b}\right) }\right)  = a$可验证(Tx,Sxy)属于等价谓词。

Note that ${\mathbf{B}}_{\mathrm{{eq}}}$ is a more general class of equality predicates compared with the ones used in [16],that will serve in our automata models for comparing tuples by "equality" in different subsets of attributes. We take here a more semantic presentation, where the equality comparison between tuples is directly given by the functions $\bar{B}$ and $\bar{B}$ and not symbolically by some formula.

需注意${\mathbf{B}}_{\mathrm{{eq}}}$是比文献[16]所用更广义的等价谓词类，这类谓词将在我们的自动机模型中用于通过属性不同子集的"等价性"来比较元组。本文采用更具语义性的表述方式，元组间的等价比较直接由函数$\bar{B}$和$\bar{B}$给出，而非通过符号化公式表示。

Chain complex event automata. A Chain Complex Event Automaton (CCEA) [16] is a tuple $\mathcal{C}$ = $\left( {Q,\mathbf{U},\mathbf{B},\Omega ,\Delta ,I,F}\right)$ where $Q$ is a finite set of states, $\mathbf{U}$ is a set of unary predicates, $\mathbf{B}$ is a set of binary predicates, $\Omega$ is a finite set of labels, $I : Q \rightarrow  \mathbf{U} \times  \left( {{2}^{\Omega }\smallsetminus \{ \varnothing \} }\right)$ is a partial initial function, $F \subseteq  Q$ is the set of final states,and $\Delta$ is a finite transition relation of the form: $\Delta  \subseteq  Q \times  \mathbf{U} \times  \mathbf{B} \times  \left( {{2}^{\Omega }\smallsetminus \{ \varnothing \} }\right)  \times  Q$ . Let $\mathcal{S} = {t}_{0}{t}_{1}\ldots$ be a stream. A configuration of $\mathcal{C}$ over $\mathcal{S}$ is a tuple $\left( {p,i,L}\right)  \in  Q \times  \mathbb{N} \times  \left( {{2}^{\Omega }\smallsetminus \{ \varnothing \} }\right)$ ,representing that the automaton $\mathcal{C}$ ,is at state $p$ after having read and marked ${t}_{i}$ with the set of labels $L$ . For $\ell  \in  \Omega$ , we say that(p,i,L)marked position $i$ with $\ell$ iff $\ell  \in  L$ . Given a position $n \in  \mathbb{N}$ ,we say that a configuration is accepting at position $n$ iff it is of the form(p,n,L)and $p \in  F$ . Then a run $\rho$ of $\mathcal{C}$ over $\mathcal{S}$ is a sequence of configurations:

链式复合事件自动机。链式复合事件自动机(Chain Complex Event Automaton, CCEA)[16]是一个元组$\mathcal{C}$=$\left( {Q,\mathbf{U},\mathbf{B},\Omega ,\Delta ,I,F}\right)$，其中$Q$是有限状态集合，$\mathbf{U}$是一元谓词集合，$\mathbf{B}$是二元谓词集合，$\Omega$是有限标签集合，$I : Q \rightarrow  \mathbf{U} \times  \left( {{2}^{\Omega }\smallsetminus \{ \varnothing \} }\right)$是部分初始函数，$F \subseteq  Q$是终止状态集合，$\Delta$是形如$\Delta  \subseteq  Q \times  \mathbf{U} \times  \mathbf{B} \times  \left( {{2}^{\Omega }\smallsetminus \{ \varnothing \} }\right)  \times  Q$的有限转移关系。设$\mathcal{S} = {t}_{0}{t}_{1}\ldots$为流数据。自动机$\mathcal{C}$在$\mathcal{S}$上的配置是一个元组$\left( {p,i,L}\right)  \in  Q \times  \mathbb{N} \times  \left( {{2}^{\Omega }\smallsetminus \{ \varnothing \} }\right)$，表示该自动机在读取并用标签集$L$标记${t}_{i}$后处于状态$p$。对于$\ell  \in  \Omega$，当且仅当$\ell  \in  L$时，称(p,i,L)用$\ell$标记了位置$i$。给定位置$n \in  \mathbb{N}$，当配置呈(p,n,L)形式且$p \in  F$时，称该配置在位置$n$可接受。于是自动机$\mathcal{C}$在$\mathcal{S}$上的运行$\rho$就是配置序列：

$$
\rho  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left( {{p}_{0},{i}_{0},{L}_{0}}\right) ,\left( {{p}_{1},{i}_{1},{L}_{1}}\right) ,\ldots ,\left( {{p}_{n},{i}_{n},{L}_{n}}\right) 
$$

such that ${i}_{0} < {i}_{1} < \ldots  < {i}_{n},I\left( {p}_{0}\right)  = \left( {U,{L}_{0}}\right)$ is defined and ${t}_{{i}_{0}} \in  U$ ,and there exists a transition $\left( {{p}_{j - 1},{U}_{j},{B}_{j},{L}_{j},{p}_{j}}\right)  \in  \Delta$ such that ${t}_{{i}_{j}} \in  {U}_{j}$ and $\left( {{t}_{{i}_{j - 1}},{t}_{{i}_{j}}}\right)  \in  {B}_{j}$ for every $j \in  \left\lbrack  {1,n}\right\rbrack$ . Intuitively,a run of a CCEA is a subsequence of the stream that can follow a path of transitions, where each transition checks a local condition (i.e.,the unary predicate $\left. {U}_{j}\right)$ and a join condition (i.e.,the binary predicate $\left. {B}_{j}\right)$ with the previous tuple. For the first tuple, a CCEA can only check a local condition (i.e., there is no previous tuple).

需满足${i}_{0} < {i}_{1} < \ldots  < {i}_{n},I\left( {p}_{0}\right)  = \left( {U,{L}_{0}}\right)$已定义且${t}_{{i}_{0}} \in  U$，同时存在转移$\left( {{p}_{j - 1},{U}_{j},{B}_{j},{L}_{j},{p}_{j}}\right)  \in  \Delta$使得对每个$j \in  \left\lbrack  {1,n}\right\rbrack$都有${t}_{{i}_{j}} \in  {U}_{j}$和$\left( {{t}_{{i}_{j - 1}},{t}_{{i}_{j}}}\right)  \in  {B}_{j}$。直观而言，CCEA的运行是流数据中能遵循转移路径的子序列，其中每次转移都会检查局部条件（即一元谓词$\left. {U}_{j}\right)$）和连接条件（即与前一个元组的二元谓词$\left. {B}_{j}\right)$）。对于首个元组，CCEA仅能检查局部条件（即不存在前驱元组）。

Given a run $\rho$ like above,we define its valuation ${\nu }_{\rho } : \Omega  \rightarrow  {2}^{\mathbb{N}}$ such that ${\nu }_{\rho }\left( \ell \right)$ is the set consisting of all positions in $\rho$ marked by $\ell$ ,formally, ${\nu }_{\rho }\left( \ell \right)  = \left\{  {{i}_{j} \mid  j \leq  n \land  \ell  \in  {L}_{j}}\right\}$ . Further,given a position $n \in  \mathbb{N}$ ,we say that $\rho$ is an accepting run at position $n$ iff $\left( {{p}_{n},{i}_{n},{L}_{n}}\right)$ is an accepting configuration at $n$ . Then the output of $\mathcal{C}$ over $\mathcal{S}$ at position $n$ is defined as:

给定如上所述的运行$\rho$，我们定义其估值${\nu }_{\rho } : \Omega  \rightarrow  {2}^{\mathbb{N}}$使得${\nu }_{\rho }\left( \ell \right)$是由$\rho$中所有被$\ell$标记的位置组成的集合，形式化表示为${\nu }_{\rho }\left( \ell \right)  = \left\{  {{i}_{j} \mid  j \leq  n \land  \ell  \in  {L}_{j}}\right\}$。进一步地，给定位置$n \in  \mathbb{N}$，当且仅当$\left( {{p}_{n},{i}_{n},{L}_{n}}\right)$在位置$n$处为接受配置时，我们称$\rho$在位置$n$处是接受运行。那么$\mathcal{C}$在$\mathcal{S}$上于位置$n$处的输出定义为：

$$
{\left\lbrack  \mathcal{C}\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  = \left\{  {{\nu }_{\rho } \mid  \rho \text{ is an accepting run at position }n\text{ of }\mathcal{C}\text{ over }S}\right\}  .
$$

Example 2.1. Below,we show an example of a CCEA over the schema ${\sigma }_{0}$ with $\Omega  = \{  \bullet  \}$ :

示例2.1 下面我们展示一个基于模式${\sigma }_{0}$且具有$\Omega  = \{  \bullet  \}$的CCEA实例：

<!-- Media -->

<!-- figureText: ${\mathcal{C}}_{0} : \;\overset{T/ \bullet  }{ \rightarrow  }\overset{⏜}{\left( {q}_{0}\right) }\;\overset{S,\left( {{Tx},{Sxy}}\right) / \bullet  }{ \rightarrow  }\overset{⏜}{\left( {q}_{1}\right) }\;\overset{⏜}{\left( Sxy,Rxy\right) }/ \bullet  \overset{⏜}{\left( {q}_{2}\right) }$ -->

<img src="https://cdn.noedgeai.com/01962f52-ddb8-725d-9fa0-a2c6fbb368f4_3.jpg?x=402&y=410&w=850&h=81&r=0"/>

<!-- Media -->

We use $T$ to denote the predicate $T = \{ T\left( a\right)  \mid  a \in  \mathbf{D}\}$ and similar for $S$ and $R$ . Further,we use (Tx,Sxy)and(Sxy,Rxy)to denote equality predicates as defined above. An accepting run of ${\mathcal{C}}_{0}$ over ${\mathcal{S}}_{0}$ is $\rho  = \left( {{q}_{0},1,\{  \bullet  \} }\right) ,\left( {{q}_{1},3,\{  \bullet  \} }\right) ,\left( {{q}_{2},5,\{  \bullet  \} }\right)$ which produces the valuation ${\nu }_{\rho } = \{  \bullet   \mapsto  \{ 1,3,5\} \}$ that represents the subsequence $T\left( 2\right) ,S\left( {2,{11}}\right) ,R\left( {2,{11}}\right)$ of ${\mathcal{S}}_{0}$ . Intuitively, ${\mathcal{C}}_{0}$ defines all subsequences of the form $T\left( a\right) ,S\left( {a,b}\right) ,R\left( {a,b}\right)$ for every $a,b \in  \mathbf{D}$ .

我们用$T$表示谓词$T = \{ T\left( a\right)  \mid  a \in  \mathbf{D}\}$，同理适用于$S$和$R$。此外，使用(Tx,Sxy)和(Sxy,Rxy)表示上述定义的等价谓词。${\mathcal{C}}_{0}$在${\mathcal{S}}_{0}$上的接受运行是$\rho  = \left( {{q}_{0},1,\{  \bullet  \} }\right) ,\left( {{q}_{1},3,\{  \bullet  \} }\right) ,\left( {{q}_{2},5,\{  \bullet  \} }\right)$，它生成表示${\mathcal{S}}_{0}$子序列$T\left( 2\right) ,S\left( {2,{11}}\right) ,R\left( {2,{11}}\right)$的估值${\nu }_{\rho } = \{  \bullet   \mapsto  \{ 1,3,5\} \}$。直观上，${\mathcal{C}}_{0}$为每个$a,b \in  \mathbf{D}$定义了形式为$T\left( a\right) ,S\left( {a,b}\right) ,R\left( {a,b}\right)$的所有子序列。

Note that the definition of CCEA above differs from [16] to fit our purpose better. Specifically, we use a set of labels $\Omega$ to annotate positions in the streams and define valuations in the same spirit as the model of annotated automata used in [3, 23. One can see this extension as a generalization to the model in [16],where $\left| \Omega \right|  = 1$ . This extension will be helpful to enrich the outputs of our models for comparing them with hierarchical conjunctive queries with self-joins (see Section 4).

需注意，上述CCEA的定义与文献[16]不同以更好地满足我们的需求。具体而言，我们使用标签集$\Omega$来标注数据流中的位置，并按照文献[3,23]中标注自动机模型的思路定义估值。可以将此扩展视为对文献[16]模型的泛化，其中$\left| \Omega \right|  = 1$。该扩展有助于丰富模型输出，以便与带自连接的分层合取查询进行比较（参见第4节）。

Computational model. For our algorithms, we assume the computational model of Random Access Machines (RAM) with uniform cost measure, and addition as it basic operation [1, 15]. This RAM has read-only registers for the input, read-writes registers for the work, and write-only registers for the output. This computation model is a standard assumption in the literature [5, 6].

计算模型。对于算法设计，我们采用具有统一成本度量、以加法为基本操作的随机存取存储器(RAM)模型[1,15]。该RAM包含只读输入寄存器、读写工作寄存器及只写输出寄存器。此计算模型是文献[5,6]中的标准假设。

## 3 Parallelized complex event automata

## 3 并行化复杂事件自动机

This section presents our automata model for specifying CER queries with conjunction called Parallelized Complex Event Automata (PCEA), which strictly generalized CCEA by adding a new feature called parallelization. For the sake of presentation, we first formalize the notion of parallelization for NFA to extend the idea to CCEA. Before this, we need the notation of labeled trees that will be useful for our definitions and proofs.

本节提出用于指定带合取的CER查询的自动机模型——并行化复杂事件自动机(PCEA)，其通过新增并行化特性严格泛化了CCEA。为便于阐述，我们首先形式化NFA的并行化概念，再将此思想扩展至CCEA。在此之前，需要引入标记树的概念以支持后续定义和证明。

Labeled trees. As it is common in the area [24], we define (unordered) trees as a finite set of strings $t \subseteq  {\mathbb{N}}^{ * }$ that satisfies two conditions: (1) $t$ contains the empty string,(i.e., $\varepsilon  \in  t$ ),and (2) $t$ is a prefix-closed set,namely,if ${a}_{1}\ldots {a}_{n} \in  t$ ,then ${a}_{1}\ldots {a}_{j} \in  t$ for every $j < n$ . We will refer to the string of $t$ as nodes,and the root of a tree,root(t),will be the empty string $\varepsilon$ .

标记树。如该领域常见做法[24]，我们将（无序）树定义为满足以下两个条件的有限字符串集合$t \subseteq  {\mathbb{N}}^{ * }$：(1) $t$包含空字符串（即$\varepsilon  \in  t$），(2) $t$是前缀封闭集，即若${a}_{1}\ldots {a}_{n} \in  t$，则对于任意$j < n$都有${a}_{1}\ldots {a}_{j} \in  t$。我们将$t$中的字符串称为节点，树的根节点root(t)即为空字符串$\varepsilon$。

Let $\bar{u},\bar{v} \in  t$ be nodes. The depth of $\bar{u}$ will be given by its length ${\operatorname{depth}}_{t}\left( \bar{u}\right)  = \left| \bar{u}\right|$ . We say that $\bar{u}$ is the parent of $\bar{v}$ and write parent ${}_{t}\left( \bar{v}\right)  = \bar{u}$ if $\bar{v} = \bar{u} \cdot  n$ for some $n \in  \mathbb{N}$ . Likewise,we say that $\bar{v}$ is a child of $\bar{u}$ if $\bar{u}$ is the parent of $\bar{v}$ and define children ${}_{t}\left( \bar{u}\right)  = \left\{  {\bar{v} \in  t \mid  {\operatorname{parent}}_{t}\left( \bar{v}\right)  = \bar{u}}\right.$ . Similarly,we define the descendants of $\bar{u}$ as ${\operatorname{desc}}_{t}\left( \bar{u}\right)  = \left\{  {\bar{v} \in  t \mid  \bar{u}{ \leq  }_{p}\bar{v}}\right\}$ and the ancestors as ${\operatorname{ancst}}_{t}\left( \bar{u}\right)  = \left\{  {\bar{v} \in  t \mid  \bar{v}{ \leq  }_{p}\bar{u}}\right\}$ ; note that $\bar{u} \in  {\operatorname{desc}}_{t}\left( \bar{u}\right)$ and $\bar{u} \in  {\operatorname{ancst}}_{t}\left( \bar{u}\right)$ . A node $\bar{u}$ is a leaf of $t$ if ${\operatorname{desc}}_{t}\left( \bar{u}\right)  = \{ \bar{u}\}$ ,and an inner node if it is not a leaf node. We define the set of leaves of $\bar{u}$ as ${\operatorname{leaves}}_{t}\left( \bar{u}\right)  = \left\{  {\bar{v} \in  {\operatorname{desc}}_{t}\left( \bar{u}\right)  \mid  \bar{v}}\right.$ is a leaf node $\}$ .

设$\bar{u},\bar{v} \in  t$为节点。$\bar{u}$的深度由其长度${\operatorname{depth}}_{t}\left( \bar{u}\right)  = \left| \bar{u}\right|$决定。若存在$n \in  \mathbb{N}$使得$\bar{v} = \bar{u} \cdot  n$，则称$\bar{u}$是$\bar{v}$的父节点，记作parent${}_{t}\left( \bar{v}\right)  = \bar{u}$。同理，当$\bar{u}$是$\bar{v}$的父节点时，称$\bar{v}$为$\bar{u}$的子节点，并定义children${}_{t}\left( \bar{u}\right)  = \left\{  {\bar{v} \in  t \mid  {\operatorname{parent}}_{t}\left( \bar{v}\right)  = \bar{u}}\right.$。类似地，$\bar{u}$的后代节点集定义为${\operatorname{desc}}_{t}\left( \bar{u}\right)  = \left\{  {\bar{v} \in  t \mid  \bar{u}{ \leq  }_{p}\bar{v}}\right\}$，祖先节点集为${\operatorname{ancst}}_{t}\left( \bar{u}\right)  = \left\{  {\bar{v} \in  t \mid  \bar{v}{ \leq  }_{p}\bar{u}}\right\}$；注意$\bar{u} \in  {\operatorname{desc}}_{t}\left( \bar{u}\right)$且$\bar{u} \in  {\operatorname{ancst}}_{t}\left( \bar{u}\right)$。若${\operatorname{desc}}_{t}\left( \bar{u}\right)  = \{ \bar{u}\}$，则称节点$\bar{u}$为$t$的叶节点，否则为内部节点。我们将$\bar{u}$的叶节点集合定义为${\operatorname{leaves}}_{t}\left( \bar{u}\right)  = \left\{  {\bar{v} \in  {\operatorname{desc}}_{t}\left( \bar{u}\right)  \mid  \bar{v}}\right.$是叶节点$\}$。

A labeled tree $\tau$ is a function $\tau  : t \rightarrow  L$ where $t$ is a tree and $L$ is any finite set of labels. We use $\operatorname{dom}\left( \tau \right)$ to denote the underlying tree structure $t$ of $\tau$ . Given that $\tau$ is a function,we can write $\tau \left( \bar{u}\right)$ to denote the label of node $\bar{u} \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( \tau \right)$ . To simplify the notation,we extend all the definitions above for a tree $t$ to labeled tree $\tau$ ,changing $t$ by $\operatorname{dom}\left( \tau \right)$ . For example,we write $\bar{u} \in  \tau$ to refer to $\bar{u} \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( \tau \right)$ ,or ${\text{parent}}_{\tau }\left( \bar{u}\right)$ to refer to ${\operatorname{parent}}_{\operatorname{dom}\left( \tau \right) }\left( \bar{u}\right)$ . Finally,we say that two labeled trees $\tau$ and ${\tau }^{\prime }$ are isomorphic if there exists a bijection $f : \operatorname{dom}\left( \tau \right)  \rightarrow  \operatorname{dom}\left( {\tau }^{\prime }\right)$ such that $\bar{u}{ \preccurlyeq  }_{p}\bar{v}$ iff $f\left( \bar{u}\right) { \preccurlyeq  }_{p}f\left( \bar{v}\right)$ and $\tau \left( \bar{u}\right)  = {\tau }^{\prime }\left( {f\left( \bar{u}\right) }\right)$ for every $\bar{u},\bar{v} \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( \tau \right)$ . We will usually say that $\tau$ and ${\tau }^{\prime }$ are equal,meaning they are isomorphic.

带标签树$\tau$是一个函数$\tau  : t \rightarrow  L$，其中$t$是树结构，$L$是任意有限的标签集合。我们用$\operatorname{dom}\left( \tau \right)$表示$\tau$的基础树结构$t$。由于$\tau$是函数，可用$\tau \left( \bar{u}\right)$表示节点$\bar{u} \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( \tau \right)$的标签。为简化符号，我们将上述针对树$t$的所有定义扩展至带标签树$\tau$，将$t$替换为$\operatorname{dom}\left( \tau \right)$。例如，用$\bar{u} \in  \tau$指代$\bar{u} \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( \tau \right)$，或用${\text{parent}}_{\tau }\left( \bar{u}\right)$指代${\operatorname{parent}}_{\operatorname{dom}\left( \tau \right) }\left( \bar{u}\right)$。最后，若存在双射$f : \operatorname{dom}\left( \tau \right)  \rightarrow  \operatorname{dom}\left( {\tau }^{\prime }\right)$使得当且仅当$\bar{u}{ \preccurlyeq  }_{p}\bar{v}$时$f\left( \bar{u}\right) { \preccurlyeq  }_{p}f\left( \bar{v}\right)$成立，且对每个$\bar{u},\bar{v} \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( \tau \right)$都有$\tau \left( \bar{u}\right)  = {\tau }^{\prime }\left( {f\left( \bar{u}\right) }\right)$，则称两带标签树$\tau$与${\tau }^{\prime }$同构。通常我们称$\tau$与${\tau }^{\prime }$相等即表示二者同构。

Parallelized finite automata. A Parallelized Finite Automaton (PFA) is a tuple $\mathcal{P} = \left( {Q,\sum ,\Delta ,I,F}\right)$ where $Q$ is a finite set of states, $\sum$ is a finite alphabet, $I,F \subseteq  Q$ are the sets of initial and accepting states,respectively,and $\Delta  \subseteq  {2}^{Q} \times  \sum  \times  Q$ is the transition relation. We define the size of $\mathcal{P}$ as $\left| \mathcal{P}\right|  =$ $\left| Q\right|  + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{\left( P,a,q\right) }\left( {\left| P\right|  + 1}\right)$ ,namely,the number of states plus the size of encoding the transitions.

并行化有限自动机。并行化有限自动机(PFA)是一个元组$\mathcal{P} = \left( {Q,\sum ,\Delta ,I,F}\right)$，其中$Q$是有限状态集，$\sum$是有限字母表，$I,F \subseteq  Q$分别为初始状态集和接受状态集，$\Delta  \subseteq  {2}^{Q} \times  \sum  \times  Q$是转移关系。我们将$\mathcal{P}$的大小定义为$\left| \mathcal{P}\right|  =$$\left| Q\right|  + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{\left( P,a,q\right) }\left( {\left| P\right|  + 1}\right)$，即状态数加上转移关系的编码规模。

A run tree of a PFA $\mathcal{P}$ over a string $\bar{s} = {a}_{1}\ldots {a}_{n} \in  {\sum }^{ * }$ is a labeled tree $\tau  : t \rightarrow  \operatorname{dom}\left( \tau \right)$ such tha ${\operatorname{depth}}_{\tau }\left( \bar{u}\right)  = n$ for every leaf $\bar{u} \in  \tau$ ; in other words,every node of $\tau$ is labeled by a state of $\mathcal{P}$ and all branches have the same length $n$ . In addition, $\tau$ must satisfy the following two conditions: (1) every leaf node $\bar{u}$ of $t$ is labeled by an initial state (i.e., $\tau \left( \bar{u}\right)  \in  I$ ) and (2) for every inner node $\bar{v}$ at depth $i$ (i.e., ${\operatorname{depth}}_{\tau }\left( \bar{v}\right)  = i$ ) there must be a transition $\left( {P,{a}_{n - i},q}\right)  \in  \Delta$ such that $\tau \left( \bar{v}\right)  = q$ ,|children ${\tau }_{\tau }\left( \bar{v}\right) \left|  = \right| P$ and $P = \left\{  {\tau \left( \bar{u}\right)  \mid  \bar{u} \in  {\operatorname{children}}_{\tau }\left( \bar{v}\right) }\right\}$ ,that is,children have different labels and $P$ is the set of labels in the children of $\bar{v}$ . We say that $\tau$ is an accepting run of $\mathcal{P}$ over $\bar{s}$ iff $\tau$ is a run of $\mathcal{P}$ over $\bar{s}$ and $\tau \left( \varepsilon \right)  \in  F$ (recall that $\varepsilon  = \operatorname{root}\left( \tau \right)$ ). We say that $\mathcal{P}$ accepts a string $\bar{s} \in  {\sum }^{ * }$ if there is an accepting run of $\mathcal{P}$ over $\bar{s}$ and we define the language recognized by $\mathcal{P},\mathcal{L}\left( \mathcal{P}\right)$ ,as the set of strings that $\mathcal{P}$ accepts.

概率有限自动机(PFA) $\mathcal{P}$在字符串$\bar{s} = {a}_{1}\ldots {a}_{n} \in  {\sum }^{ * }$上的运行树是一个带标签的树结构$\tau  : t \rightarrow  \operatorname{dom}\left( \tau \right)$，其满足：每个叶节点$\bar{u} \in  \tau$；换言之，$\tau$的每个节点都由$\mathcal{P}$的状态标记且所有分支具有相同长度$n$。此外，$\tau$必须满足以下两个条件：(1) 每个叶节点$\bar{u}$必须被初始状态标记（即$\tau \left( \bar{u}\right)  \in  I$）；(2) 对于深度为$i$的每个内部节点$\bar{v}$（即${\operatorname{depth}}_{\tau }\left( \bar{v}\right)  = i$），必须存在转移$\left( {P,{a}_{n - i},q}\right)  \in  \Delta$使得$\tau \left( \bar{v}\right)  = q$，|子节点${\tau }_{\tau }\left( \bar{v}\right) \left|  = \right| P$与$P = \left\{  {\tau \left( \bar{u}\right)  \mid  \bar{u} \in  {\operatorname{children}}_{\tau }\left( \bar{v}\right) }\right\}$，这意味着子节点具有不同标签且$P$是$\bar{v}$子节点标签的集合。当且仅当$\tau$是$\mathcal{P}$在$\bar{s}$上的运行且满足$\tau \left( \varepsilon \right)  \in  F$（注意$\varepsilon  = \operatorname{root}\left( \tau \right)$），我们称$\tau$是$\mathcal{P}$在$\bar{s}$上的接受运行。若存在$\mathcal{P}$在$\bar{s}$上的接受运行，则称$\mathcal{P}$接受字符串$\bar{s} \in  {\sum }^{ * }$，并将$\mathcal{P},\mathcal{L}\left( \mathcal{P}\right)$识别的语言定义为$\mathcal{P}$接受的所有字符串集合。

Figure 1: On the left, an example of a PFA and, on the right, an example of a PCEA.

图1：左侧展示了一个PFA示例，右侧展示了一个PCEA示例。

<!-- Media -->

Example 3.1. In Figure 1 (left),we show the example of a PFA ${\mathcal{P}}_{0}$ over the alphabet $\sum  = \{ T,S,R\}$ . Intuitively,the upper part (i.e., ${p}_{0},{p}_{1}$ ) looks for a symbol $T$ ,the lower part (i.e., ${p}_{2},{p}_{3}$ ) for a symbol $S$ , and both runs join together in ${p}_{4}$ when they see a symbol $R$ . Then, ${\mathcal{P}}_{0}$ defines all strings that contain symbols $T$ and $S$ (in any order) before a symbol $R$ .

示例3.1 图1（左）展示了字母表$\sum  = \{ T,S,R\}$上PFA ${\mathcal{P}}_{0}$的实例。直观来看，上部（即${p}_{0},{p}_{1}$）寻找符号$T$，下部（即${p}_{2},{p}_{3}$）寻找符号$S$，当两者遇到符号$R$时在${p}_{4}$处汇合。因此，${\mathcal{P}}_{0}$定义了所有在符号$R$之前包含$T$和$S$（顺序不限）的字符串。

One can see that PFA is a generalization of an NFA. Indeed, NFA is a special case of an PFA where each run tree $\tau$ is a line. Nevertheless,PFA do not add expressive power to NFA,given that PFA is another model for recognizing regular languages, as the next result shows.

可以看出PFA是非确定性有限自动机(NFA)的泛化形式。实际上，NFA是PFA的特例——当每个运行树$\tau$为线性结构时。尽管如此，如下文结果所示，由于PFA是识别正则语言的另一种模型，其并未增强NFA的表达能力。

Proposition 3.2. For every ${PFA}\mathcal{P}$ with $n$ states there exists a ${DFA}\mathcal{A}$ with at most ${2}^{n}$ states such that $\mathcal{L}\left( \mathcal{P}\right)  = \mathcal{L}\left( \mathcal{A}\right)$ . In particular,all languages defined by PFA are regular.

命题3.2：对于每个具有${PFA}\mathcal{P}$状态且满足$n$条件的${DFA}\mathcal{A}$，都存在一个最多具有${2}^{n}$状态的等效${DFA}\mathcal{A}$使得$\mathcal{L}\left( \mathcal{P}\right)  = \mathcal{L}\left( \mathcal{A}\right)$成立。特别地，由PFA定义的所有语言都是正则语言。

Intuitively, one could interpret a PFA as an Alternating Finite Automaton (AFA) [7] that runs backwards over the string (however, they still process the string in a forward direction). It was shown in [7] Theorem 5.2 and 5.3] that for every AFA that defines a language $L$ with $n$ states,there exists an equivalent DFA with ${2}^{{2}^{n}}$ states in the worst case that recognizes $L$ . Nevertheless,they argued that the reverse language ${L}^{R} = \left\{  {{a}_{1}{a}_{2}\ldots {a}_{n} \in  {\sum }^{ * } \mid  {a}_{n}\ldots {a}_{2}{a}_{1} \in  L}\right\}$ can always be accepted by a DFA with at most ${2}^{n}$ states. Then,one can see Proposition 3.2 as a consequence of reversing an alternating automaton. Despite this connection, we use here PFA as a proper automata model, which was not studied or used in [7]. Another related proposal is the parallel finite automata model presented in [25]. Indeed, one can consider PFA as a restricted case of this model, although it was not studied in [25]. For this reason, we decided to name the PFA model with the same acronym but a slightly different name as in [25] .

直观而言，可将PFA视为在字符串上反向运行的交替有限自动机(AFA)[7]（但其实际处理字符串方向仍为正向）。文献[7]定理5.2和5.3表明：对于每个能定义语言$L$且具有$n$状态的AFA，在最坏情况下存在一个具有${2}^{{2}^{n}}$状态的等效DFA可识别$L$。然而他们论证指出，逆向语言${L}^{R} = \left\{  {{a}_{1}{a}_{2}\ldots {a}_{n} \in  {\sum }^{ * } \mid  {a}_{n}\ldots {a}_{2}{a}_{1} \in  L}\right\}$总能被最多具有${2}^{n}$状态的DFA接受。因此，命题3.2可视为交替自动机逆向操作的推论。尽管存在这种关联，本文仍将PFA作为独立自动机模型进行研究，这与文献[7]的视角不同。另一相关提案是文献[25]提出的并行有限自动机模型——实际上PFA可视为该模型的受限特例（虽未在[25]中探讨）。为此，我们决定采用与[25]相同缩写但稍异名称来命名PFA模型。

Parallelized complex event automata. A Parallelized Complex Event Automaton (PCEA) is the extension of CCEA with the idea of parallelization as in PFA. Specifically,a PCEA is a tuple $\mathcal{P} =$ $\left( {Q,\mathbf{U},\mathbf{B},\Omega ,\Delta ,F}\right)$ ,where $Q,\mathbf{U},\mathbf{B},\Omega$ ,and $F$ are the same as for CCEA,and $\Delta$ is a finite transition

并行化复杂事件自动机。并行化复杂事件自动机(PCEA)是在CCEA基础上引入PFA并行化理念的扩展。具体而言，PCEA是一个元组$\mathcal{P} =$$\left( {Q,\mathbf{U},\mathbf{B},\Omega ,\Delta ,F}\right)$，其中$Q,\mathbf{U},\mathbf{B},\Omega$和$F$与CCEA相同，而$\Delta$是有限转移

relation of the form:

关系，其形式为：

$$
\Delta  \subseteq  {2}^{Q} \times  \mathbf{U} \times  {\mathbf{B}}^{Q} \times  \left( {{2}^{\Omega }\smallsetminus \{ \varnothing \} }\right)  \times  Q.
$$

where ${\mathbf{B}}^{Q}$ are all partial functions $\mathcal{B} : Q \rightarrow  \mathbf{B}$ ,that associate a state $q$ to a binary predicate $\mathcal{B}\left( q\right)$ . We define the size of $\mathcal{P}$ as $\left| \mathcal{P}\right|  = \left| Q\right|  + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\left( {P,U,\mathcal{B},L,q}\right)  \in  \Delta }}\left( {\left| P\right|  + \left| L\right| }\right)$ . Note that $\mathcal{P}$ does not define the initial function explicitly. As we will see,transitions of the form $\left( {\varnothing ,U,\mathcal{B},L,q}\right)$ will play the role of the initial function on a run of $\mathcal{P}$ .

其中${\mathbf{B}}^{Q}$均为将状态$q$关联到二元谓词$\mathcal{B}\left( q\right)$的部分函数$\mathcal{B} : Q \rightarrow  \mathbf{B}$。我们定义$\mathcal{P}$的大小为$\left| \mathcal{P}\right|  = \left| Q\right|  + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{\left( {P,U,\mathcal{B},L,q}\right)  \in  \Delta }}\left( {\left| P\right|  + \left| L\right| }\right)$。需注意$\mathcal{P}$并未明确定义初始函数。后续将看到，形式为$\left( {\varnothing ,U,\mathcal{B},L,q}\right)$的转移将在$\mathcal{P}$运行中承担初始函数的角色。

Next,we extend the notion of a run from CCEA to its parallelized version. Let $\mathcal{S} = {t}_{0}{t}_{1}\ldots$ be a stream. A run tree of $\mathcal{P}$ over $\mathcal{S}$ is now a labeled tree $\tau  : t \rightarrow  \left( {Q \times  \mathbb{N} \times  \left( {{2}^{\Omega }\smallsetminus \{ \varnothing \} }\right) }\right)$ where each node $\bar{u} \in  \tau$ is labeled with a configuration $\tau \left( \bar{u}\right)  = \left( {q,i,L}\right)$ such that,for every child $\bar{v} \in  {\operatorname{children}}_{\tau }\left( \bar{u}\right)$ with $\tau \left( \bar{v}\right)  = \left( {p,j,M}\right)$ ,it holds that $j < i$ . In other words,the positions of $\tau$ -configurations increase towards the root of $\tau$ ,similar to the runs of a CCEA. In addition, $\bar{u}$ must satisfy the transition relation $\Delta$ ,that is,there must exist a transition $\left( {P,U,\mathcal{B},L,q}\right)  \in  \Delta$ such that (1) ${t}_{i} \in  U,\left( 2\right) \left| {{\operatorname{children}}_{\tau }\left( \bar{u}\right) }\right|  = \left| P\right|$ and $P = \left\{  {p\mid \exists \bar{v} \in  {\operatorname{children}}_{\tau }\left( \bar{u}\right) .\tau \left( \bar{v}\right)  = \left( {p,j,M}\right) }\right\}$ ,and (3) for every $\bar{v} \in  {\operatorname{children}}_{\tau }\left( \bar{u}\right)$ with $\tau \left( \bar{v}\right)  = \left( {p,j,M}\right)$ , $\left( {{t}_{j},{t}_{i}}\right)  \in  \mathcal{B}\left( p\right)$ . Similar to PFA,condition (2) forces that there exists a bijection between $P$ and the states at the children of $\bar{u}$ . Instead,condition (3) forces that two consecutive configurations(p,j,M) and(q,i,L)must satisfy the binary predicate in $\mathcal{B}\left( p\right)$ associated with $p$ . Notice that,if $\bar{u}$ is a leaf node in $\tau$ ,then it must hold that $P = \varnothing$ and condition (3) is trivially satisfied. Also,note that we do not assume that all leaves are at the same depth.

接下来，我们将运行的概念从CCEA扩展到其并行化版本。设$\mathcal{S} = {t}_{0}{t}_{1}\ldots$为流。$\mathcal{P}$在$\mathcal{S}$上的运行树现在是一个带标签的树$\tau  : t \rightarrow  \left( {Q \times  \mathbb{N} \times  \left( {{2}^{\Omega }\smallsetminus \{ \varnothing \} }\right) }\right)$，其中每个节点$\bar{u} \in  \tau$被标记为配置$\tau \left( \bar{u}\right)  = \left( {q,i,L}\right)$，使得对于每个带有$\tau \left( \bar{v}\right)  = \left( {p,j,M}\right)$的子节点$\bar{v} \in  {\operatorname{children}}_{\tau }\left( \bar{u}\right)$，都满足$j < i$。换句话说，$\tau$配置的位置向$\tau$的根部递增，类似于CCEA的运行。此外，$\bar{u}$必须满足转移关系$\Delta$，即必须存在一个转移$\left( {P,U,\mathcal{B},L,q}\right)  \in  \Delta$，使得(1)${t}_{i} \in  U,\left( 2\right) \left| {{\operatorname{children}}_{\tau }\left( \bar{u}\right) }\right|  = \left| P\right|$和$P = \left\{  {p\mid \exists \bar{v} \in  {\operatorname{children}}_{\tau }\left( \bar{u}\right) .\tau \left( \bar{v}\right)  = \left( {p,j,M}\right) }\right\}$，(3)对于每个带有$\tau \left( \bar{v}\right)  = \left( {p,j,M}\right)$的$\bar{v} \in  {\operatorname{children}}_{\tau }\left( \bar{u}\right)$，$\left( {{t}_{j},{t}_{i}}\right)  \in  \mathcal{B}\left( p\right)$。与PFA类似，条件(2)强制要求$P$与$\bar{u}$子节点的状态之间存在双射。而条件(3)则强制要求两个连续配置(p,j,M)和(q,i,L)必须满足与$p$关联的$\mathcal{B}\left( p\right)$中的二元谓词。注意，如果$\bar{u}$是$\tau$中的叶节点，则必须满足$P = \varnothing$，且条件(3)自然满足。另外，请注意我们并不假设所有叶子节点都处于同一深度。

Given a position $n \in  \mathbb{N}$ ,we say that $\tau$ is an accepting run at position $n$ iff the root configuration $\tau \left( \varepsilon \right)$ is accepting at position $n$ . Further,we define the output of a run $\tau$ as the valuation ${\nu }_{\tau } : \Omega  \rightarrow  {2}^{\mathbb{N}}$ such that ${\nu }_{\tau }\left( \ell \right)  = \left\{  {i\mid \exists \bar{u} \in  \tau .\tau \left( \bar{u}\right)  = \left( {q,i,L}\right)  \land  \ell  \in  L}\right\}$ for every label $\ell  \in  \Omega$ . Finally,the output of a PCEA $\mathcal{P}$ over $\mathcal{S}$ at the position $n$ is defined as:

给定一个位置$n \in  \mathbb{N}$，我们说$\tau$在位置$n$是一个接受运行，当且仅当根配置$\tau \left( \varepsilon \right)$在位置$n$是接受的。此外，我们将运行$\tau$的输出定义为估值${\nu }_{\tau } : \Omega  \rightarrow  {2}^{\mathbb{N}}$，使得对于每个标签$\ell  \in  \Omega$，${\nu }_{\tau }\left( \ell \right)  = \left\{  {i\mid \exists \bar{u} \in  \tau .\tau \left( \bar{u}\right)  = \left( {q,i,L}\right)  \land  \ell  \in  L}\right\}$。最后，PCEA$\mathcal{P}$在$\mathcal{S}$上位置$n$的输出定义为：

$$
{\left\lbrack  \mathcal{P}\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  = \left\{  {{\nu }_{\tau } \mid  \tau \text{ is an accepting run at position }n\text{ of }\mathcal{P}\text{ over }S}\right\}  .
$$

Example 3.3. In Figure 1 (right),we show an example of a PCEA ${\mathcal{P}}_{0}$ over schema ${\sigma }_{0}$ with $\Omega  = \{  \bullet  \}$ . We use the same notation as in Example 2.1 to represent unary and equality predicates. If we run ${\mathcal{P}}_{0}$ over ${\mathcal{S}}_{0}$ ,we have the following two run trees at position 5 :

示例3.3 在图1（右）中，我们展示了模式${\sigma }_{0}$上带$\Omega  = \{  \bullet  \}$的PCEA${\mathcal{P}}_{0}$实例。沿用示例2.1的符号体系表示一元谓词和等值谓词。若在${\mathcal{S}}_{0}$上运行${\mathcal{P}}_{0}$，将在位置5得到以下两棵运行树：

<!-- Media -->

<img src="https://cdn.noedgeai.com/01962f52-ddb8-725d-9fa0-a2c6fbb368f4_5.jpg?x=448&y=707&w=281&h=130&r=0"/>

<img src="https://cdn.noedgeai.com/01962f52-ddb8-725d-9fa0-a2c6fbb368f4_5.jpg?x=926&y=709&w=273&h=127&r=0"/>

that produces the valuation ${\nu }_{{\tau }_{0}} = \left\{  {\bullet  \mapsto  \{ 1,3,5\} }\right\}$ and ${\nu }_{{\tau }_{1}} = \{  \bullet   \mapsto  \{ 0,1,5\} \}$ representing the subsequences $T\left( 2\right) ,S\left( {2,{11}}\right) ,R\left( {2,{11}}\right)$ and $S\left( {2,{11}}\right) ,T\left( 2\right) ,R\left( {2,{11}}\right)$ of ${\mathcal{S}}_{0}$ ,respectively. Note that the former is an output of ${\mathcal{C}}_{0}$ in Example 2.1,but the latter is not.

该过程生成分别表示${\mathcal{S}}_{0}$子序列$T\left( 2\right) ,S\left( {2,{11}}\right) ,R\left( {2,{11}}\right)$和$S\left( {2,{11}}\right) ,T\left( 2\right) ,R\left( {2,{11}}\right)$的估值${\nu }_{{\tau }_{0}} = \left\{  {\bullet  \mapsto  \{ 1,3,5\} }\right\}$与${\nu }_{{\tau }_{1}} = \{  \bullet   \mapsto  \{ 0,1,5\} \}$。需注意前者是示例2.1中${\mathcal{C}}_{0}$的输出，而后者不是。

It is easy to see that every CCEA is a PCEA where every transition $\left( {P,U,\mathcal{B},L,q}\right)  \in  \Delta$ satisfies that $\left| P\right|  \leq  1$ . Additionally,the previous example gives evidence that PCEA is a strict generalization of CCEA,namely,there exists no CCEA that can define ${\mathcal{P}}_{0}$ . Intuitively,since a CCEA can only compare the current tuple to the last tuple,for a stream like $\mathcal{S} = R\left( {a,b}\right) ,T\left( a\right) ,S\left( {a,b}\right)$ it would be impossible to check conditions over the second attribute of tuples $R\left( {a,b}\right)$ and $S\left( {a,b}\right)$ .

显然每个CCEA都是满足$\left| P\right|  \leq  1$条件的PCEA，其中每个转移$\left( {P,U,\mathcal{B},L,q}\right)  \in  \Delta$都需符合该条件。此外，前例证明PCEA是CCEA的严格泛化——即不存在能定义${\mathcal{P}}_{0}$的CCEA。直观而言，由于CCEA只能比较当前元组与末位元组，对于$\mathcal{S} = R\left( {a,b}\right) ,T\left( a\right) ,S\left( {a,b}\right)$这类流，要检查元组$R\left( {a,b}\right)$和$S\left( {a,b}\right)$第二属性的条件是不可能的。

Proposition 3.4. PCEA is strictly more expressive than CCEA.

命题3.4 PCEA的表达能力严格强于CCEA。

Unambiguous PCEA. We end this section by introducing a subclass of PCEA relevant to our algorithmic results. Let $\mathcal{P}$ be a PCEA and $\tau$ a run of $\mathcal{P}$ over some stream. We say that $\tau$ is simple iff for every two different nodes $\bar{u},{\bar{u}}^{\prime } \in  \tau$ with $\tau \left( \bar{u}\right)  = \left( {q,i,L}\right)$ and $\tau \left( {\bar{u}}^{\prime }\right)  = \left( {{q}^{\prime },{i}^{\prime },{L}^{\prime }}\right)$ ,if $i = {i}^{\prime }$ ,then $L \cap  {L}^{\prime } = \varnothing$ . In other words, $\tau$ is simple if all positions of the valuation ${\nu }_{\tau }$ are uniquely represented in $\tau$ . We say that $\mathcal{P}$ is unambiguous if (1) every accepting run of $\mathcal{P}$ is simple and (2) for every stream $\mathcal{S}$ and accepting run ${\tau }^{\prime }$ of $\mathcal{P}$ over $\mathcal{S}$ with valuation ${\nu }_{\tau }$ ,there is no other run $\tau$ of $\mathcal{P}$ with valuation ${\nu }_{{\tau }^{\prime }}$ such that ${\nu }_{\tau } = {\nu }_{{\tau }^{\prime }}$ . For example,the reader can check that ${\mathcal{P}}_{0}$ is unambiguous.

无歧义PCEA。本节最后介绍与算法结果相关的PCEA子类。设$\mathcal{P}$为PCEA，$\tau$是其在某流上的运行。当且仅当对于任意两个不同节点$\bar{u},{\bar{u}}^{\prime } \in  \tau$（满足$\tau \left( \bar{u}\right)  = \left( {q,i,L}\right)$和$\tau \left( {\bar{u}}^{\prime }\right)  = \left( {{q}^{\prime },{i}^{\prime },{L}^{\prime }}\right)$），若$i = {i}^{\prime }$则$L \cap  {L}^{\prime } = \varnothing$时，称$\tau$为简单运行。换言之，若估值${\nu }_{\tau }$的所有位置在$\tau$中均有唯一对应，则$\tau$是简单的。当满足：(1)$\mathcal{P}$的每个接受运行都是简单的；(2)对于任意流$\mathcal{S}$及其上带估值${\nu }_{\tau }$的接受运行${\tau }^{\prime }$，不存在其他带估值${\nu }_{{\tau }^{\prime }}$的运行$\tau$使得${\nu }_{\tau } = {\nu }_{{\tau }^{\prime }}$时，称$\mathcal{P}$为无歧义的。例如，读者可验证${\mathcal{P}}_{0}$是无歧义的。

Condition (2) of unambiguous PCEA ensures that each output is witnessed by exactly one run. This condition is common in MSO enumeration [2, 22] for a one-to-one correspondence between outputs and runs. Condition (1) forces a correspondence between the size of the run and the size of the output it represents. As we will see, both conditions will be helpful for our evaluation algorithm, and satisfied by our translation of hierarchical conjunctive queries into PCEA in the next section.

明确性PCEA的条件(2)确保每个输出恰好由一条运行路径见证。该条件在MSO枚举[2,22]中很常见，用于建立输出与运行路径间的一一对应关系。条件(1)强制要求运行路径的规模与其所代表输出的规模相对应。如后文所示，这两个条件对我们的评估算法都很有帮助，且将在下一节中将层次化连接查询转换为PCEA时得到满足。

## 4 Representing hierarchical conjunctive queries

## 4 层次化连接查询的表示

This section studies the connection between PCEA and hierarchical conjunctive queries (HCQ) over streams. For this purpose, we must first define the semantics of HCQ over streams and how to relate their expressiveness with PCEA. We connect them by using a bag semantics of CQ. We start by introducing bags that will be useful throughout this section.

本节研究PCEA与流上层次化连接查询(HCQ)的关联。为此，我们首先需要定义流上HCQ的语义，以及如何将其表达能力与PCEA联系起来。我们通过使用CQ的包语义来建立这种联系。首先介绍本节将用到的包概念。

Bags. A bag (also called a multiset) is usually defined in the literature as a function that maps each element to its multiplicity (i.e., the number of times it appears). In this work, we use a different but equivalent representation of a bag where each element has its own identity. This representation will be helpful in our context to deal with duplicates in the stream and define the semantics of hierarchical CQ in the case of self joins.

包。文献中通常将包(又称多重集)定义为映射元素到其重数的函数(即元素出现次数)。本工作采用不同但等效的包表示法：每个元素具有独立标识。这种表示法有助于处理流中的重复数据，并为自连接情况下的层次化CQ定义语义。

We define a bag (with own identity) $B$ as a surjective function $B : I \rightarrow  U$ where $I$ is a finite set of identifiers (i.e.,the identity of each element) and $U$ is the underlying set of the bag. Given any bag $B$ ,we refer to these components as $I\left( B\right)$ and $U\left( B\right)$ ,respectively. For example,a bag $B = \{ \{ a,a,b\} \}$ (where $a$ is repeated twice) can be represented with a surjective function ${B}_{0} = \{ 0 \mapsto  a,1 \mapsto  a,2 \mapsto  b\}$ where $I\left( {B}_{0}\right)  = \{ 0,1,2\}$ and $U\left( {B}_{0}\right)  = \{ a,b\}$ . In general,we will use the standard notation for bags $\left\{  \left\{  {{a}_{0},\ldots ,{a}_{n - 1}}\right\}  \right\}$ to denote the bag $B$ whose identifiers are $I\left( B\right)  = \{ 0,\ldots ,n - 1\}$ and $B\left( i\right)  = {a}_{i}$ for each $i \in  I\left( B\right)$ . Note that if $B : I \rightarrow  U$ is injective,then $B$ encodes a set (i.e.,no repetitions). We write $a \in  B$ if $B\left( i\right)  = a$ for some $i \in  I\left( B\right)$ and define the empty bag $\varnothing$ such that $I\left( \varnothing \right)  = \varnothing$ and $U\left( \varnothing \right)  = \varnothing$ .

我们将(带独立标识的)包$B$定义为满射函数$B : I \rightarrow  U$，其中$I$是有限的标识符集合(即各元素的身份标识)，$U$是包的基础集合。给定任意包$B$，我们分别称这些组成部分为$I\left( B\right)$和$U\left( B\right)$。例如，包$B = \{ \{ a,a,b\} \}$(其中$a$重复两次)可表示为满射函数${B}_{0} = \{ 0 \mapsto  a,1 \mapsto  a,2 \mapsto  b\}$，其中$I\left( {B}_{0}\right)  = \{ 0,1,2\}$且$U\left( {B}_{0}\right)  = \{ a,b\}$。通常我们使用标准包记法$\left\{  \left\{  {{a}_{0},\ldots ,{a}_{n - 1}}\right\}  \right\}$表示包$B$，其标识符为$I\left( B\right)  = \{ 0,\ldots ,n - 1\}$且对每个$i \in  I\left( B\right)$有$B\left( i\right)  = {a}_{i}$。注意若$B : I \rightarrow  U$是单射，则$B$编码的是集合(即无重复)。当$B\left( i\right)  = a$对某个$i \in  I\left( B\right)$成立时记作$a \in  B$，并定义空包$\varnothing$满足$I\left( \varnothing \right)  = \varnothing$且$U\left( \varnothing \right)  = \varnothing$。

For a bag $B$ and an element $a$ ,we define the multiplicity of $a$ in $B$ as ${\operatorname{mult}}_{B}\left( a\right)  = \left| {\{ i \mid  B\left( i\right)  = a\} }\right|$ . Then,we say that a bag ${B}^{\prime }$ is contained in $B$ ,denoted as ${B}^{\prime } \subseteq  B$ ,iff ${\operatorname{mult}}_{{B}^{\prime }}\left( a\right)  \leq  {\operatorname{mult}}_{B}\left( a\right)$ for every $a$ . We also say that two bags ${B}^{\prime }$ and $B$ are equal,and write $B = {B}^{\prime }$ ,if ${B}^{\prime } \subseteq  B$ and $B \subseteq  {B}^{\prime }$ . Note that two bags can be equal although the set of identifiers can be different (i.e., they are equal up to a renaming of the identifiers). Given a set $A$ ,we say that $B$ is a bag from elements of $A$ (or just a bag of $A$ ) if $U\left( B\right)  \subseteq  A$ .

对于一个包$B$和一个元素$a$，我们将$a$在$B$中的重数定义为${\operatorname{mult}}_{B}\left( a\right)  = \left| {\{ i \mid  B\left( i\right)  = a\} }\right|$。当且仅当对于每个$a$满足${\operatorname{mult}}_{{B}^{\prime }}\left( a\right)  \leq  {\operatorname{mult}}_{B}\left( a\right)$时，称包${B}^{\prime }$包含于$B$中，记作${B}^{\prime } \subseteq  B$。若同时满足${B}^{\prime } \subseteq  B$和$B \subseteq  {B}^{\prime }$，则称两个包${B}^{\prime }$与$B$相等，记作$B = {B}^{\prime }$。注意：即使标识符集合不同（即通过重命名标识符可实现相等），两个包仍可能相等。给定集合$A$，当$U\left( B\right)  \subseteq  A$时，称$B$是由$A$的元素构成的包（或简称为$A$的包）。

Relational databases. Recall that $\mathbf{D}$ is our set of data values and let $\sigma  = \left( {\mathbf{T}\text{,arity}}\right)$ be a schema. A relational database $D$ (with duplicates) over $\sigma$ is a bag of Tuples $\left\lbrack  \sigma \right\rbrack$ . Given a relation name $R \in  \mathbf{T}$ , we write ${R}^{D}$ as the bag of $D$ containing only the $R$ -tuples of $D$ ,formally, $I\left( {R}^{D}\right)  = \left\{  {i \in  I\left( D\right)  \mid  D\left( i\right)  = }\right.$ $R\left( \bar{a}\right)$ for some $\bar{a}\}$ and ${R}^{D}\left( i\right)  = D\left( i\right)$ for every $i \in  I\left( {R}^{D}\right)$ . For example,consider again the schema ${\sigma }_{0}$ . Then a database ${D}_{0}$ over ${\sigma }_{0}$ is the bag:

关系型数据库。设$\mathbf{D}$为数据值集合，$\sigma  = \left( {\mathbf{T}\text{,arity}}\right)$为模式。基于$\sigma$的关系型数据库$D$（允许重复）是元组包$\left\lbrack  \sigma \right\rbrack$。给定关系名$R \in  \mathbf{T}$，记${R}^{D}$为$D$中仅包含$R$元组的子包，形式化定义为：存在$\bar{a}\}$使得$I\left( {R}^{D}\right)  = \left\{  {i \in  I\left( D\right)  \mid  D\left( i\right)  = }\right.$$R\left( \bar{a}\right)$，且对所有$i \in  I\left( {R}^{D}\right)$满足${R}^{D}\left( i\right)  = D\left( i\right)$。例如，重新考虑模式${\sigma }_{0}$，则基于该模式的数据库${D}_{0}$可表示为如下包：

$$
{D}_{0} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \{ \{ S\left( {2,{11}}\right) ,T\left( 2\right) ,R\left( {1,{10}}\right) ,S\left( {2,{11}}\right) ,T\left( 1\right) ,R\left( {2,{11}}\right) \} \} .
$$

Here,one can check that ${T}^{{D}_{0}} = \{ \{ T\left( 2\right) ,T\left( 1\right) \} \}$ and ${S}^{{D}_{0}} = \{ \{ S\left( {2,{11}}\right) ,S\left( {2,{11}}\right) \}$ .

此处可验证${T}^{{D}_{0}} = \{ \{ T\left( 2\right) ,T\left( 1\right) \} \}$与${S}^{{D}_{0}} = \{ \{ S\left( {2,{11}}\right) ,S\left( {2,{11}}\right) \}$。

Conjunctive queries. Fix a schema $\sigma  = \left( {\mathbf{T}\text{,arity}}\right)$ and a set of variables $\mathbf{X}$ disjoint from $\mathbf{D}$ (i.e. $\mathbf{X} \cap  \mathbf{D} = \varnothing$ ). A Conjunctive Query(CQ)over relational schema $\sigma$ is a syntactic structure of the form:

合取查询。固定模式$\sigma  = \left( {\mathbf{T}\text{,arity}}\right)$和与$\mathbf{D}$不相交的变量集$\mathbf{X}$（即$\mathbf{X} \cap  \mathbf{D} = \varnothing$）。关系模式$\sigma$上的合取查询(CQ)是形如以下语法的结构：

$$
Q\left( \bar{x}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{0}\left( {\bar{x}}_{0}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{m - 1}\left( {\bar{x}}_{m - 1}\right) 
$$

(†)

such that $Q$ is a relational name not in $\mathbf{T},{R}_{i} \in  \mathbf{T},{\bar{x}}_{i}$ is a sequence of variables in $\mathbf{X}$ and data values in $\mathbf{D}$ ,and $\left| {\bar{x}}_{i}\right|  = \operatorname{arity}\left( {R}_{i}\right)$ for every $i < m$ . Further, $\bar{x}$ is a sequence of variables in ${\bar{x}}_{0},\ldots ,{\bar{x}}_{m - 1}$ . We will denote a CQ like [5] by $Q$ ,where $Q\left( \bar{x}\right)$ and ${R}_{0}\left( {\bar{x}}_{0}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{m - 1}\left( {\bar{x}}_{m - 1}\right)$ are called the head and the body of $Q$ ,respectively. Furthermore,we call each ${R}_{i}\left( {\bar{x}}_{i}\right)$ an atom of $Q$ . For example,the following are two conjunctive queries ${Q}_{0}$ and ${Q}_{1}$ over the schema ${\sigma }_{0}$ :

使得$Q$是一个不在$\mathbf{T},{R}_{i} \in  \mathbf{T},{\bar{x}}_{i}$中的关系名称，$\mathbf{X}$中的变量序列与$\mathbf{D}$中的数据值，且对于每个$i < m$都有$\left| {\bar{x}}_{i}\right|  = \operatorname{arity}\left( {R}_{i}\right)$。此外，$\bar{x}$是${\bar{x}}_{0},\ldots ,{\bar{x}}_{m - 1}$中的变量序列。我们将形如[5]的CQ表示为$Q$，其中$Q\left( \bar{x}\right)$和${R}_{0}\left( {\bar{x}}_{0}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{m - 1}\left( {\bar{x}}_{m - 1}\right)$分别称为$Q$的头部和主体。进一步地，我们称每个${R}_{i}\left( {\bar{x}}_{i}\right)$为$Q$的一个原子。例如，以下是模式${\sigma }_{0}$上的两个合取查询${Q}_{0}$和${Q}_{1}$：

$$
{Q}_{0}\left( {x,y}\right)  \leftarrow  T\left( x\right) ,S\left( {x,y}\right) ,R\left( {x,y}\right) \;{Q}_{1}\left( {x,y}\right)  \leftarrow  T\left( x\right) ,R\left( {x,y}\right) ,S\left( {2,y}\right) ,T\left( x\right) 
$$

Note that a query can repeat atoms. For this reason,we will regularly consider $Q$ as a bag of atoms, where $I\left( Q\right)$ are the positions of $Q$ and $U\left( Q\right)$ is the set of distinct atoms. For instance,we can consider ${Q}_{1}$ above as a bag of atoms,where $I\left( {Q}_{1}\right)  = \{ 0,1,2,3\}$ (i.e.,the position of the atoms) and ${Q}_{1}\left( 0\right)  = T\left( x\right)$ , ${Q}_{1}\left( 1\right)  = R\left( {x,y}\right) ,{Q}_{1}\left( 2\right)  = S\left( {2,y}\right) ,{Q}_{1}\left( 3\right)  = T\left( x\right)$ . We say that a CQ $Q$ has self joins if there are two atoms with the same relation name. We can see in the previous example that ${Q}_{1}$ has self joins,while ${Q}_{0}$ does not.

注意查询可以重复原子。因此，我们通常将$Q$视为原子的多重集合，其中$I\left( Q\right)$是$Q$的位置，$U\left( Q\right)$是不同原子的集合。例如，我们可以将上述${Q}_{1}$视为原子的多重集合，其中$I\left( {Q}_{1}\right)  = \{ 0,1,2,3\}$（即原子的位置）以及${Q}_{1}\left( 0\right)  = T\left( x\right)$、${Q}_{1}\left( 1\right)  = R\left( {x,y}\right) ,{Q}_{1}\left( 2\right)  = S\left( {2,y}\right) ,{Q}_{1}\left( 3\right)  = T\left( x\right)$。若存在两个具有相同关系名称的原子，则称CQ$Q$存在自连接。从前面例子可见${Q}_{1}$存在自连接，而${Q}_{0}$则没有。

Homomorphisms and CQ bag semantics. Let $Q$ be a CQ,and $D$ be a database over the same schema $\sigma$ . A homomorphism is any function $h : \mathbf{X} \cup  \mathbf{D} \rightarrow  \mathbf{D}$ such that $h\left( a\right)  = a$ for every $a \in  \mathbf{D}$ . We extend $h$ as a function from atoms to tuples such that $h\left( {R\left( \bar{x}\right) }\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} R\left( {h\left( \bar{x}\right) }\right)$ for every atom $R\left( \bar{x}\right)$ . We say that $h$ is a homomorphism from $Q$ to $D$ if $h$ is a homomorphism and $h\left( {R\left( \bar{x}\right) }\right)  \in  D$ for every atom $R\left( \bar{x}\right)$ in $Q$ . We denote by $\operatorname{Hom}\left( {Q,D}\right)$ the set of all homomorphisms from $Q$ to $D$ .

同态与CQ多重集合语义。设$Q$为CQ，$D$为同一模式$\sigma$上的数据库。同态是任意满足对每个$a \in  \mathbf{D}$都有$h\left( a\right)  = a$的函数$h : \mathbf{X} \cup  \mathbf{D} \rightarrow  \mathbf{D}$。我们将$h$扩展为从原子到元组的函数，使得对每个原子$R\left( \bar{x}\right)$都有$h\left( {R\left( \bar{x}\right) }\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} R\left( {h\left( \bar{x}\right) }\right)$。若$h$是同态且对$Q$中每个原子$R\left( \bar{x}\right)$都有$h\left( {R\left( \bar{x}\right) }\right)  \in  D$，则称$h$是从$Q$到$D$的同态。用$\operatorname{Hom}\left( {Q,D}\right)$表示从$Q$到$D$的所有同态集合。

To define the bag semantics of $\mathrm{{CQ}}$ ,we need a more refined notion of homomorphism that specifies the correspondence between atoms in $Q$ and tuples in $D$ . Formally,a tuple-homomorphism from $Q$ to $D$ (or $t$ -homomorphism for short) is a function $\eta  : I\left( Q\right)  \rightarrow  I\left( D\right)$ such that there exists a homomorphism ${h}_{\eta }$ from $Q$ to $D$ satisfying that ${h}_{\eta }\left( {Q\left( i\right) }\right)  = D\left( {\eta \left( i\right) }\right)$ for every $i \in  I\left( Q\right)$ . For example,consider again ${Q}_{0}$ and ${D}_{0}$ above,then ${\eta }_{0} = \{ 0 \mapsto  1,1 \mapsto  3,2, \mapsto  5\}$ and ${\eta }_{1} = \{ 0 \mapsto  1,1 \mapsto  0,2 \mapsto  5\}$ are two t-homomorphism from ${Q}_{0}$ to ${D}_{0}$ .

为定义$\mathrm{{CQ}}$的包语义，我们需要一种更精细的同态概念，明确指定$Q$中原子与$D$中元组的对应关系。形式上，从$Q$到$D$的元组同态（简称$t$-同态）是一个函数$\eta  : I\left( Q\right)  \rightarrow  I\left( D\right)$，存在满足${h}_{\eta }\left( {Q\left( i\right) }\right)  = D\left( {\eta \left( i\right) }\right)$的同态${h}_{\eta }$作用于每个$i \in  I\left( Q\right)$。例如再次考虑上文的${Q}_{0}$和${D}_{0}$，则${\eta }_{0} = \{ 0 \mapsto  1,1 \mapsto  3,2, \mapsto  5\}$与${\eta }_{1} = \{ 0 \mapsto  1,1 \mapsto  0,2 \mapsto  5\}$是从${Q}_{0}$到${D}_{0}$的两个t-同态。

Intuitively, a t-homomorphism is like a homomorphism, but it additionally specifies the correspondence between atoms (i.e., $I\left( Q\right)$ ) and tuples (i.e., $I\left( D\right)$ ) in the underlying bags. One can easily check that if $\eta$ is a t-homomorphism,then ${h}_{\eta }$ (restricted to the variables of $Q$ ) is unique. For this reason,we usually say that ${h}_{\eta }$ is the homomorphism associated to $\eta$ . Note that the converse does not hold: for $h$ from $Q$ to $D$ ,there can be several t-homomorphisms $\eta$ such that $h = {h}_{\eta }$ .

直观而言，t-同态类似于同态，但额外规定了底层包中原子（即$I\left( Q\right)$）与元组（即$I\left( D\right)$）的对应关系。容易验证：若$\eta$是t-同态，则${h}_{\eta }$（限制在$Q$变量上）具有唯一性。因此我们通常称${h}_{\eta }$为$\eta$的关联同态。需注意反之不成立：对于从$Q$到$D$的$h$，可能存在多个满足$h = {h}_{\eta }$的t-同态$\eta$。

Let $Q\left( \bar{x}\right)$ be the head of $Q$ . We define the output of a $\mathrm{{CQ}}Q$ over a database $D$ as:

设$Q\left( \bar{x}\right)$为$Q$的头部。我们将$\mathrm{{CQ}}Q$在数据库$D$上的输出定义为：

$$
\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  \left( D\right)  = \left\{  {\left\{  {Q\left( {{h}_{\eta }\left( \bar{x}\right) }\right) }\right\}   \mid  \eta \text{ is a t-homomorphism from }Q\text{ to }D}\right\}  .
$$

Note that the result is another relation where each $Q\left( {{h}_{\eta }\left( \bar{x}\right) }\right)$ is witnessed by a t-homomorphism from $Q$ to $D$ . In other words,there is a one-to-one correspondence between tuples in $\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  \left( D\right)$ and t-homomorphisms from $Q$ to $D$ .

注意结果关系中的每个$Q\left( {{h}_{\eta }\left( \bar{x}\right) }\right)$都由从$Q$到$D$的t-同态见证。换言之，$\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  \left( D\right)$中的元组与从$Q$到$D$的t-同态存在一一对应关系。

Discussion. In the literature, homomorphisms are usually used to define the set semantics of a CQ $Q$ over a database $D$ . They are helpful for set semantics but "inconvenient" for bag semantics since it does not specify the correspondence between atoms and tuples; namely, they only witness the existence of such correspondence. In [8], Chaudhuri and Vardi introduced the bag semantics of CQ by using homomorphisms,which we recall next. Let $Q$ be a CQ like (†) and $D$ a database over the same schema $\sigma$ ,and let $h \in  \operatorname{Hom}\left( {Q,D}\right)$ . We define the multiplicity of $h$ with respect to $Q$ and $D$ by:

讨论。文献中通常用同态定义CQ$Q$在数据库$D$上的集合语义，这对集合语义有效但"不便"用于包语义——因其未明确原子与元组的对应关系，仅能证明这种对应存在。Chaudhuri和Vardi在[8]中通过同态引入CQ的包语义，现重述如下：设$Q$为(†)式CQ，$D$为模式$\sigma$上的数据库，且$h \in  \operatorname{Hom}\left( {Q,D}\right)$。我们通过下式定义$h$相对于$Q$和$D$的重数：

$$
{\operatorname{mult}}_{Q,D}\left( h\right)  = \mathop{\prod }\limits_{{i = 0}}^{{m - 1}}{\operatorname{mult}}_{D}\left( {h\left( {{R}_{i}\left( {\bar{x}}_{i}\right) }\right) }\right) 
$$

Chaudhuri and Vardi defined the bag semantics $\parallel Q\parallel$ of $Q$ over $D$ as the bag $\parallel Q\parallel \left( D\right)$ such that each tuple $Q\left( \bar{a}\right)$ has multiplicity equal to:

Chaudhuri和Vardi将$Q$在$D$上的包语义$\parallel Q\parallel$定义为满足以下条件的包$\parallel Q\parallel \left( D\right)$：每个元组$Q\left( \bar{a}\right)$的重数等于：

$$
{\operatorname{mult}}_{\lceil Q\rfloor \left( D\right) }\left( {Q\left( \bar{a}\right) }\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{h \in  \operatorname{Hom}\left( {Q,D}\right)  : h\left( \bar{x}\right)  = \bar{a}}}{\operatorname{mult}}_{Q,D}\left( h\right) 
$$

In Appendix B,we prove that for every $\mathrm{{CQ}}Q$ and database $D$ it holds that $\llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right)  = \llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right)$ , namely, the bag semantics introduced here (i.e., with t-homomorphisms) is equivalent to the standard bag semantics of CQ. The main difference is that the standard bag semantics of CQ are defined in terms of homomorphisms and multiplicities, and there is no direct correspondence between outputs and homomorphisms. For this reason,we redefine the bag semantics of $\mathrm{{CQ}}$ in terms of t-homomorphism that will connect the outputs of CQ with the outputs of PCEA over streams.

在附录B中，我们证明对于任意$\mathrm{{CQ}}Q$和数据库$D$，都满足$\llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right)  = \llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right)$，即此处引入的包语义（即基于t-同态）与标准CQ包语义等价。主要区别在于：标准CQ包语义通过同态和重数定义，而输出与同态之间不存在直接对应关系。因此，我们基于t-同态重新定义$\mathrm{{CQ}}$的包语义，这将连接CQ输出与流上PCEA的输出。

CQ over streams. Now, we define the semantics of CQ over streams, formalizing its comparison with queries in complex event recognition. For this purpose, we must show how to interpret streams as databases and encode CQ’s outputs as valuations. Fix a schema $\sigma$ and a stream $\mathcal{S} = {t}_{0}{t}_{1}\cdots$ over $\sigma$ . Given a position $n \in  \mathbb{N}$ ,we define the database of $\mathcal{S}$ at position $n$ as the $\sigma$ -database ${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack   = \left\{  \left\{  {{t}_{0},{t}_{1},\ldots ,{t}_{n}}\right\}  \right\}$ . For example, ${D}_{5}\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{S}}_{0}\right\rbrack   = {D}_{0}$ . One can interpret here that $\mathcal{S}$ is a sequence of inserts,and then ${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack$ is the database version at position $n$ . Since ${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack$ is a bag,the identifiers $I\left( {{D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  }\right)$ coincide with the positions of the sequence ${t}_{0}\ldots {t}_{n}$ .

流上的CQ。现在我们定义流上CQ的语义，形式化其与复杂事件识别中查询的对比。为此，需说明如何将流解释为数据库，并将CQ输出编码为估值。给定模式$\sigma$及其上的流$\mathcal{S} = {t}_{0}{t}_{1}\cdots$，对于位置$n \in  \mathbb{N}$，我们将$\mathcal{S}$在位置$n$的数据库定义为$\sigma$-数据库${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack   = \left\{  \left\{  {{t}_{0},{t}_{1},\ldots ,{t}_{n}}\right\}  \right\}$。例如${D}_{5}\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{S}}_{0}\right\rbrack   = {D}_{0}$。此处可理解为$\mathcal{S}$是插入序列，而${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack$是位置$n$的数据库版本。由于${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack$是包，标识符$I\left( {{D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  }\right)$与序列${t}_{0}\ldots {t}_{n}$的位置重合。

Let $Q$ be a CQ over $\sigma$ ,and let $\eta  : I\left( Q\right)  \rightarrow  I\left( {{D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  }\right)$ be a t-homomorphism from $Q$ to ${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack$ . If we consider $\Omega  = I\left( Q\right)$ ,we can interpret $\eta$ as a valuation $\widehat{\eta } : \Omega  \rightarrow  {2}^{\mathbb{N}}$ that maps each atom of $Q$ to a set with a single position; formally, $\widehat{\eta }\left( i\right)  = \{ \eta \left( i\right) \}$ for every $i \in  I\left( Q\right)$ . Then,we define the semantics of $Q$ over stream $\mathcal{S}$ at position $n$ as:

设$Q$是$\sigma$上的CQ，$\eta  : I\left( Q\right)  \rightarrow  I\left( {{D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  }\right)$是从$Q$到${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack$的t-同态。若考虑$\Omega  = I\left( Q\right)$，可将$\eta$解释为估值$\widehat{\eta } : \Omega  \rightarrow  {2}^{\mathbb{N}}$——它将$Q$的每个原子映射到含单个位置的集合；形式化表示为：对每个$i \in  I\left( Q\right)$有$\widehat{\eta }\left( i\right)  = \{ \eta \left( i\right) \}$。则流$\mathcal{S}$上位置$n$处$Q$的语义定义为：

$$
{\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  = \left\{  {\widehat{\eta } \mid  \eta \text{ is a t-homomorphism from }Q\text{ to }{D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  }\right\}  
$$

Note that ${\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$ is equivalent to evaluating $Q$ over ${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack$ where instead of outputting a bag of tuples $\llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( {{D}_{n}\lbrack \mathcal{S}\rbrack }\right)$ ,we output the t-homomorphisms (i.e.,as valuations) that are in a one-to-one correspondence with the tuples in $\llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( {{D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  }\right)$ .

注意${\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$等价于在${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack$上评估$Q$，此时我们输出与$\llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( {{D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  }\right)$中元组一一对应的t-同态（即作为估值），而非输出元组包$\llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( {{D}_{n}\lbrack \mathcal{S}\rbrack }\right)$。

Hierarchical conjunctive queries and main results. Let $Q$ be a CQ of the form (7). Given a variable $x \in  \mathbf{X}$ ,define atoms(x)as the bag of all atoms ${R}_{i}\left( {\bar{x}}_{i}\right)$ of $Q$ such that $x$ appears in ${\bar{x}}_{i}$ . We say that $Q$ is full if every variable appearing in ${\bar{x}}_{0},\ldots ,{\bar{x}}_{m - 1}$ also appears in $\bar{x}$ . Then, $Q$ is a Hierarchical Conjunctive Query (HCQ) [12] iff $Q$ is full and for every pair of variables $x,y \in  \mathbf{X}$ it holds that atoms $\left( x\right)  \subseteq  \operatorname{atoms}\left( y\right)$ , atoms $\left( y\right)  \subseteq  \operatorname{atoms}\left( x\right)$ or $\operatorname{atoms}\left( x\right)  \cap  \operatorname{atoms}\left( y\right)  = \varnothing$ . For example,one can check that ${Q}_{0}$ is a HCQ,but ${Q}_{1}$ is not.

层次化合取查询与主要结果。设$Q$为形如(7)的合取查询。给定变量$x \in  \mathbf{X}$，定义atoms(x)为$Q$中所有包含$x$的原子公式${R}_{i}\left( {\bar{x}}_{i}\right)$的集合。若$Q$中每个出现在${\bar{x}}_{0},\ldots ,{\bar{x}}_{m - 1}$的变量也出现在$\bar{x}$中，则称该查询是完整的。当且仅当$Q$是完整查询，且对于任意变量对$x,y \in  \mathbf{X}$都满足atoms$\left( x\right)  \subseteq  \operatorname{atoms}\left( y\right)$、atoms$\left( y\right)  \subseteq  \operatorname{atoms}\left( x\right)$或$\operatorname{atoms}\left( x\right)  \cap  \operatorname{atoms}\left( y\right)  = \varnothing$时，$Q$称为层次化合取查询(HCQ)[12]。例如，可验证${Q}_{0}$是HCQ，而${Q}_{1}$不是。

HCQ is a subset of CQ that can be evaluated with constant-delay enumeration under updates [5, 18]. Moreover, it is the greatest class of full conjunctive queries that can be evaluated with such guarantees under fine-grained complexity assumptions. Therefore, HCQ is the right yardstick to measure the expressive power of PCEA for defining queries with strong efficiency guarantees. Given a PCEA $\mathcal{P}$ and a CQ $Q$ over the same schema $\sigma$ ,we say that $\mathcal{P}$ is equivalent to $Q$ (denoted as $\mathcal{P} \equiv  Q$ ) iff for every stream $\mathcal{S}$ over $\sigma$ and every position $n$ it holds that $\llbracket \mathcal{P}{\rrbracket }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  = \llbracket Q{\rrbracket }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$ .

HCQ是合取查询(CQ)的子集，可在更新时实现恒定延迟枚举[5,18]。在细粒度复杂度假设下，它还是能保持这种性能保证的最大完整合取查询类。因此，HCQ是衡量PCEA表达能力的理想标尺，用于定义具有强效率保证的查询。给定同模式$\sigma$上的PCEA$\mathcal{P}$和CQ$Q$，当且仅当对于$\sigma$上的所有数据流$\mathcal{S}$和所有位置$n$都满足$\llbracket \mathcal{P}{\rrbracket }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  = \llbracket Q{\rrbracket }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$时，称$\mathcal{P}$等价于$Q$(记作$\mathcal{P} \equiv  Q$)。
