Figure 2: An illustration of constructing an PCEA from a HCQ. On the left,the HCQ ${Q}_{0}$ and its $q$ -tree. On the right,a PCEA ${\mathcal{P}}_{{Q}_{0}}$ equivalent to ${Q}_{0}$ . For presentation purposes,states are repeated several times and ? ${xy}$ means a binary relation with any relation name (i.e., $R$ or $S$ ).

图2：从HCQ构建PCEA的示意图。左侧为HCQ${Q}_{0}$及其$q$树，右侧是与${Q}_{0}$等价的PCEA${\mathcal{P}}_{{Q}_{0}}$。为展示需要，状态被多次重复，?${xy}$表示具有任意关系名的二元关系(即$R$或$S$)。

<!-- Media -->

Theorem 4.1. Let $\sigma$ be a schema. For every ${HCQQ}$ over $\sigma$ ,there exists a ${PCEA}{\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$ over $\sigma$ with unary predicates in ${\mathbf{U}}_{\text{lin }}$ and binary predicates in ${\mathbf{B}}_{\text{eq }}$ such that ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q} \equiv  Q$ . Furthermore, ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$ is unambiguous and of at most exponential size with respect to $Q$ . If $Q$ does not have self joins,then ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$ is of quadratic size.

定理4.1。设$\sigma$为模式。对于$\sigma$上的每个${HCQQ}$，存在$\sigma$上的PCEA${PCEA}{\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$，其一元谓词在${\mathbf{U}}_{\text{lin }}$中，二元谓词在${\mathbf{B}}_{\text{eq }}$中，且满足${\mathcal{P}}_{Q} \equiv  Q$。此外，${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$是明确的，其规模相对于$Q$至多为指数级。若$Q$无自连接，则${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$规模为二次方。

Proof sketch. We give an example of the construction to provide insights on the expressive power of PCEA for defining HCQ (the full technical proof is in the appendix). For this construction, we rely on a $q$ -tree of a HCQ,a structure introduced in [5]. Formally,let $Q$ be a HCQ and assume,for the sake of simplification,that $Q$ is connected (i.e.,the Gaifman graph associated to $Q$ is connected). A $q$ -tree for $Q$ is a labeled tree, ${\tau }_{Q} : t \rightarrow  I\left( Q\right)  \cup  \{ \bar{x}\}$ ,where for every $x \in  \{ \bar{x}\}$ there is a unique inner node $\bar{u} \in  t$ such that ${\tau }_{Q}\left( \bar{u}\right)  = x$ ,and for every atom $i \in  I\left( Q\right)$ there is a unique leaf node $\bar{v} \in  t$ such that ${\tau }_{Q}\left( \bar{v}\right)  = i$ . Further,if ${\bar{u}}_{1},\ldots ,{\bar{u}}_{k}$ are the inner nodes of the path from the root until $\bar{v}$ ,then $\left\{  {\bar{x}}_{i}\right\}   = \left\{  {{\tau }_{Q}\left( {\bar{u}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{\tau }_{Q}\left( {\bar{u}}_{k}\right) }\right\}$ . In [5], it was shown that a CQ $Q$ is hierarchical and connected iff there exists a $q$ -tree for $Q$ . For instance, in Figure 2 (left) we display again the HCQ ${Q}_{0}$ ,labeled with the identifiers of the atoms,and below a $q$ -tree for ${Q}_{0}$ .

证明概要。我们通过构造示例来阐释PCEA在定义HCQ时的表达能力（完整技术证明见附录）。该构造基于HCQ的$q$树结构，此概念引自文献[5]。形式化地，设$Q$为HCQ，为简化起见假设$Q$是连通的（即其对应的Gaifman图连通）。$Q$的$q$树是带标注的树结构${\tau }_{Q} : t \rightarrow  I\left( Q\right)  \cup  \{ \bar{x}\}$，其中每个$x \in  \{ \bar{x}\}$对应唯一内部节点$\bar{u} \in  t$满足${\tau }_{Q}\left( \bar{u}\right)  = x$，每个原子$i \in  I\left( Q\right)$对应唯一叶节点$\bar{v} \in  t$满足${\tau }_{Q}\left( \bar{v}\right)  = i$。此外，若${\bar{u}}_{1},\ldots ,{\bar{u}}_{k}$是从根节点到$\bar{v}$路径上的内部节点，则$\left\{  {\bar{x}}_{i}\right\}   = \left\{  {{\tau }_{Q}\left( {\bar{u}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{\tau }_{Q}\left( {\bar{u}}_{k}\right) }\right\}$。文献[5]证明：当且仅当存在$q$树时，CQ查询$Q$是分层且连通的。例如图2左部再次展示了带原子标识符的HCQ${Q}_{0}$，其下方是对应的$q$树。

For a connected HCQ without self-joins the idea of the construction is to use the $q$ -tree of $Q$ as the underlying structure of the PCEA ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$ . Indeed,the nodes of the $q$ -tree will be the states of ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$ . For example,in Figure 2 (right) we present a PCEA ${\mathcal{P}}_{{Q}_{0}}$ equivalent to ${Q}_{0}$ ,where we use multiple copies of the states for presentation purposes (i.e., if two states have the same label, they are the same state in the figure). As you can check,the states are $\{ 0,1,2,x,y\}$ ,which are the nodes of the $q$ -tree. Furthermore, the leaves of the $q$ -trees (i.e.,the atoms) are the initial states $\{ 0,1,2\}$ where ${\mathcal{P}}_{{Q}_{0}}$ uses a unary predicate to check that the tuples have arrived and annotates with the corresponding identifier.

对于无自连接的连通HCQ，构造思路是将$Q$的$q$树作为PCEA${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$的基础结构。实际上，$q$树的节点将成为${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$的状态。例如图2右部展示了与${Q}_{0}$等价的PCEA${\mathcal{P}}_{{Q}_{0}}$，其中出于展示目的复制了部分状态（即图中相同标签的状态实为同一状态）。可见这些状态$\{ 0,1,2,x,y\}$正是$q$树的节点。此外，$q$树的叶节点（即原子）作为初始状态$\{ 0,1,2\}$，${\mathcal{P}}_{{Q}_{0}}$在此使用一元谓词检测元组到达并使用对应标识符进行标注。

For every atom ${R}_{i}\left( {\bar{x}}_{i}\right)$ and every variable $x \in  \left\{  {\bar{x}}_{i}\right\}  ,{\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$ jumps with a transition to the state $x$ which is a node in the $q$ -tree and joins with all the atoms and variables "hanging" from the path from $x$ to the leave $i$ in the $q$ -tree. For example,consider the first component (i.e.,top-left) of ${\mathcal{P}}_{{Q}_{0}}$ in Figure 2 When ${\mathcal{P}}_{{Q}_{0}}$ reads a tuple $R\left( {a,b}\right)$ ,it jumps to state $x$ and joins with all the atoms hanging from the path from $x$ to 2,namely,the atoms $T$ and $S$ . Similarly,consider the last component (i.e.,below) of ${\mathcal{P}}_{{Q}_{0}}$ in Figure 2. When ${\mathcal{P}}_{{Q}_{0}}$ reads a tuple $R\left( {a,b}\right)$ ,it also jumps to state $y$ ,but now the only atom hanging from the path from $y$ to 2 in the $q$ -tree is 1,which corresponds to a single transition from 1 to $y$ joining with the atom $S\left( {x,y}\right)$ . Finally,when ${\mathcal{P}}_{{Q}_{0}}$ reads a tuple $T\left( a\right)$ ,the only variable that hangs in the path from the root to 0 is the variable $y$ ,and then there is a single transition from $y$ to $x$ ,joining with an equality predicate $\left( {?{xy},{Tx}}\right)$ where $?{xy}$ means a binary relation with any relational name (i.e., $R$ or $S$ ). Finally, the root of the $q$ -tree serves as the final state of the ${\mathcal{P}}_{{Q}_{0}}$ ,namely,all atoms were found. Note that an accepting run tree of ${\mathcal{P}}_{{Q}_{0}}$ serves as a witness that the $q$ -tree is complete. The construction of HCQ with self-joins is more involved, and we present the details in the appendix.

对于每个原子${R}_{i}\left( {\bar{x}}_{i}\right)$和每个变量$x \in  \left\{  {\bar{x}}_{i}\right\}  ,{\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$，都会通过状态转移跳转到状态$x$，该状态是$q$树中的一个节点，并与从$x$到叶子节点$i$路径上"悬挂"的所有原子和变量进行连接。例如，考虑图2中${\mathcal{P}}_{{Q}_{0}}$的第一个组件（即左上部分），当${\mathcal{P}}_{{Q}_{0}}$读取元组$R\left( {a,b}\right)$时，它会跳转到状态$x$，并与从$x$到2的路径上悬挂的所有原子（即原子$T$和$S$）进行连接。类似地，考虑图2中${\mathcal{P}}_{{Q}_{0}}$的最后一个组件（即下方部分），当读取元组$R\left( {a,b}\right)$时，它也会跳转到状态$y$，但此时$q$树中从$y$到2的路径上唯一悬挂的原子是1，这对应于从1到$y$的单个转移，并与原子$S\left( {x,y}\right)$进行连接。最后，当${\mathcal{P}}_{{Q}_{0}}$读取元组$T\left( a\right)$时，从根节点到0的路径上唯一悬挂的变量是$y$，随后存在从$y$到$x$的单个转移，与等式谓词$\left( {?{xy},{Tx}}\right)$进行连接，其中$?{xy}$表示具有任意关系名称的二元关系（即$R$或$S$）。最终，$q$树的根节点作为${\mathcal{P}}_{{Q}_{0}}$的终止状态，表明所有原子都已找到。需注意，${\mathcal{P}}_{{Q}_{0}}$的接受运行树可作为$q$树完备性的证明。带自连接的HCQ构建过程更为复杂，具体细节见附录。

The previous result shows that PCEA has the expressive power to specify every HCQ. Given that HCQ characterize the full CQ that can be evaluated in a dynamic setting (under complexity assumptions), a natural question is to ask whether PCEA has the right expressive power, in the sense that it cannot define non-hierarchical CQ. We answer this question positively by focusing on acyclic CQ. Let $Q$ be a CQ of the form (†). A join-tree for $Q$ is labeled tree $\tau  : t \rightarrow  U\left( Q\right)$ such that for every variable $x$ the set $\{ \bar{u} \in  t \mid  \tau \left( \bar{u}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( x\right) \}$ form a connected tree in $\tau$ . We say that $Q$ is acyclic if $Q$ has a join tree. One can check that both ${Q}_{0}$ and ${Q}_{1}$ are examples of acyclic CQ.

前述结果表明PCEA具备表达所有HCQ的能力。鉴于HCQ表征了动态环境下可评估的完整CQ（在复杂度假设下），一个自然的问题是：PCEA是否具有精确的表达力，即它无法定义非层次化的CQ？我们通过聚焦于无环CQ对此问题给出肯定回答。设$Q$为形式(†)的CQ，$Q$的连接树是带标签的树$\tau  : t \rightarrow  U\left( Q\right)$，使得对于每个变量$x$，集合$\{ \bar{u} \in  t \mid  \tau \left( \bar{u}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( x\right) \}$在$\tau$中形成连通子树。若$Q$存在连接树，则称其为无环CQ。可以验证${Q}_{0}$和${Q}_{1}$均为无环CQ的实例。

Theorem 4.2. Let $\sigma$ be a schema. For every acyclic ${CQQ}$ over $\sigma$ ,if $Q$ is not hierarchical,then $\mathcal{P} ≢ Q$ for all ${PCEA}\mathcal{P}$ over $\sigma$ .

定理4.2. 设$\sigma$为模式。对于$\sigma$上的任意无环${CQQ}$，若$Q$非层次化，则对于$\sigma$上的所有${PCEA}\mathcal{P}$都有$\mathcal{P} ≢ Q$。

We note that, although PCEA can only define acyclic CQ that are hierarchical, it can define queries that are not CQ. For instance, ${\mathcal{P}}_{0}$ in Example 3.3 cannot be defined by any CQ,since a CQ cannot express that the $R$ -tuple must arrive after $T$ and $S$ . Therefore,the class of queries defined by PCEA is strictly more expressive than HCQ.

需注意的是，虽然PCEA只能定义层次化的无环CQ，但它能定义非CQ类查询。例如例3.3中的${\mathcal{P}}_{0}$无法被任何CQ定义，因为CQ无法表达$R$元组必须在$T$和$S$之后到达的条件。因此，PCEA定义的查询类在表达能力上严格强于HCQ。

By Theorems 4.1 and 4.2 PCEA capture the expressibility of HCQ among acyclic CQ. In the next section, we show that they also share their good algorithmic properties for streaming evaluation.

根据定理4.1和4.2，PCEA（路径约束扩展自动机）在无环CQ（合取查询）中捕获了HCQ（层次化合取查询）的表达能力。下一节我们将证明它们在流式评估中也具有相同的优良算法特性。

## 5 An evaluation algorithm for PCEA

## 5 PCEA评估算法

Below, we present our evaluation algorithm for unambiguous PCEA with equality predicates. We do this in a streaming setting where the algorithm reads a stream sequentially, and at each position, we can enumerate the new outputs fired by the last tuple. Furthermore, our algorithm works under a sliding window scenario,where we only want to enumerate the outputs inside the last $w$ items for some window size $w$ . This scenario is motivated by CER [14,11,6],where the importance of data decreases with time, and then, we want the outputs inside some relevant time window.

下文将提出针对带等式谓词的非歧义PCEA的评估算法。该算法在流式处理场景中顺序读取数据流，并在每个位置枚举最新元组触发的新输出。此外，我们的算法支持滑动窗口场景——仅需枚举最近$w$项窗口大小$w$内的输出。该场景源自CER（复杂事件处理）[14,11,6]的需求，其中数据重要性随时间递减，因此需要获取特定相关时间窗口内的输出。

In the following, we start by defining the evaluation problem and stating the main theorem, followed by describing our data structure for storing valuations. We end this section by explaining the algorithm and stating its correctness.

接下来我们将首先定义评估问题并陈述主要定理，随后描述用于存储赋值的数椐结构。本节最后将解释算法流程并论证其正确性。

The streaming evaluation problem. Let $\sigma$ be a fixed schema. For a valuation $\nu  : \Omega  \rightarrow  {2}^{\mathbb{N}}$ ,we define $\min \left( \nu \right)  = \min \{ i \mid  \exists \ell  \in  \Omega .i \in  \nu \left( \ell \right) \}$ ,namely,the minimum position appearing in $\nu$ . In this section,we study the following evaluation problem of PCEA over streams:

流式评估问题。设$\sigma$为固定模式。对于赋值$\nu  : \Omega  \rightarrow  {2}^{\mathbb{N}}$，我们定义$\min \left( \nu \right)  = \min \{ i \mid  \exists \ell  \in  \Omega .i \in  \nu \left( \ell \right) \}$为$\nu$中出现的最小位置。本节研究PCEA在数据流上的如下评估问题：

Problem: $\;\operatorname{EvalPCEA}\left\lbrack  \sigma \right\rbrack$

问题：$\;\operatorname{EvalPCEA}\left\lbrack  \sigma \right\rbrack$

Input: An unambiguous PCEA $\mathcal{P} = \left( {Q,{\mathbf{U}}_{\text{lin }},{\mathbf{B}}_{\mathrm{{eq}}},\Omega ,\Delta ,F}\right)$ over $\sigma$ ,

输入：定义在$\sigma$上的非歧义PCEA$\mathcal{P} = \left( {Q,{\mathbf{U}}_{\text{lin }},{\mathbf{B}}_{\mathrm{{eq}}},\Omega ,\Delta ,F}\right)$，

a window size $w \in  \mathbb{N}$ ,and a stream $\mathcal{S} = {t}_{0}{t}_{1}\ldots$

窗口大小$w \in  \mathbb{N}$，以及数据流$\mathcal{S} = {t}_{0}{t}_{1}\ldots$

Output: At each position $i$ ,enumerate all valuations $\nu  \in  {\left\lbrack  \mathcal{P}\right\rbrack  }_{i}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$

输出：在每个位置$i$，枚举所有估值$\nu  \in  {\left\lbrack  \mathcal{P}\right\rbrack  }_{i}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$

such that $\left| {i - \min \left( \nu \right) }\right|  \leq  w$ . The goal is to output the set ${\left\lbrack  \mathcal{P}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  = \left\{  {\nu  \in  {\left\lbrack  \mathcal{P}\right\rbrack  }_{i}\left( \mathcal{S}\right) \left| \right| i - \min \left( \nu \right)  \mid   \leq  w}\right\}$ by reading the stream $\mathcal{S}$ tuple-by-tuple sequentially. We assume here a method yield $\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack$ such that each call retrieves the next tuple,that is,the $i$ -th call to yield $\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack$ retrieves ${t}_{i}$ for each $i \geq  0$ .

使得$\left| {i - \min \left( \nu \right) }\right|  \leq  w$。目标是通过逐元组顺序读取流$\mathcal{S}$来输出集合${\left\lbrack  \mathcal{P}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  = \left\{  {\nu  \in  {\left\lbrack  \mathcal{P}\right\rbrack  }_{i}\left( \mathcal{S}\right) \left| \right| i - \min \left( \nu \right)  \mid   \leq  w}\right\}$。此处我们假设存在一个yield方法$\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack$，每次调用可获取下一个元组，即第$i$次调用yield$\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack$将获取${t}_{i}$（对于每个$i \geq  0$）。

For solving $\operatorname{EvalPCEA}\left\lbrack  \sigma \right\rbrack$ ,our desire is to find a streaming evaluation algorithm [16,18] that,for each tuple ${t}_{i}$ ,updates its internal state quickly and enumerates the set ${\left\lbrack  \mathcal{P}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$ with output-linear delay. More precisely,let $f : {\mathbb{N}}^{3} \rightarrow  \mathbb{N}$ . A streaming enumeration algorithm $\mathcal{E}$ with $f$ -update time for EvalPCEA $\left\lbrack  \sigma \right\rbrack$ works as follows. Before reading the stream $\mathcal{S},\mathcal{E}$ receives as input a PCEA $\mathcal{P}$ and $w \in  \mathbb{N}$ , and does some preprocessing. By calling yield $\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  ,\mathcal{E}$ reads $\mathcal{S}$ sequentially and processes the next tuple ${t}_{i}$ in two phases called the update phase and enumeration phase,respectively. In the update phase, $\mathcal{E}$ updates a data structure DS with ${t}_{i}$ taking time $\mathcal{O}\left( {f\left( {\left| \mathcal{P}\right| ,\left| {t}_{i}\right| ,w}\right) }\right)$ . In the enumeration phase, $\mathcal{E}$ uses DS for enumerating ${\left\lbrack  \mathcal{P}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$ with output-linear delay. Formally,if ${\left\lbrack  \mathcal{P}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  = \left\{  {{\nu }_{1},\ldots ,{\nu }_{k}}\right\}$ (i.e.,in arbitrary order),the algorithm prints $\# {\nu }_{1}\# {\nu }_{2}\# \ldots \# {\nu }_{k}\#$ to the output registers,sequentially. Furthermore, $\mathcal{E}$ prints the first and last symbols # when the enumeration phase starts and ends, respectively, and the time difference (i.e.,the delay) between printing the #-symbols surrounding ${\nu }_{i}$ is in $\mathcal{O}\left( \left| {\nu }_{i}\right| \right)$ . Finally, if such an algorithm exists, we say that EvalPCEA[σ] admits a streaming evaluation algorithm with $f$ -update time and output-linear delay.

为解决$\operatorname{EvalPCEA}\left\lbrack  \sigma \right\rbrack$，我们期望找到一种流式求值算法[16,18]，该算法能针对每个元组${t}_{i}$快速更新内部状态，并以输出线性延迟枚举集合${\left\lbrack  \mathcal{P}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$。具体而言，设$f : {\mathbb{N}}^{3} \rightarrow  \mathbb{N}$。具有$f$更新时间的流式枚举算法$\mathcal{E}$实现EvalPCEA$\left\lbrack  \sigma \right\rbrack$的流程如下：在读取流$\mathcal{S},\mathcal{E}$前，算法接收PCEA$\mathcal{P}$和$w \in  \mathbb{N}$作为输入并进行预处理。通过调用yield$\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  ,\mathcal{E}$顺序读取$\mathcal{S}$，并在更新阶段和枚举阶段分别处理下一个元组${t}_{i}$。更新阶段中，$\mathcal{E}$用$\mathcal{O}\left( {f\left( {\left| \mathcal{P}\right| ,\left| {t}_{i}\right| ,w}\right) }\right)$时间更新数据结构DS；枚举阶段则利用DS以输出线性延迟枚举${\left\lbrack  \mathcal{P}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$。若${\left\lbrack  \mathcal{P}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  = \left\{  {{\nu }_{1},\ldots ,{\nu }_{k}}\right\}$（即任意顺序），算法会依次将$\# {\nu }_{1}\# {\nu }_{2}\# \ldots \# {\nu }_{k}\#$输出至寄存器，并在枚举阶段开始和结束时分别输出首尾符号#，且输出${\nu }_{i}$前后#符号的时间差（即延迟）在$\mathcal{O}\left( \left| {\nu }_{i}\right| \right)$范围内。若存在此类算法，则称EvalPCEA[σ]具有$f$更新时间及输出线性延迟的流式求值算法。

In the following, we prove the following algorithmic result for evaluating PCEA.

下文我们将证明关于PCEA求值的算法结果。

Theorem 5.1. EvalPCEA $\left\lbrack  \sigma \right\rbrack$ admits a streaming evaluation algorithm with $\left( {\left| \mathcal{P}\right|  \cdot  \left| t\right|  + \left| \mathcal{P}\right|  \cdot  \log \left( \left| \mathcal{P}\right| \right)  + \left| \mathcal{P}\right|  \cdot  }\right.$ $\log \left( w\right) )$ -update time and output-linear delay.

定理5.1：EvalPCEA$\left\lbrack  \sigma \right\rbrack$允许采用具有$\left( {\left| \mathcal{P}\right|  \cdot  \left| t\right|  + \left| \mathcal{P}\right|  \cdot  \log \left( \left| \mathcal{P}\right| \right)  + \left| \mathcal{P}\right|  \cdot  }\right.$$\log \left( w\right) )$更新时间和输出线性延迟的流式评估算法。

Note that the update time does not depend on the number of outputs seen so far, and regarding data complexity (i.e.,assuming that $\mathcal{P}$ and the size of the tuples, $\left| t\right|$ are fixed),the update time is logarithmic in the size of the sliding window. Theorem 5.1 improves with respect to [16] by considering a more general class of queries and evaluating over a sliding window. In contrast, Theorem 5.1 is incomparable to the algorithms for dynamic query evaluation of HCQ in [5, 18]. On the one hand, [5, 18] show constant update time algorithms for HCQ under insertions and deletions. On the other hand, Theorem 5.1 works for CER queries that can compare the order over tuples. If we restrict to HCQ, the algorithms in [5, 18 have better complexity, given that there is no need to maintain and check the order of how tuples are inserted or deleted.

需注意更新时间不依赖于当前已输出的数量，且就数据复杂度而言（即假设$\mathcal{P}$与元组大小$\left| t\right|$固定时），更新时间随滑动窗口大小呈对数增长。定理5.1相较于文献[16]的改进在于考虑了更广泛的查询类别并在滑动窗口上进行评估。相反，定理5.1与文献[5,18]中HCQ动态查询评估算法不可直接比较。一方面，文献[5,18]展示了HCQ在插入删除操作下的恒定更新时间算法；另一方面，定理5.1适用于能比较元组顺序的CER查询。若限定于HCQ，由于无需维护检查元组插入删除顺序，文献[5,18]算法具有更优复杂度。

It is important to note that we base the algorithm of Theorem 5.1 on the ideas introduced in [16. Nevertheless, it has several new insights that are novel and are not present in [16]. First, our algorithm evaluates PCEA, which is a generalization of CCEA, and then the approach in [16] requires several changes. Second, the data structure for our algorithm must manage the evaluation of a sliding window and simultaneously combine parallel runs into one. This challenge requires a new strategy for enumeration that combines cross-products with checking a time condition. Finally, maintaining the runs that are valid inside the sliding window with logarithmic update time requires the design of a new data structure based on the principles of a heap, which is novel. We believe this data structure is interesting in its own right, which could lead to new advances in streaming evaluation algorithms with enumeration.

需要强调的是，虽然定理5.1算法基于文献[16]的思想，但包含多项创新性见解：首先，我们的算法评估PCEA（CCEA的泛化形式），这需要对文献[16]方法进行多处修改；其次，数据结构需同时管理滑动窗口评估与并行运行的合并，这要求采用结合笛卡尔积与时间条件检查的新型枚举策略；最后，以对数更新时间维护滑动窗口内有效运行需要基于堆原理设计新型数据结构，该结构本身具有研究价值，可能推动带枚举功能的流式评估算法发展。

We dedicate the rest of this section to explaining the streaming evaluation algorithm of Theorem 5.1. starting by describing the data structure DS.

本节剩余部分将重点阐述定理5.1的流式评估算法，首先介绍数据结构DS。

The data structure. Fix a set of labels $\Omega$ . For representing sets of valuations $\nu  : \Omega  \rightarrow  {2}^{\mathbb{N}}$ ,we use a data structure composed of nodes, where each node stores a position, a set of labels, and pointers to other nodes. Formally, the data structure DS is composed by a set of nodes, denoted by Nodes(DS), where each node $\mathrm{n}$ has a set $L\left( \mathrm{n}\right)  \subseteq  \Omega$ ,a position $i\left( \mathrm{n}\right)  \in  \mathbb{N}$ ,a set prod( $\mathrm{n}$ ) $\subseteq  \operatorname{Nodes}\left( \mathrm{{DS}}\right)$ ,and two links to other nodes uleft(n),uright(n) $\in$ Nodes(DS). We assume that the directed graph ${G}_{\mathrm{{DS}}}$ with $V\left( {G}_{\mathrm{{DS}}}\right)  =$ Nodes(DS) and $E\left( {G}_{\mathrm{{DS}}}\right)  = \left\{  {\left( {{n}_{1},{n}_{2}}\right)  \mid  {n}_{2} \in  \operatorname{prod}\left( {n}_{1}\right)  \vee  {n}_{2} = \operatorname{uleft}\left( {n}_{1}\right)  \vee  {n}_{2} = \operatorname{uright}\left( {n}_{1}\right) }\right\}$ is acyclic. In addition,we assume a special node $\downarrow   \in$ Nodes(DS) that serves as a bottom node (i.e.,all components above are undefined for 1) and $\bot   \notin  \operatorname{prod}\left( \mathrm{n}\right)$ for every $\mathrm{n}$ .

数据结构：固定标签集$\Omega$。为表示估值集合$\nu  : \Omega  \rightarrow  {2}^{\mathbb{N}}$，我们采用由节点组成的数据结构，每个节点存储位置信息、标签集及指向其他节点的指针。形式化定义中，数据结构DS由节点集合Nodes(DS)构成，每个节点$\mathrm{n}$包含集合$L\left( \mathrm{n}\right)  \subseteq  \Omega$、位置$i\left( \mathrm{n}\right)  \in  \mathbb{N}$、集合prod($\mathrm{n}$)$\subseteq  \operatorname{Nodes}\left( \mathrm{{DS}}\right)$及左右节点指针uleft(n),uright(n)$\in$Nodes(DS)。假设有向图${G}_{\mathrm{{DS}}}$（顶点集$V\left( {G}_{\mathrm{{DS}}}\right)  =$Nodes(DS)，边集$E\left( {G}_{\mathrm{{DS}}}\right)  = \left\{  {\left( {{n}_{1},{n}_{2}}\right)  \mid  {n}_{2} \in  \operatorname{prod}\left( {n}_{1}\right)  \vee  {n}_{2} = \operatorname{uleft}\left( {n}_{1}\right)  \vee  {n}_{2} = \operatorname{uright}\left( {n}_{1}\right) }\right\}$）无环。此外设存在特殊节点$\downarrow   \in$Nodes(DS)作为底部节点（即所有组件对1未定义），且满足$\bot   \notin  \operatorname{prod}\left( \mathrm{n}\right)$对所有$\mathrm{n}$成立。

Each node in DS represents a bag of valuations. For explaining this representation, we need to first introduce some algebraic operations on valuations. Given two valuations $\nu ,{\nu }^{\prime } : \Omega  \rightarrow  {2}^{\mathbb{N}}$ ,we define the product $\nu  \oplus  {\nu }^{\prime } : \Omega  \rightarrow  {2}^{\mathbb{N}}$ such that $\left\lbrack  {\nu  \oplus  {\nu }^{\prime }}\right\rbrack  \left( \ell \right)  = \nu \left( \ell \right)  \cup  {\nu }^{\prime }\left( \ell \right)$ for every $\ell  \in  \Omega$ . Further,we extend this product to bags of valuations $V$ and ${V}^{\prime }$ such that $V \oplus  {V}^{\prime } = \left\{  \left\{  {\nu  \oplus  {\nu }^{\prime } \mid  \nu  \in  V,{\nu }^{\prime } \in  {V}^{\prime }}\right\}  \right\}$ . Note that $\oplus$ is an associative and commutative operation and,thus,we can write ${ \oplus  }_{i}{V}_{i}$ for referring to a sequence of $\oplus$ -operations. Given a pair $\left( {L,i}\right)  \in  {2}^{\Omega } \times  \mathbb{N}$ ,we define the valuation ${\nu }_{L,i} : \Omega  \rightarrow  {2}^{\mathbb{N}}$ such that ${\nu }_{L,i}\left( \ell \right)  = \{ i\}$ if $\ell  \in  L$ ,and ${\nu }_{L,i}\left( \ell \right)  = \varnothing$ ,otherwise. With this notation,for every $\mathrm{n} \in$ Nodes(DS) we define the bags ${\left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack  }_{\text{prod }}$ and $\left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack$ recursively as follows:

DS中的每个节点代表一个估值集合。为解释此表示方法，我们需先介绍估值的一些代数运算。给定两个估值$\nu ,{\nu }^{\prime } : \Omega  \rightarrow  {2}^{\mathbb{N}}$，我们定义乘积$\nu  \oplus  {\nu }^{\prime } : \Omega  \rightarrow  {2}^{\mathbb{N}}$使得对于每个$\ell  \in  \Omega$都有$\left\lbrack  {\nu  \oplus  {\nu }^{\prime }}\right\rbrack  \left( \ell \right)  = \nu \left( \ell \right)  \cup  {\nu }^{\prime }\left( \ell \right)$。进一步地，我们将此乘积运算扩展到估值集合$V$和${V}^{\prime }$，满足$V \oplus  {V}^{\prime } = \left\{  \left\{  {\nu  \oplus  {\nu }^{\prime } \mid  \nu  \in  V,{\nu }^{\prime } \in  {V}^{\prime }}\right\}  \right\}$。注意$\oplus$是满足结合律与交换律的运算，因此可用${ \oplus  }_{i}{V}_{i}$表示一系列$\oplus$运算的序列。给定序对$\left( {L,i}\right)  \in  {2}^{\Omega } \times  \mathbb{N}$，我们定义估值${\nu }_{L,i} : \Omega  \rightarrow  {2}^{\mathbb{N}}$满足：当$\ell  \in  L$时${\nu }_{L,i}\left( \ell \right)  = \{ i\}$，否则${\nu }_{L,i}\left( \ell \right)  = \varnothing$。基于此符号系统，对于每个$\mathrm{n} \in$节点(DS)，我们递归定义集合${\left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack  }_{\text{prod }}$和$\left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack$如下：

$$
{\left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }_{\text{prod }} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\{  \left\{  {\nu }_{L\left( n\right) ,i\left( n\right) }\right\}  \right\}   \oplus  \mathop{\bigoplus }\limits_{{{n}^{\prime } \in  \operatorname{prod}\left( n\right) }}\left\lbrack  {n}^{\prime }\right\rbrack  \;\left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack   \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }_{\text{prod }} \cup  \left\lbrack  {\operatorname{uleft}\left( n\right) }\right\rbrack   \cup  \left\lbrack  {\operatorname{uright}\left( n\right) }\right\rbrack  .
$$

For $\downarrow$ ,we define $\llbracket  \downarrow  {\rrbracket }_{\text{prod }} = \llbracket  \bot  \rrbracket  = \varnothing$ . Intuitively,the set prod( $\mathrm{n}$ ) represents the product of its nodes with the valuation ${\nu }_{L,i}$ ,and the nodes uleft(n)and uright(n)represent unions (for union-left and union-right, respectively). This interpretation is analog to the product and union nodes used in previous work of MSO enumeration [2, 22, but here we encode products and unions in a single node.

对于$\downarrow$，我们定义$\llbracket  \downarrow  {\rrbracket }_{\text{prod }} = \llbracket  \bot  \rrbracket  = \varnothing$。直观而言，集合prod($\mathrm{n}$)表示其节点与估值${\nu }_{L,i}$的乘积，而节点uleft(n)和uright(n)分别代表左并集与右并集。这种解释类似于MSO枚举研究[2,22]中使用的乘积与并集节点，但此处我们将乘积与并集编码在单一节点中。

For efficiently enumerating $\left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack$ ,we require that valuations in DS are represented without overlapping. To formalize this idea,define that the product $\nu  \oplus  {\nu }^{\prime }$ is simple if for every $\ell  \in  \Omega ,\nu \left( \ell \right)$ and ${\nu }^{\prime }\left( \ell \right)$ are disjoint and $\left\lbrack  {\nu  \oplus  {\nu }^{\prime }}\right\rbrack  \left( \ell \right)  = \nu \left( \ell \right)  \cup  {\nu }^{\prime }\left( \ell \right)$ . Accordingly,we extend this notion to bags of valuations: $V \oplus  {V}^{\prime }$ is simple if $\nu  \oplus  {\nu }^{\prime }$ is simple for every $\nu  \in  V$ and ${\nu }^{\prime } \in  {V}^{\prime }$ . We say that DS is simple if $\left\{  \left\{  {\nu }_{L\left( \mathrm{n}\right) ,i\left( \mathrm{n}\right) }\right\}  \right\}   \oplus  { \oplus  }_{{\mathrm{n}}^{\prime } \in  \operatorname{prod}\left( \mathrm{n}\right) }\left\lbrack  {\mathrm{n}}^{\prime }\right\rbrack$ is simple for every $\mathrm{n} \in  \operatorname{Nodes}\left( \mathrm{{DS}}\right)$ . This notion is directly related to unambiguous PCEA in Section 3. Intuitively, the first condition of unambiguous PCEA will help us to force that DS is always simple.

为高效枚举$\left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack$，我们要求DS（数据结构）中的估值必须采用非重叠方式表示。为形式化这一概念，定义当满足以下条件时，乘积$\nu  \oplus  {\nu }^{\prime }$是简单的：对于任意$\ell  \in  \Omega ,\nu \left( \ell \right)$和${\nu }^{\prime }\left( \ell \right)$均互不相交且$\left\lbrack  {\nu  \oplus  {\nu }^{\prime }}\right\rbrack  \left( \ell \right)  = \nu \left( \ell \right)  \cup  {\nu }^{\prime }\left( \ell \right)$。相应地，我们将此概念扩展至估值集合：若对于每个$\nu  \in  V$和${\nu }^{\prime } \in  {V}^{\prime }$，$\nu  \oplus  {\nu }^{\prime }$都是简单的，则称$V \oplus  {V}^{\prime }$是简单的。当每个$\mathrm{n} \in  \operatorname{Nodes}\left( \mathrm{{DS}}\right)$对应的$\left\{  \left\{  {\nu }_{L\left( \mathrm{n}\right) ,i\left( \mathrm{n}\right) }\right\}  \right\}   \oplus  { \oplus  }_{{\mathrm{n}}^{\prime } \in  \operatorname{prod}\left( \mathrm{n}\right) }\left\lbrack  {\mathrm{n}}^{\prime }\right\rbrack$都简单时，称DS本身是简单的。该概念与第3节中明确PCEA（无歧义部分候选枚举算法）直接相关——直观上，明确PCEA的第一条件将确保DS始终保持简单性。

The next step is to incorporate the window-size restriction to DS. For a node $\mathrm{n} \in  \operatorname{Nodes}\left( \mathrm{{DS}}\right)$ ,let $\max \left( \mathrm{n}\right)  = \max \{ i \in  \nu \left( \ell \right)  \mid  \nu  \in  \llbracket \mathrm{n}\rrbracket  \land  \ell  \in  \Omega \}$ . Then,given a position $i \geq  \max \left( \mathrm{n}\right)$ and a window size $w \in  \mathbb{N}$ , define the bag:

下一步是将窗口大小限制整合到DS中。对于节点$\mathrm{n} \in  \operatorname{Nodes}\left( \mathrm{{DS}}\right)$，令$\max \left( \mathrm{n}\right)  = \max \{ i \in  \nu \left( \ell \right)  \mid  \nu  \in  \llbracket \mathrm{n}\rrbracket  \land  \ell  \in  \Omega \}$。给定位置$i \geq  \max \left( \mathrm{n}\right)$和窗口尺寸$w \in  \mathbb{N}$时，定义该集合：

$$
{\left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \{ \{ \nu  \in  \left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack  \left| \right| i - \min \left( \nu \right)  \mid   \leq  w\} \} .
$$

Our plan is to represent ${\left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}$ and enumerate its valuations with output-linear delay. For this goal, from now on we fix a $w \in  \mathbb{N}$ and write ${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$ to denote the data structure with window size $w$ . For the enumeration of ${\left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}$ ,in each node $\mathrm{n}$ we store the value:

我们的目标是表示${\left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}$并以输出线性延迟枚举其估值。为此，从此刻起固定$w \in  \mathbb{N}$，并用${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$表示窗口大小为$w$的数据结构。在枚举${\left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}$时，每个节点$\mathrm{n}$内存储的值为：

$$
\operatorname{max-start}\left( \mathrm{n}\right)  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \max \left\{  {\min \left( \nu \right)  \mid  \nu  \in  {\left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack  }_{\text{prod }}}\right\}  
$$

This value will be helpful to verify whether ${\left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}$ is non-empty or not; in particular,one can check that ${\left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w} \neq  \varnothing$ iff $\left| {i - \operatorname{max-start}\left( \mathrm{n}\right) }\right|  \leq  w$ . We always assume that $\left| {\max \left( \mathrm{n}\right)  - \operatorname{max-start}\left( \mathrm{n}\right) }\right|  \leq  w$ (otherwise ${\left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w} = \varnothing$ ). In addition,we require an order with uleft(n) and uright(n) to discard empty unions easily. For every node $\mathrm{n} \in  \operatorname{Nodes}\left( {\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}\right)$ ,we require:

该值有助于验证${\left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}$是否非空；特别地，当且仅当$\left| {i - \operatorname{max-start}\left( \mathrm{n}\right) }\right|  \leq  w$时，可判定${\left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w} \neq  \varnothing$。我们始终假设$\left| {\max \left( \mathrm{n}\right)  - \operatorname{max-start}\left( \mathrm{n}\right) }\right|  \leq  w$（否则${\left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w} = \varnothing$）。此外，需要通过uleft(n)和uright(n)的排序来快速剔除空并集。对于每个节点$\mathrm{n} \in  \operatorname{Nodes}\left( {\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}\right)$，需满足：

$$
\text{max-start}\left( n\right)  \geq  \text{max-start}\left( {\operatorname{uleft}\left( n\right) }\right) \text{and}\operatorname{max-start}\left( n\right)  \geq  \text{max-start}\left( {\operatorname{uright}\left( n\right) }\right) 
$$

(‡)

whenever uleft $\left( \mathrm{n}\right)  \neq   \bot   \neq$ uright(n). Intuitively,the binary tree formed by $\mathrm{n}$ and all nodes that can be reached by following uleft(-) and uright(-) is not strictly ordered; however,it follows the same principle [1] as a heap [10]. Note that it is not our goal to use ${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$ as a priority queue (since removing the max element from a heap takes logarithmic time, and we need constant time), but to use condition (I) to quickly check if there are more outputs to enumerate in uleft(n) or uright(n) by comparing the max-start value of a node with the start of the current location of the time window.

每当uleft$\left( \mathrm{n}\right)  \neq   \bot   \neq$uright(n)时。直观上，由$\mathrm{n}$及所有通过uleft(-)和uright(-)可达节点构成的二叉树并非严格有序；但它遵循与堆[10]相同的原理[1]。需注意，我们的目标并非将${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$用作优先级队列（因为从堆中移除最大元素需对数时间，而我们需要常数时间），而是利用条件(I)通过比较节点的max-start值与时间窗口当前位置的起始值，快速检查uleft(n)或uright(n)中是否存在更多待枚举的输出。

Theorem 5.2. Let $w \in  \mathbb{N}$ be a window size and assume that ${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$ is simple. Then,for every $\mathrm{n} \in$ $\operatorname{Nodes}\left( {\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}\right)$ and every position $i \geq  \max \left( \mathrm{n}\right)$ ,the valuations in ${\left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}$ can be enumerated with output-linear delay and without preprocessing (i.e., the enumeration starts immediately).

定理5.2。设$w \in  \mathbb{N}$为窗口大小且假定${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$是简单的。那么对于每个$\mathrm{n} \in$$\operatorname{Nodes}\left( {\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}\right)$和每个位置$i \geq  \max \left( \mathrm{n}\right)$，${\left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}$中的估值可在无预处理情况下（即枚举立即开始）以输出线性延迟进行枚举。

We require two procedures, called extend and union, for operating nodes in our algorithm. The first procedure extend(L,i,N)receives as input a set $L \subseteq  \Omega$ ,a position $i \in  \mathbb{N}$ ,and $\mathrm{N} \subseteq  \operatorname{Nodes}\left( {\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}\right)$ such that $i\left( \mathrm{n}\right)  < i$ for every $\mathrm{n} \in  \mathrm{N}$ . The procedure outputs a fresh node ${\mathrm{n}}_{e}$ such that ${\left\lbrack  {\mathrm{n}}_{e}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\{  \left\lbrack  {\nu }_{L,i}\right\rbrack  \right\}   \oplus  { \oplus  }_{\mathrm{n} \in  \mathrm{N}}{\left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}$ . By the construction of ${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$ ,this operation is straightforward to implement by defining $L\left( {\mathrm{n}}_{e}\right)  = L,i\left( {\mathrm{n}}_{e}\right)  = i$ , $\operatorname{prod}\left( {\mathrm{n}}_{e}\right)  = \mathrm{N}$ ,and uleft $\left( {\mathrm{n}}_{e}\right)  = \operatorname{uright}\left( {\mathrm{n}}_{e}\right)  =  \bot$ . Further,we can compute max-start $\left( {\mathrm{n}}_{e}\right)$ from the set $\mathrm{N}$ as follows: $\operatorname{max-start}\left( {\mathrm{n}}_{e}\right)  = \min \{ i,\min \{ \operatorname{max-start}\left( \mathrm{n}\right)  \mid  \mathrm{n} \in  \mathrm{N}\} \}$ . Overall,we can implement $\operatorname{extend}\left( {L,i,\mathrm{N}}\right)$ with running time $\mathcal{O}\left( \left| \mathrm{N}\right| \right)$ .

我们的算法需要两个操作节点的过程：extend和union。第一个过程extend(L,i,N)接收输入集合$L \subseteq  \Omega$、位置$i \in  \mathbb{N}$及满足对每个$\mathrm{n} \in  \mathrm{N}$都有$i\left( \mathrm{n}\right)  < i$的$\mathrm{N} \subseteq  \operatorname{Nodes}\left( {\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}\right)$。该过程输出一个新节点${\mathrm{n}}_{e}$使得${\left\lbrack  {\mathrm{n}}_{e}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\{  \left\lbrack  {\nu }_{L,i}\right\rbrack  \right\}   \oplus  { \oplus  }_{\mathrm{n} \in  \mathrm{N}}{\left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}$。根据${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$的构造，通过定义$L\left( {\mathrm{n}}_{e}\right)  = L,i\left( {\mathrm{n}}_{e}\right)  = i$、$\operatorname{prod}\left( {\mathrm{n}}_{e}\right)  = \mathrm{N}$及uleft$\left( {\mathrm{n}}_{e}\right)  = \operatorname{uright}\left( {\mathrm{n}}_{e}\right)  =  \bot$可轻松实现此操作。此外，我们可以从集合$\mathrm{N}$计算max-start$\left( {\mathrm{n}}_{e}\right)$如下：$\operatorname{max-start}\left( {\mathrm{n}}_{e}\right)  = \min \{ i,\min \{ \operatorname{max-start}\left( \mathrm{n}\right)  \mid  \mathrm{n} \in  \mathrm{N}\} \}$。总体而言，我们能在$\mathcal{O}\left( \left| \mathrm{N}\right| \right)$运行时间内实现$\operatorname{extend}\left( {L,i,\mathrm{N}}\right)$。

The second procedure union $\left( {{n}_{1},{n}_{2}}\right)$ receives as inputs two nodes ${n}_{1},{n}_{2} \in  \operatorname{Nodes}\left( {\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}\right)$ such that $\max \left( {\mathbf{n}}_{1}\right)  \leq  i\left( {\mathbf{n}}_{2}\right)$ and uleft $\left( {\mathbf{n}}_{2}\right)  = \operatorname{uright}\left( {\mathbf{n}}_{2}\right)  =  \bot$ . It outputs a fresh node ${\mathbf{n}}_{u}$ such that ${\left\lbrack  {\mathbf{n}}_{u}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w} \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\left\lbrack  {\mathbf{n}}_{1}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w} \cup$ ${\left\lbrack  {\mathrm{n}}_{2}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}$ . The implementation of this procedure is more involved since it requires inserting ${\mathrm{n}}_{2}$ into ${\mathrm{n}}_{1}$ by using uleft $\left( {n}_{1}\right)$ and uright $\left( {n}_{1}\right)$ ,and maintaining condition [5]. Furthermore,we require them to be fully persistent [13],namely, ${n}_{1}$ and ${n}_{2}$ are unmodified after each operation.

第二个操作union $\left( {{n}_{1},{n}_{2}}\right)$接收两个节点${n}_{1},{n}_{2} \in  \operatorname{Nodes}\left( {\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}\right)$作为输入，满足条件$\max \left( {\mathbf{n}}_{1}\right)  \leq  i\left( {\mathbf{n}}_{2}\right)$及uleft $\left( {\mathbf{n}}_{2}\right)  = \operatorname{uright}\left( {\mathbf{n}}_{2}\right)  =  \bot$。该操作输出一个新节点${\mathbf{n}}_{u}$，使得${\left\lbrack  {\mathbf{n}}_{u}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w} \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\left\lbrack  {\mathbf{n}}_{1}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w} \cup$${\left\lbrack  {\mathrm{n}}_{2}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}$。此过程的实现较为复杂，因为需要通过uleft $\left( {n}_{1}\right)$和uright $\left( {n}_{1}\right)$将${\mathrm{n}}_{2}$插入${\mathrm{n}}_{1}$，并维持条件[5]。此外，我们要求这些操作具备完全持久性[13]，即每次操作后${n}_{1}$与${n}_{2}$保持不变。

Proposition 5.3. Let $k \in  \mathbb{N}$ and assume that one performs union $\left( {{\mathrm{n}}_{1},{\mathrm{n}}_{2}}\right)$ over ${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$ with the same position $i = i\left( {\mathrm{n}}_{2}\right)$ at most $k$ times. Then one can implement $\operatorname{union}\left( {{\mathrm{n}}_{1},{\mathrm{n}}_{2}}\right)$ with running time $\mathcal{O}\left( {\log \left( {k \cdot  w}\right) }\right)$ per call.

命题5.3。设$k \in  \mathbb{N}$，并假设在${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$上对同一位置$i = i\left( {\mathrm{n}}_{2}\right)$执行union $\left( {{\mathrm{n}}_{1},{\mathrm{n}}_{2}}\right)$操作最多$k$次。则每次调用$\operatorname{union}\left( {{\mathrm{n}}_{1},{\mathrm{n}}_{2}}\right)$的时间复杂度可控制在$\mathcal{O}\left( {\log \left( {k \cdot  w}\right) }\right)$。

The streaming evaluation algorithm. In Algorithm 1, we present the main procedures of the evaluation algorithm given a fixed schema $\sigma$ . The algorithm receives as input a $\operatorname{PCEA}\mathcal{P} = \left( {Q,{\mathbf{U}}_{\text{lin }},{\mathbf{B}}_{\mathrm{{eq}}},\Omega ,\Delta ,F}\right)$ over $\sigma$ ,a window size $w \in  \mathbb{N}$ ,and a reference to a stream $\mathcal{S}$ . We assume that these inputs are globally accessible by all procedures. Recall that we can test if $t \in  U$ in linear time for any $U \in  {\mathbf{U}}_{\text{lin }}$ . Further, recall that ${\mathbf{B}}_{\mathrm{{eq}}}$ are equality predicates and,for every $B \in  {\mathbf{B}}_{\mathrm{{eq}}}$ ,there exists linear time computable partial functions $\bar{B}$ and $\overrightarrow{B}$ such that $\left( {{t}_{1},{t}_{2}}\right)  \in  B$ iff $\bar{B}\left( {t}_{1}\right)$ and $\overrightarrow{B}\left( {t}_{2}\right)$ are defined and $\bar{B}\left( {t}_{1}\right)  = \overrightarrow{B}\left( {t}_{2}\right)$ ,for every ${t}_{1},{t}_{2} \in  \operatorname{Tuples}\left\lbrack  \sigma \right\rbrack$ .

流式评估算法。在算法1中，我们给出了给定固定模式$\sigma$时评估算法的主要流程。该算法接收一个基于$\sigma$的$\operatorname{PCEA}\mathcal{P} = \left( {Q,{\mathbf{U}}_{\text{lin }},{\mathbf{B}}_{\mathrm{{eq}}},\Omega ,\Delta ,F}\right)$、窗口大小$w \in  \mathbb{N}$及数据流引用$\mathcal{S}$作为输入。假定所有过程均可全局访问这些输入。需注意，对于任意$U \in  {\mathbf{U}}_{\text{lin }}$，我们可在线性时间内检测$t \in  U$。此外，${\mathbf{B}}_{\mathrm{{eq}}}$为等值谓词，且对于每个$B \in  {\mathbf{B}}_{\mathrm{{eq}}}$，存在可线性时间计算的偏函数$\bar{B}$与$\overrightarrow{B}$，使得当且仅当$\bar{B}\left( {t}_{1}\right)$和$\overrightarrow{B}\left( {t}_{2}\right)$有定义且$\bar{B}\left( {t}_{1}\right)  = \overrightarrow{B}\left( {t}_{2}\right)$时，$\left( {{t}_{1},{t}_{2}}\right)  \in  B$成立（对每个${t}_{1},{t}_{2} \in  \operatorname{Tuples}\left\lbrack  \sigma \right\rbrack$）。

For the algorithm, we require some data structures. First, we use the previously described data structure ${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$ and its nodes $\operatorname{Nodes}\left( {\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}\right)$ . Second,we consider a look-up table $\mathrm{H}$ that maps triples of the form(e,p,d)to nodes in $\operatorname{Nodes}\left( {\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}\right)$ where $e \in  \Delta ,p \in  Q$ ,and $d$ is the output of any partial function $\bar{B}$ or $\bar{B}$ . We write $\mathrm{H}\left\lbrack  {e,p,d}\right\rbrack$ for accessing its node,and $\mathrm{H}\left\lbrack  {e,p,d}\right\rbrack   \leftarrow  \mathrm{n}$ for updating a node $\mathrm{n}$ at entry (e,p,d). Also,we write $\mathrm{H}\left\lbrack  {e,p,d}\right\rbrack   = \varnothing$ or $\mathrm{H}\left\lbrack  {e,p,d}\right\rbrack   \neq  \varnothing$ for checking whether there is a node or not at entry(e,p,d). We assume all entries are empty at the beginning. Intuitively,for $e = \left( {P,U,\mathcal{B},L,q}\right)  \in  \Delta$ and $p \in  P$ ,we use $\mathsf{H}\left\lbrack  {e,p, \cdot  }\right\rbrack$ to check if the equality predicate ${\mathcal{B}}_{p}$ is satisfied or not (here ${\mathcal{B}}_{p} = \mathcal{B}\left( p\right)$ ). As it is standard in the literature [5, 18 (i.e., by adopting the RAM model), we assume that each operation over look-up tables takes constant time. Finally,we assume a set of nodes ${\mathrm{N}}_{p}$ for each $p \in  Q$ whose use will be clear later.

针对该算法，我们需要若干数据结构。首先采用先前描述的数据结构${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$及其节点$\operatorname{Nodes}\left( {\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}\right)$；其次构建一个查找表$\mathrm{H}$，用于将形式为(e,p,d)的三元组映射到$\operatorname{Nodes}\left( {\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}\right)$中的节点，其中$e \in  \Delta ,p \in  Q$，且$d$是任意偏函数$\bar{B}$或$\bar{B}$的输出。通过$\mathrm{H}\left\lbrack  {e,p,d}\right\rbrack$访问节点，$\mathrm{H}\left\lbrack  {e,p,d}\right\rbrack   \leftarrow  \mathrm{n}$更新条目(e,p,d)处的节点$\mathrm{n}$，$\mathrm{H}\left\lbrack  {e,p,d}\right\rbrack   = \varnothing$或$\mathrm{H}\left\lbrack  {e,p,d}\right\rbrack   \neq  \varnothing$检查该条目是否存在节点。初始时假定所有条目均为空。直观而言，对于$e = \left( {P,U,\mathcal{B},L,q}\right)  \in  \Delta$和$p \in  P$，使用$\mathsf{H}\left\lbrack  {e,p, \cdot  }\right\rbrack$来验证等式谓词${\mathcal{B}}_{p}$是否满足（此处${\mathcal{B}}_{p} = \mathcal{B}\left( p\right)$）。根据文献[5,18]的标准做法（即采用RAM模型），我们假定查找表上的每个操作均可在常数时间内完成。最后，为每个$p \in  Q$预设节点集${\mathrm{N}}_{p}$，其用途将在后文阐明。

Algorithm 1 Evaluation of an unambiguous PCEA $\mathcal{P} = \left( {Q,{\mathbf{U}}_{\text{lin }},{\mathbf{B}}_{\text{eq }},\Omega ,\Delta ,F}\right)$ with equality predicates over a stream $\mathcal{S}$ under a sliding window of size $w$ .

算法1：在大小为$w$的滑动窗口下，对数据流$\mathcal{S}$上带等式谓词的无歧义PCEA$\mathcal{P} = \left( {Q,{\mathbf{U}}_{\text{lin }},{\mathbf{B}}_{\text{eq }},\Omega ,\Delta ,F}\right)$进行评估

---

procedure $\operatorname{EVALUATION}\left( {\mathcal{P},w,\mathcal{S}}\right)$

过程$\operatorname{EVALUATION}\left( {\mathcal{P},w,\mathcal{S}}\right)$

	${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w} \leftarrow  \varnothing$

	$i \leftarrow   - 1$

	while $t \leftarrow$ yield $\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack$ do

	当$t \leftarrow$生成$\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack$时执行

		RESET(   )

		重置()

		FIRETRANSITIONS(t,i)

		触发转移(t,i)

		UPDATEINDICES(t,i)

		更新索引(t,i)

		for each $n \in  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{p \in  F}}{\mathrm{\;N}}_{p}$ do

		遍历每个$n \in  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{p \in  F}}{\mathrm{\;N}}_{p}$执行

			ENUMERATE(n,i,w)

			枚举(n,i,w)

procedure RESET(   )

过程 重置()

	$i \leftarrow  i + 1$

	for each $p \in  Q$ do

	遍历每个$p \in  Q$执行

		${\mathrm{N}}_{p} \leftarrow  \varnothing$

	procedure FIRETRANSITIONS(t,i)

	过程 触发转移(t,i)

		for each $e = \left( {P,U,\mathcal{B},L,q}\right)  \in  \Delta$ do

		遍历每个$e = \left( {P,U,\mathcal{B},L,q}\right)  \in  \Delta$执行

			if $t \in  U \land  \mathop{\bigwedge }\limits_{{p \in  P}}\mathrm{\;H}\left\lbrack  {e,p,{\overrightarrow{\mathcal{B}}}_{p}\left( t\right) }\right\rbrack   \neq  \varnothing$ then

			若$t \in  U \land  \mathop{\bigwedge }\limits_{{p \in  P}}\mathrm{\;H}\left\lbrack  {e,p,{\overrightarrow{\mathcal{B}}}_{p}\left( t\right) }\right\rbrack   \neq  \varnothing$成立则

					$\mathrm{N} \leftarrow  \left\{  {\mathrm{H}\left\lbrack  {e,p,{\overrightarrow{\mathcal{B}}}_{p}\left( t\right) }\right\rbrack   \mid  p \in  P}\right\}$

					${\mathrm{N}}_{q} \leftarrow  {\mathrm{N}}_{q} \cup  \{$ extend $\left( {L,i,\mathrm{\;N}}\right) \}$

					${\mathrm{N}}_{q} \leftarrow  {\mathrm{N}}_{q} \cup  \{$扩展$\left( {L,i,\mathrm{\;N}}\right) \}$

20:

	procedure UPDATEINDICES(t)

	过程 更新索引(t)

		for each $e = \left( {P,U,\mathcal{B},L,q}\right)  \in  \Delta$ do

		对每个$e = \left( {P,U,\mathcal{B},L,q}\right)  \in  \Delta$执行

			for each $p \in  P \land  \mathrm{n} \in  {\mathrm{N}}_{p}$ do

			对每个$p \in  P \land  \mathrm{n} \in  {\mathrm{N}}_{p}$执行

					if $\mathrm{H}\left\lbrack  {e,p,{\overline{\mathcal{B}}}_{p}\left( t\right) }\right\rbrack   = \varnothing$ then

					若$\mathrm{H}\left\lbrack  {e,p,{\overline{\mathcal{B}}}_{p}\left( t\right) }\right\rbrack   = \varnothing$成立

						$\mathrm{H}\left\lbrack  {e,p,{\overline{\mathcal{B}}}_{p}\left( t\right) }\right\rbrack   \leftarrow  \mathrm{n}$

					else

					否则

						${\mathrm{n}}^{\prime } \leftarrow  \mathrm{H}\left\lbrack  {e,p,{\overline{\mathcal{B}}}_{p}\left( t\right) }\right\rbrack$

						$\mathrm{H}\left\lbrack  {e,p,{\overline{\mathcal{B}}}_{p}\left( t\right) }\right\rbrack   \leftarrow  \operatorname{union}\left( {{\mathrm{n}}^{\prime },\mathrm{n}}\right)$

<!-- Media -->

Algorithm 1 starts at the main procedure EVALUATION. It initializes the data structure ${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$ to empty (i.e.,the only node it has is the special node $\bot$ ) and the index $i$ for keeping the current position in the stream (lines 2-3). Then,the algorithm loops by reading the next tuple yield $\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack$ ,performs the update phase (lines 5-7), followed by the enumeration phase (lines 8-9), and repeats the process over again. Next, we explain the update phase and enumeration phase separately.

算法1从主过程EVALUATION开始。它初始化数据结构${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$为空（即仅包含特殊节点$\bot$），并设置用于记录流当前位置的索引$i$（第2-3行）。接着，算法通过循环读取下一个元组产出$\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack$，执行更新阶段（第5-7行），随后进行枚举阶段（第8-9行），并重复此过程。下文将分别阐述更新阶段和枚举阶段。

The update phase is composed of three steps, encoded as procedures. The first one, RESET, is in charge of starting a new iteration by updating $i$ to the next position and emptying the sets ${\mathrm{N}}_{p}$ (lines 12- 14). The second step,FIRETRANSITIONS,uses the new tuple $t$ to fire all transitions $e = \left( {P,U,\mathcal{B},L,q}\right)  \in  \Delta$ of $\mathcal{P}$ (lines 16-19). We do this by checking if $t$ satisfies $U$ and all equality predicates ${\left\{  {\mathcal{B}}_{p}\right\}  }_{p \in  P}$ (line 17). The main intuition is that the algorithm stores partial runs in the look-up table $\mathrm{H}$ ,whose outputs are represented by nodes in ${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$ . Then the call $\mathrm{H}\left\lbrack  {e,p,{\overrightarrow{\mathcal{B}}}_{p}\left( t\right) }\right\rbrack$ is used to verify the equality ${\widetilde{\mathcal{B}}}_{p}\left( {t}^{\prime }\right)  = {\overrightarrow{\mathcal{B}}}_{p}\left( t\right)$ for some previous tuple ${t}^{\prime }$ . Furthermore,if $\mathsf{H}\left\lbrack  {e,p,{\overline{\mathcal{B}}}_{p}\left( t\right) }\right\rbrack$ is non-empty,it contains the node that represents all runs that have reached $p$ . If $U$ and all predicates ${\left\{  {\mathcal{B}}_{p}\right\}  }_{p \in  P}$ are satisfied,we collect all nodes at states $P$ in the set $\mathrm{N}$ (line 18),and symbolically extend these runs by using the method extend(L,i,N)of ${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$ . We collect the output node of extend in the set ${\mathrm{N}}_{q}$ for use in the next procedure UPDATEINDICES.

更新阶段由三个步骤构成，分别编码为子过程。第一步RESET负责通过更新$i$至下一位置并清空集合${\mathrm{N}}_{p}$来启动新迭代（第12-14行）。第二步FIRETRANSITIONS利用新元组$t$触发$\mathcal{P}$的所有转移$e = \left( {P,U,\mathcal{B},L,q}\right)  \in  \Delta$（第16-19行），通过检查$t$是否满足$U$及所有等值谓词${\left\{  {\mathcal{B}}_{p}\right\}  }_{p \in  P}$实现（第17行）。核心思想是算法将部分运行存储在查找表$\mathrm{H}$中，其输出由${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$中的节点表示。随后调用$\mathrm{H}\left\lbrack  {e,p,{\overrightarrow{\mathcal{B}}}_{p}\left( t\right) }\right\rbrack$来验证某些先前元组${t}^{\prime }$的等值条件${\widetilde{\mathcal{B}}}_{p}\left( {t}^{\prime }\right)  = {\overrightarrow{\mathcal{B}}}_{p}\left( t\right)$。此外，若$\mathsf{H}\left\lbrack  {e,p,{\overline{\mathcal{B}}}_{p}\left( t\right) }\right\rbrack$非空，则包含代表所有到达$p$的运行节点。当$U$和所有谓词${\left\{  {\mathcal{B}}_{p}\right\}  }_{p \in  P}$满足时，我们在集合$\mathrm{N}$中收集状态$P$的所有节点（第18行），并通过${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$的extend(L,i,N)方法符号化扩展这些运行。将extend的输出节点收集到集合${\mathrm{N}}_{q}$中，供后续过程UPDATEINDICES使用。

The last step of the update phase,UPDATEINDICES,is to update the look-up table $\mathrm{H}$ by using $t$ and the nodes stored at the sets ${\left\{  {\mathrm{N}}_{p}\right\}  }_{p \in  Q}$ (lines 22-28). Intuitively,the nodes in ${\mathrm{N}}_{p}$ represent new runs (i.e., valuations) that reached state $p$ when reading $t$ . Then,for every transition $e = \left( {P,U,\mathcal{B},L,q}\right)  \in  \Delta$ such that $p \in  P$ ,we want to update the entry $\left( {e,p,{\overline{\mathcal{B}}}_{p}\left( t\right) }\right)$ of $\mathrm{H}$ with the nodes from ${\mathrm{N}}_{p}$ ,to be ready to be fired for future tuples. For this goal,we check each $\mathrm{n} \in  {\mathrm{N}}_{p}$ and,if $\mathrm{H}\left\lbrack  {e,p,{\overline{\mathcal{B}}}_{p}\left( t\right) }\right\rbrack$ is empty,we just place $\mathrm{n}$ at the entry $\left( {e,p,{\overline{\mathcal{B}}}_{p}\left( t\right) }\right)$ (lines 23-25). Otherwise,we use the union operator of ${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$ ,to combine the previous outputs with the new ones of $n$ (lines 26-28). Note that the call to union $\left( {{n}^{\prime },n}\right)$ satisfies the requirements of this operator,given that $\mathrm{n}$ was created recently.

更新阶段的最后一步UPDATEINDICES，是通过利用$t$和存储在集合${\left\{  {\mathrm{N}}_{p}\right\}  }_{p \in  Q}$中的节点（第22-28行）来更新查找表$\mathrm{H}$。直观上，${\mathrm{N}}_{p}$中的节点表示在读取$t$时到达状态$p$的新运行（即估值）。接着，对于每个满足$p \in  P$的转移$e = \left( {P,U,\mathcal{B},L,q}\right)  \in  \Delta$，我们需要用${\mathrm{N}}_{p}$中的节点更新$\mathrm{H}$的条目$\left( {e,p,{\overline{\mathcal{B}}}_{p}\left( t\right) }\right)$，以便为未来的元组触发做好准备。为此，我们检查每个$\mathrm{n} \in  {\mathrm{N}}_{p}$，如果$\mathrm{H}\left\lbrack  {e,p,{\overline{\mathcal{B}}}_{p}\left( t\right) }\right\rbrack$为空，则直接将$\mathrm{n}$放入条目$\left( {e,p,{\overline{\mathcal{B}}}_{p}\left( t\right) }\right)$（第23-25行）。否则，我们使用${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$的联合操作符，将之前的输出与$n$的新输出合并（第26-28行）。注意，调用union$\left( {{n}^{\prime },n}\right)$满足该操作符的要求，因为$\mathrm{n}$是最近创建的。

Based on the previous description, the enumeration phase is straightforward. Given that the nodes in ${\left\{  {\mathrm{N}}_{p}\right\}  }_{p \in  Q}$ represent new runs at the last position, $\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{p \in  F}}{\mathrm{\;N}}_{p}$ are all new runs that reached some final state. Then,for each node $n \in  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{p \in  F}}{\mathrm{\;N}}_{p}$ we call the procedure ENUMERATE(n,i,w)that enumerates all valuations in ${\left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}$ . Theorem 5.2 shows that this method exists with the desired guarantees given that $\mathcal{P}$ is unambiguous which implies that ${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$ is simple. Further,runs correspond with valuations,namely, ${\left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}$ is a set,and,thus,we enumerate the outputs without repetitions.

基于上述描述，枚举阶段是直截了当的。由于${\left\{  {\mathrm{N}}_{p}\right\}  }_{p \in  Q}$中的节点表示最后位置的新运行，$\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{p \in  F}}{\mathrm{\;N}}_{p}$即为到达某些最终状态的所有新运行。接着，对于每个节点$n \in  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{p \in  F}}{\mathrm{\;N}}_{p}$，我们调用过程ENUMERATE(n,i,w)，该过程枚举${\left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}$中的所有估值。定理5.2表明，由于$\mathcal{P}$是明确的（这意味着${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$是简单的），该方法存在且能提供所需的保证。此外，运行与估值一一对应，即${\left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}$是一个集合，因此我们枚举的输出不会重复。

Proposition 5.4. For every unambiguous PCEA $\mathcal{P}$ with equality predicates, $w \in  \mathbb{N}$ ,stream $\mathcal{S}$ ,and position $i \in  \mathbb{N}$ ,Algorithm 1 enumerates all valuations ${\left\lbrack  \mathcal{P}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$ without repetitions.

命题5.4. 对于每个具有相等谓词的明确PCEA$\mathcal{P}$、$w \in  \mathbb{N}$、流$\mathcal{S}$和位置$i \in  \mathbb{N}$，算法1能无重复地枚举所有估值${\left\lbrack  \mathcal{P}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$。

We end by discussing the update time of Algorithm 1 By inspection, one can check that we performed a linear pass over $\Delta$ during the update phase,where each iteration takes linear time over each transition. Overall,we made at most $\mathcal{O}\left( \left| \mathcal{P}\right| \right)$ calls to unary predicates,the look-up table,or the data structure ${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$ . Each call to a unary predicate takes $\mathcal{O}\left( \left| t\right| \right)$ -time and,thus,at most $\mathcal{O}\left( {\left| \mathcal{P}\right|  \cdot  \left| t\right| }\right)$ -time in total. The operations to the look-up table or extend take constant time. Instead,we performed at most $\mathcal{O}\left( \left| \mathcal{P}\right| \right)$ unions over the same position $i$ . By Proposition 5.3,each union takes time $\mathcal{O}\left( {\log \left( {\left| \mathcal{P}\right|  \cdot  w}\right) }\right)$ . Summing up,the updating time is $\mathcal{O}\left( {\left| \mathcal{P}\right|  \cdot  \left| t\right|  + \left| \mathcal{P}\right|  \cdot  \log \left( \left| \mathcal{P}\right| \right)  + \left| \mathcal{P}\right|  \cdot  \log \left( w\right) }\right)$ .

最后我们讨论算法1的更新时间。通过观察可知，在更新阶段我们对$\Delta$进行了线性遍历，其中每次迭代在每个转移上消耗线性时间。总体而言，我们对一元谓词、查找表或数据结构${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$最多进行了$\mathcal{O}\left( \left| \mathcal{P}\right| \right)$次调用。每次一元谓词调用耗时$\mathcal{O}\left( \left| t\right| \right)$，因此总耗时不超过$\mathcal{O}\left( {\left| \mathcal{P}\right|  \cdot  \left| t\right| }\right)$。查找表或扩展操作仅需常数时间。而我们在同一位置$i$上最多执行了$\mathcal{O}\left( \left| \mathcal{P}\right| \right)$次并集运算。根据命题5.3，每次并集运算耗时$\mathcal{O}\left( {\log \left( {\left| \mathcal{P}\right|  \cdot  w}\right) }\right)$。综上所述，更新时间总计为$\mathcal{O}\left( {\left| \mathcal{P}\right|  \cdot  \left| t\right|  + \left| \mathcal{P}\right|  \cdot  \log \left( \left| \mathcal{P}\right| \right)  + \left| \mathcal{P}\right|  \cdot  \log \left( w\right) }\right)$。

## 6 Future work

## 6 未来工作

We present an automata model for CER that expresses $\mathrm{{HCQ}}$ and can be evaluated in a streaming fashion under a sliding window with a logarithmic update and output-linear delay. These results achieve the primary goal of this paper but leave several directions for future work. First, defining a query language that characterizes the expressive power of PCEA will be interesting. Second, one would like to understand a disambiguation procedure to convert any PCEA into an unambiguous PCEA or to decide when this is possible. Last, we study here algorithms for PCEA with equality predicates, but the model works for any binary predicate. Then, it would be interesting to understand for which other predicates (e.g., inequalities) the model still admits efficient streaming evaluation.

我们提出了一种用于复杂事件处理(CER)的自动机模型，该模型能表达$\mathrm{{HCQ}}$，并可在滑动窗口下以对数更新时间和输出线性延迟进行流式评估。这些成果实现了本文的主要目标，同时为未来研究指明了若干方向：首先，定义能表征并行化有限自动机(PCEA)表达能力的查询语言将颇具意义；其次，需要研究消歧流程以将任意PCEA转换为明确PCEA，或判定其可行性；最后，本文研究了带等值谓词的PCEA算法，但该模型适用于任意二元谓词，因此探索其他谓词(如不等式)在模型中仍保持高效流式评估的条件也很有价值。

## A Proofs of Section 3

## 附录A 第3节证明

## Proof of Proposition 3.2

## 命题3.2证明

Proof. To prove this statement, we follow the same principle used in the subset construction. To simulate all possible run trees of a PFA with a DFA, we start at the leaves, with all initial states. Then for each symbol we move up on the tree, firing all transitions that used a subset of the current set of states. At the end of the string, if the last set has a final state, then it means that one can construct a run tree that accepts the input.

证明。为证此命题，我们沿用子集构造法的原理。为用确定性有限自动机(DFA)模拟概率有限自动机(PFA)所有可能的运行树，我们从包含所有初始状态的叶子节点开始，对每个符号沿树向上移动时，触发当前状态集中所有可能的转移。若字符串处理完毕时最终状态集包含接受状态，则说明存在接受该输入的运行树。

Let $\mathcal{P} = \left( {Q,\sum ,\Delta ,I,F}\right)$ be a parallelized finite automata. We build the DFA $\mathcal{A} = \left( {{2}^{Q},\sum ,\delta ,I,{F}^{\prime }}\right)$ such that ${F}^{\prime } = \{ P \mid  P \cap  F \neq  \varnothing \}$ and $\delta \left( {P,a}\right)  = \left\{  {q \mid  \exists {P}^{\prime } \subseteq  P.\left( {{P}^{\prime },a,q}\right)  \in  \Delta }\right\}  \}$ for every $P \subseteq  Q$ and $a \in  \sum$ . We now prove that both automata define the same language.

设$\mathcal{P} = \left( {Q,\sum ,\Delta ,I,F}\right)$为并行化有限自动机。我们构造的DFA$\mathcal{A} = \left( {{2}^{Q},\sum ,\delta ,I,{F}^{\prime }}\right)$满足：对任意$P \subseteq  Q$和$a \in  \sum$，有${F}^{\prime } = \{ P \mid  P \cap  F \neq  \varnothing \}$且$\delta \left( {P,a}\right)  = \left\{  {q \mid  \exists {P}^{\prime } \subseteq  P.\left( {{P}^{\prime },a,q}\right)  \in  \Delta }\right\}  \}$。现证明两者定义的语言等价。

$\mathcal{L}\left( \mathcal{P}\right)  \subseteq  \mathcal{L}\left( \mathcal{A}\right)$ . Let $\bar{s} = {a}_{1}\ldots {a}_{n} \in  {\sum }^{ * }$ be a string such that $\bar{s} \in  \mathcal{L}\left( \mathcal{P}\right)$ and let $\tau  : t \rightarrow  Q$ be an accepting run tree of $\mathcal{P}$ over $\bar{s}$ . We need to prove that the run $\rho  : {S}_{n}\overset{{a}_{1}}{ \rightarrow  }{S}_{n - 1}\overset{{a}_{2}}{ \rightarrow  }\ldots \overset{{a}_{n}}{ \rightarrow  }{S}_{0}$ is an accepting run of $\mathcal{A}$ over $\bar{s}$ ,i.e. ${S}_{n} \in  {F}^{\prime }$ . To this end,we define ${L}_{i} = \left\{  {\tau \left( \bar{u}\right)  \mid  {\operatorname{depth}}_{\tau }\left( \bar{u}\right)  = i}\right\}$ as the set of states labeling $\tau$ at depth $i$ and prove that ${L}_{i} \subseteq  {S}_{i}$ for all $0 \leq  i \leq  n$ . Since ${L}_{0} = \{ \tau \left( \varepsilon \right) \}$ ,this in return means that ${S}_{n} \cap  F \neq  \varnothing$ and ${S}_{n} \in  {F}^{\prime }$ .

$\mathcal{L}\left( \mathcal{P}\right)  \subseteq  \mathcal{L}\left( \mathcal{A}\right)$。设$\bar{s} = {a}_{1}\ldots {a}_{n} \in  {\sum }^{ * }$为满足$\bar{s} \in  \mathcal{L}\left( \mathcal{P}\right)$的字符串，且$\tau  : t \rightarrow  Q$是$\mathcal{P}$在$\bar{s}$上的接受运行树。需证明运行$\rho  : {S}_{n}\overset{{a}_{1}}{ \rightarrow  }{S}_{n - 1}\overset{{a}_{2}}{ \rightarrow  }\ldots \overset{{a}_{n}}{ \rightarrow  }{S}_{0}$是$\mathcal{A}$在$\bar{s}$上的接受运行，即${S}_{n} \in  {F}^{\prime }$。为此，我们定义${L}_{i} = \left\{  {\tau \left( \bar{u}\right)  \mid  {\operatorname{depth}}_{\tau }\left( \bar{u}\right)  = i}\right\}$为深度$i$处标记$\tau$的状态集合，并证明对所有$0 \leq  i \leq  n$都有${L}_{i} \subseteq  {S}_{i}$。由于${L}_{0} = \{ \tau \left( \varepsilon \right) \}$，这意味着${S}_{n} \cap  F \neq  \varnothing$且${S}_{n} \in  {F}^{\prime }$。

For every leaf node $\bar{u}$ it holds that ${\operatorname{depth}}_{\tau }\left( \bar{u}\right)  = n$ and $\tau \left( \bar{u}\right)  \in  I$ ,meaning ${L}_{n} \subseteq  {S}_{n} = I$ . Let us assume that ${L}_{i - 1} \subseteq  {S}_{i - 1}$ ; for every inner node $\bar{v}$ at depth $i$ there must be a transition $\left( {P,{a}_{n - i},q}\right)  \in  \Delta$ such that $\tau \left( \bar{v}\right)  = q$ and $P = \left\{  {\tau \left( \bar{u}\right)  \mid  \bar{u} \in  {\operatorname{children}}_{\tau }\left( \bar{v}\right) }\right\}$ . Following the definition of $\delta$ ,it is clear that $q \in  \delta \left( {P,a}\right)$ ,and since this is true for every node at depth $i$ ,we have that ${L}_{i} \subseteq  {S}_{i}$ .

对每个叶节点$\bar{u}$，都满足${\operatorname{depth}}_{\tau }\left( \bar{u}\right)  = n$和$\tau \left( \bar{u}\right)  \in  I$，即${L}_{n} \subseteq  {S}_{n} = I$。假设${L}_{i - 1} \subseteq  {S}_{i - 1}$；对于深度$i$处的每个内部节点$\bar{v}$，必存在转移$\left( {P,{a}_{n - i},q}\right)  \in  \Delta$使得$\tau \left( \bar{v}\right)  = q$且$P = \left\{  {\tau \left( \bar{u}\right)  \mid  \bar{u} \in  {\operatorname{children}}_{\tau }\left( \bar{v}\right) }\right\}$。根据$\delta$的定义，显然$q \in  \delta \left( {P,a}\right)$，且因该性质对深度$i$处所有节点成立，故${L}_{i} \subseteq  {S}_{i}$。

Given that ${L}_{0} \subseteq  {S}_{0}$ ,we know that ${S}_{n} \in  {F}^{\prime }$ ,which means that $\rho$ is an accepting run of $\mathcal{A}$ over $\bar{s}$ and therefore $\mathcal{L}\left( \mathcal{P}\right)  \subseteq  \mathcal{L}\left( \mathcal{A}\right)$ .

已知${L}_{0} \subseteq  {S}_{0}$，可推知${S}_{n} \in  {F}^{\prime }$，这意味着$\rho$是$\mathcal{A}$在$\bar{s}$上的接受运行，因此$\mathcal{L}\left( \mathcal{P}\right)  \subseteq  \mathcal{L}\left( \mathcal{A}\right)$。

$\mathcal{L}\left( \mathcal{A}\right)  \subseteq  \mathcal{L}\left( \mathcal{P}\right)$ . Let $\bar{s} = {a}_{1}\ldots {a}_{n} \in  {\sum }^{ * }$ be a string such that $\bar{s} \in  \mathcal{L}\left( \mathcal{A}\right)$ and let $\rho  : {S}_{n}\overset{{a}_{1}}{ \rightarrow  }{S}_{n - 1}\overset{{a}_{2}}{ \rightarrow  }\ldots \overset{{a}_{n}}{ \rightarrow  }{S}_{0}$ be the run of $\mathcal{A}$ over $\bar{s}$ . We can now construct a run tree of $\mathcal{P}$ over $\bar{s}$ .

$\mathcal{L}\left( \mathcal{A}\right)  \subseteq  \mathcal{L}\left( \mathcal{P}\right)$。设$\bar{s} = {a}_{1}\ldots {a}_{n} \in  {\sum }^{ * }$为满足$\bar{s} \in  \mathcal{L}\left( \mathcal{A}\right)$的字符串，令$\rho  : {S}_{n}\overset{{a}_{1}}{ \rightarrow  }{S}_{n - 1}\overset{{a}_{2}}{ \rightarrow  }\ldots \overset{{a}_{n}}{ \rightarrow  }{S}_{0}$表示$\mathcal{A}$在$\bar{s}$上的运行轨迹。现在我们可以构建$\mathcal{P}$在$\bar{s}$上的运行树。

Since $\rho$ is an accepting run,we know that ${S}_{0} \cap  F \leq  \varnothing$ . We define $\tau  : t \rightarrow  Q$ such that $\tau \left( \varepsilon \right)  = f$ with $f \in  {S}_{0} \cap  F$ . If we consider a node $\bar{v} \in  t$ at depth $i$ ,such that $\tau \left( \bar{v}\right)  = q$ and $q \in  {S}_{i}$ ,we can follow the definition of $\delta$ ,and inductively add nodes to $\tau$ according to the transition $\left( {P,{a}_{n - i},q}\right)  \in  \Delta$ so that $\left| {{\operatorname{children}}_{\tau }\left( \bar{v}\right) }\right|  = \left| P\right|$ and $P = \left\{  {\tau \left( \bar{u}\right)  \mid  \bar{u} \in  {\operatorname{children}}_{\tau }\left( \bar{v}\right) }\right\}$ . For every leaf node $\bar{v}$ it holds that ${\operatorname{depth}}_{\tau }\left( \bar{v}\right)  = n$ and since ${S}_{n} = I$ all of them will be labeled by initial states.

由于$\rho$是接受性运行轨迹，可知${S}_{0} \cap  F \leq  \varnothing$。我们定义$\tau  : t \rightarrow  Q$使得$\tau \left( \varepsilon \right)  = f$与$f \in  {S}_{0} \cap  F$成立。考虑深度为$i$的节点$\bar{v} \in  t$，当$\tau \left( \bar{v}\right)  = q$且$q \in  {S}_{i}$时，可依据$\delta$的定义，根据转移$\left( {P,{a}_{n - i},q}\right)  \in  \Delta$递归地向$\tau$添加节点，从而满足$\left| {{\operatorname{children}}_{\tau }\left( \bar{v}\right) }\right|  = \left| P\right|$和$P = \left\{  {\tau \left( \bar{u}\right)  \mid  \bar{u} \in  {\operatorname{children}}_{\tau }\left( \bar{v}\right) }\right\}$。对于所有叶节点$\bar{v}$，都有${\operatorname{depth}}_{\tau }\left( \bar{v}\right)  = n$成立，且由于${S}_{n} = I$，它们都将被初始状态标记。

The labeled tree $\tau$ we just constructed is an accepting run of $\mathcal{P}$ over $\bar{s}$ ,meaning $\mathcal{L}\left( \mathcal{A}\right)  \subseteq  \mathcal{L}\left( \mathcal{P}\right)$ and, therefore, $\mathcal{L}\left( \mathcal{P}\right)  = \mathcal{L}\left( \mathcal{A}\right)$ .

我们刚构建的标记树$\tau$是$\mathcal{P}$在$\bar{s}$上的接受性运行轨迹，这意味着$\mathcal{L}\left( \mathcal{A}\right)  \subseteq  \mathcal{L}\left( \mathcal{P}\right)$，因此$\mathcal{L}\left( \mathcal{P}\right)  = \mathcal{L}\left( \mathcal{A}\right)$。

## Proof of Proposition 3.4

## 命题3.4的证明

Proof. To prove this statement we just need to find a Parallelized-CEA $\mathcal{P}$ with no CCEA equivalent,i.e. there is no CCEA $\mathcal{C}$ such that $\left\lbrack  \mathcal{P}\right\rbrack  \left( \mathcal{S}\right)  = \left\lbrack  \mathcal{C}\right\rbrack  \left( \mathcal{S}\right)$ for every stream $\mathcal{S}$ . Let $\mathcal{P}$ be the PCEA represented in Figure 2,then $\mathcal{P} = \left( {Q,\mathbf{U},\mathbf{B},\Omega ,\Delta ,F}\right)$ ,with $Q = \{ R\left( {x,y}\right) ,S\left( {x,y}\right) ,T\left( x\right) ,x,y\} ,\Omega  = \{ R,S,T\} ,F = \{ x\}$ and:

证明。要证此命题，只需构造一个不存在等价CCEA的并行化CEA（Parallelized-CEA）$\mathcal{P}$，即对于任意流$\mathcal{S}$都不存在满足$\left\lbrack  \mathcal{P}\right\rbrack  \left( \mathcal{S}\right)  = \left\lbrack  \mathcal{C}\right\rbrack  \left( \mathcal{S}\right)$的CCEA$\mathcal{C}$。设$\mathcal{P}$为图2所示的PCEA，则$\mathcal{P} = \left( {Q,\mathbf{U},\mathbf{B},\Omega ,\Delta ,F}\right)$，其中$Q = \{ R\left( {x,y}\right) ,S\left( {x,y}\right) ,T\left( x\right) ,x,y\} ,\Omega  = \{ R,S,T\} ,F = \{ x\}$且满足：

$$
\Delta  = \left\{  {\left( {\varnothing ,{U}_{R\left( {x,y}\right) },\varnothing ,\{ R\left( {x,y}\right) \} ,R\left( {x,y}\right) }\right) ,}\right. 
$$

$$
\left( {\varnothing ,{U}_{S\left( {x,y}\right) },\varnothing ,\{ S\left( {x,y}\right) \} ,S\left( {x,y}\right) }\right) \text{,}
$$

$$
\left( {\varnothing ,{U}_{T\left( x\right) },\varnothing ,\{ T\left( x\right) \} ,T\left( x\right) }\right) \text{,}
$$

$$
\left( {\{ R\left( {x,y}\right) ,T\left( x\right) \} ,{U}_{S\left( {x,y}\right) },\left\{  {\left( {R\left( {x,y}\right) ,{B}_{R\left( {x,y}\right) ,S\left( {x,y}\right) }}\right) ,\left( {T\left( x\right) ,{B}_{T\left( x\right) ,S\left( {x,y}\right) }}\right) }\right\}  ,\{ S\left( {x,y}\right) \} ,x),}\right. 
$$

$$
\left( {\{ S\left( {x,y}\right) ,T\left( x\right) \} ,{U}_{R\left( {x,y}\right) },\left\{  {\left( {S\left( {x,y}\right) ,{B}_{S\left( {x,y}\right) ,R\left( {x,y}\right) }}\right) ,\left( {T\left( x\right) ,{B}_{T\left( x\right) ,R\left( {x,y}\right) }}\right) }\right\}  ,\{ R\left( {x,y}\right) \} ,x),}\right. 
$$

$$
\left( {\{ R\left( {x,y}\right) \} ,{U}_{S\left( {x,y}\right) },\left\{  \left( {R\left( {x,y}\right) ,{B}_{R\left( {x,y}\right) ,S\left( {x,y}\right) }}\right) \right\}  ,\{ S\left( {x,y}\right) \} ,y}\right) ,
$$

$$
\left( {\{ S\left( {x,y}\right) \} ,{U}_{R\left( {x,y}\right) },\left\{  \left( {S\left( {x,y}\right) ,{B}_{S\left( {x,y}\right) ,R\left( {x,y}\right) }}\right) \right\}  ,\{ R\left( {x,y}\right) \} ,y}\right) ,
$$

$$
\left( {\{ y\} ,{U}_{T\left( x\right) },\left\{  \left( {y,{B}_{y,T\left( {x,y}\right) }}\right) \right\}  ,\{ T\left( x\right) \} ,x}\right) \} 
$$

with the predicates ${U}_{R\left( \bar{x}\right) }$ and ${B}_{R\left( \bar{x}\right) ,S\left( \bar{y}\right) }$ defined as:

谓词${U}_{R\left( \bar{x}\right) }$和${B}_{R\left( \bar{x}\right) ,S\left( \bar{y}\right) }$定义如下：

$$
{U}_{R\left( \bar{x}\right) } \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\{  {R\left( \bar{a}\right)  \in  \operatorname{Tuples}\left\lbrack  \sigma \right\rbrack  \mid \exists h \in  \operatorname{Hom}.h\left( {R\left( \bar{x}\right) }\right)  = R\left( \bar{a}\right) }\right\}  
$$

and:

且：

$$
{B}_{R\left( \bar{x}\right) ,S\left( \bar{y}\right) } \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\{  {\left( {R\left( \bar{a}\right) ,S\left( \bar{b}\right) }\right) \mid \exists h \in  \operatorname{Hom}.h\left( {R\left( \bar{x}\right) }\right)  = R\left( \bar{a}\right)  \land  h\left( {S\left( \bar{y}\right) }\right)  = S\left( \bar{b}\right) }\right\}  .
$$

Let ${\mathcal{S}}_{i} = \left\{  {\{ R\left( {0,i}\right) ,T\left( 0\right) ,S\left( {0,i}\right) ,\ldots \} }\right\}$ be a family of streams over the set of data values $\mathbf{D} = \mathbb{N}$ with $i \in  \mathbb{N}$ . It is clear that the valuation $\{ \{ 0,1,2\} \}  \in  \llbracket \mathcal{P}\rrbracket \left( {\mathcal{S}}_{i}\right)$ for every $i \in  \mathbb{N}$ . Let $\mathcal{C} = \left( {{Q}^{\prime },{\mathbf{U}}^{\prime },{\mathbf{B}}^{\prime },{\Omega }^{\prime },{\Delta }^{\prime },{I}^{\prime },{F}^{\prime }}\right)$ be a deterministic CCEA such that $\llbracket \mathcal{C}\rrbracket \left( {\mathcal{S}}_{i}\right)  = \llbracket \mathcal{P}\rrbracket \left( {\mathcal{S}}_{i}\right)$ for every $i \in  \mathbb{N}$ . This means that for every stream ${\mathcal{S}}_{i}$ ,there is an accepting run of $\mathcal{C}$ over of the form ${\rho }_{i} : {q}_{i,0}\xrightarrow[]{R\left( {0,i}\right) }{q}_{i,1}\xrightarrow[]{T\left( 0\right) }{q}_{i,2}\xrightarrow[]{S\left( {0,i}\right) }{q}_{i,3}$ .

设${\mathcal{S}}_{i} = \left\{  {\{ R\left( {0,i}\right) ,T\left( 0\right) ,S\left( {0,i}\right) ,\ldots \} }\right\}$为定义在数据集$\mathbf{D} = \mathbb{N}$上的流族，其中满足$i \in  \mathbb{N}$条件。显然，对于每个$i \in  \mathbb{N}$，估值$\{ \{ 0,1,2\} \}  \in  \llbracket \mathcal{P}\rrbracket \left( {\mathcal{S}}_{i}\right)$成立。令$\mathcal{C} = \left( {{Q}^{\prime },{\mathbf{U}}^{\prime },{\mathbf{B}}^{\prime },{\Omega }^{\prime },{\Delta }^{\prime },{I}^{\prime },{F}^{\prime }}\right)$为一个确定性CCEA（可计算成本效益分析器），使得对于每个$i \in  \mathbb{N}$都有$\llbracket \mathcal{C}\rrbracket \left( {\mathcal{S}}_{i}\right)  = \llbracket \mathcal{P}\rrbracket \left( {\mathcal{S}}_{i}\right)$。这意味着对于每个流${\mathcal{S}}_{i}$，存在$\mathcal{C}$的形式为${\rho }_{i} : {q}_{i,0}\xrightarrow[]{R\left( {0,i}\right) }{q}_{i,1}\xrightarrow[]{T\left( 0\right) }{q}_{i,2}\xrightarrow[]{S\left( {0,i}\right) }{q}_{i,3}$的接受运行。

Since $\mathcal{C}$ has a finite number of states,we know that there must be two streams, ${\mathcal{S}}_{j}$ and ${\mathcal{S}}_{k}$ with $j \neq  k$ with accepting runs ${\rho }_{j} : {q}_{j,0}\xrightarrow[]{R\left( {0,j}\right) }{q}_{j,1}\xrightarrow[]{T\left( 0\right) }{q}_{j,2}\xrightarrow[]{S\left( {0,j}\right) }{q}_{j,3}$ and ${\rho }_{k} : {q}_{k,0}\xrightarrow[]{R\left( {0,k}\right) }{q}_{k,1}\xrightarrow[]{T\left( 0\right) }{q}_{k,2}\xrightarrow[]{S\left( {0,k}\right) }{q}_{k,3}$ , respectively,such that ${q}_{j,i} = {q}_{k,i}$ for every $0 \leq  i \leq  3$ .

由于$\mathcal{C}$具有有限状态数，可知必然存在两个流${\mathcal{S}}_{j}$和${\mathcal{S}}_{k}$，它们分别满足$j \neq  k$条件并具有接受运行${\rho }_{j} : {q}_{j,0}\xrightarrow[]{R\left( {0,j}\right) }{q}_{j,1}\xrightarrow[]{T\left( 0\right) }{q}_{j,2}\xrightarrow[]{S\left( {0,j}\right) }{q}_{j,3}$和${\rho }_{k} : {q}_{k,0}\xrightarrow[]{R\left( {0,k}\right) }{q}_{k,1}\xrightarrow[]{T\left( 0\right) }{q}_{k,2}\xrightarrow[]{S\left( {0,k}\right) }{q}_{k,3}$，使得对于每个$0 \leq  i \leq  3$都有${q}_{j,i} = {q}_{k,i}$成立。

Given the run ${\rho }_{k}$ of $\mathcal{C}$ ,we know that there must be a transition $\left( {{q}_{k,2},U,B,\omega ,{q}_{k,3}}\right)  \in  {\Delta }^{\prime }$ such that $S\left( {0,k}\right)  \in  U$ and $\left( {T\left( 0\right) ,S\left( {0,k}\right) }\right)  \in  B$ and since ${q}_{k,2} = {q}_{j,2}$ and ${q}_{k,3} = {q}_{j,3}$ the following will be an accepting run of $\mathcal{C}$ over the stream ${S}_{j,k} = \left\{  {\{ R\left( {0,j}\right) ,T\left( 0\right) ,S\left( {0,k}\right) \} }\right\}   : {\rho }_{j,k} : {q}_{j,0}\xrightarrow[]{R\left( {0,j}\right) }{q}_{j,1}\xrightarrow[]{T\left( 0\right) }{q}_{j,2}\xrightarrow[]{S\left( {0,k}\right) }{q}_{j,3}$ .

给定运行${\rho }_{k}$的$\mathcal{C}$，可知必然存在一个转移$\left( {{q}_{k,2},U,B,\omega ,{q}_{k,3}}\right)  \in  {\Delta }^{\prime }$使得$S\left( {0,k}\right)  \in  U$且$\left( {T\left( 0\right) ,S\left( {0,k}\right) }\right)  \in  B$，由于${q}_{k,2} = {q}_{j,2}$与${q}_{k,3} = {q}_{j,3}$，以下将成为$\mathcal{C}$在流${S}_{j,k} = \left\{  {\{ R\left( {0,j}\right) ,T\left( 0\right) ,S\left( {0,k}\right) \} }\right\}   : {\rho }_{j,k} : {q}_{j,0}\xrightarrow[]{R\left( {0,j}\right) }{q}_{j,1}\xrightarrow[]{T\left( 0\right) }{q}_{j,2}\xrightarrow[]{S\left( {0,k}\right) }{q}_{j,3}$上的接受运行。

We can easily check that there are no accepting runs of $\mathcal{P}$ over ${\mathcal{S}}_{j,k}$ ,meaning $\llbracket \mathcal{P}\rrbracket \left( {\mathcal{S}}_{j,k}\right)  \neq  \llbracket \mathcal{C}\rrbracket \left( {\mathcal{S}}_{j,k}\right)$ and therefore there is no CCEA $\mathcal{C}$ such that $\llbracket \mathcal{P}\rrbracket \left( \mathcal{S}\right)  = \llbracket \mathcal{C}\rrbracket \left( \mathcal{S}\right)$ for every stream $\mathcal{S}$ .

可轻易验证$\mathcal{P}$在${\mathcal{S}}_{j,k}$上不存在接受运行，这意味着$\llbracket \mathcal{P}\rrbracket \left( {\mathcal{S}}_{j,k}\right)  \neq  \llbracket \mathcal{C}\rrbracket \left( {\mathcal{S}}_{j,k}\right)$，因此不存在满足$\llbracket \mathcal{P}\rrbracket \left( \mathcal{S}\right)  = \llbracket \mathcal{C}\rrbracket \left( \mathcal{S}\right)$的CCEA$\mathcal{C}$适用于每个流$\mathcal{S}$。

## B Proofs of Section 4

## B 第4节证明

## Proof of equivalence between CQ bag-semantics

## CQ包语义等价性证明

Fix a schema $\sigma$ ,a set of data values $\mathbf{D}$ and a CQ $Q$ over $\sigma$ of the form:

设定模式$\sigma$、数据值集合$\mathbf{D}$及形式如下的$\sigma$上的CQ$Q$：

$$
Q\left( \bar{x}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{0}\left( {\bar{x}}_{0}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{m - 1}\left( {\bar{x}}_{m - 1}\right) 
$$

To prove that $\llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right)  = \llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right)$ we need to prove that $\llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right)  \subseteq  \llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right)$ and $\llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right)  \subseteq  \llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right)$ , where $\parallel Q\parallel \left( D\right)  \subseteq  \left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  \left( D\right)$ if $U\left( {\parallel Q\parallel \left( D\right) }\right)  \subseteq  U\left( {\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  \left( D\right) }\right)$ and for every $a$ in $I\left( {\parallel Q\parallel \left( D\right) }\right) ,{\operatorname{mult}}_{\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  \left( D\right) }\left( a\right)  \leq$ ${\operatorname{mult}}_{\llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right) }\left( a\right)$ .

欲证$\llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right)  = \llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right)$，需证明$\llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right)  \subseteq  \llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right)$与$\llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right)  \subseteq  \llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right)$，其中当$U\left( {\parallel Q\parallel \left( D\right) }\right)  \subseteq  U\left( {\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  \left( D\right) }\right)$时$\parallel Q\parallel \left( D\right)  \subseteq  \left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  \left( D\right)$，且对于$I\left( {\parallel Q\parallel \left( D\right) }\right) ,{\operatorname{mult}}_{\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  \left( D\right) }\left( a\right)  \leq$中的每个$a$都有${\operatorname{mult}}_{\llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right) }\left( a\right)$。

Both $\llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right)$ and $\llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right)$ map every atom of $Q$ to the database $D$ ,meaning $U\left( {\llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right) }\right)  =$ $U\left( {\llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right) }\right)$ ,so now for every $Q\left( \bar{a}\right)$ in $I\left( {\llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right) }\right)$ we just need to prove that for every $a$ in $I\left( {\llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right) }\right)$ , ${\operatorname{mult}}_{\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  \left( D\right) }\left( {Q\left( \bar{a}\right) }\right)  = {\operatorname{mult}}_{\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  \left( D\right) }\left( a\right) .$

无论$\llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right)$还是$\llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right)$都将$Q$的每个原子映射到数据库$D$，这意味着$U\left( {\llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right) }\right)  =$$U\left( {\llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right) }\right)$，因此现在对于$I\left( {\llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right) }\right)$中的每个$Q\left( \bar{a}\right)$，我们只需证明对于$I\left( {\llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right) }\right)$中的每个$a$，${\operatorname{mult}}_{\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  \left( D\right) }\left( {Q\left( \bar{a}\right) }\right)  = {\operatorname{mult}}_{\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  \left( D\right) }\left( a\right) .$

By following the definitions given previously for the multiplicities we get:

根据前文给出的多重性定义可得：

$$
{\operatorname{mult}}_{\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  \left( D\right) }\left( {Q\left( \bar{a}\right) }\right)  = \left| {\{ j \mid  \left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  \left( D\right) \left( j\right)  = Q\left( \bar{a}\right) \} }\right| 
$$

We also know that if $\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  \left( D\right) \left( j\right)  = Q\left( \bar{a}\right)$ holds,there must be a t-homomorphism $\eta$ such that $Q\left( {{h}_{\eta }\left( \bar{x}\right) }\right)  =$ $Q\left( \bar{a}\right)$ . Since every t-homomorphism can map more than one atom,it is clear that:

我们还知道，若$\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  \left( D\right) \left( j\right)  = Q\left( \bar{a}\right)$成立，则必存在一个t-同态$\eta$使得$Q\left( {{h}_{\eta }\left( \bar{x}\right) }\right)  =$$Q\left( \bar{a}\right)$。由于每个t-同态可映射多个原子，显然：

$$
{\operatorname{mult}}_{\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  \left( D\right) }\left( {Q\left( \bar{a}\right) }\right)  = \left| {\mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{\substack{{\eta  \in  \operatorname{Hom}\left( {Q,D}\right) } \\  {{h}_{\eta }\left( \bar{x}\right)  = \bar{a}} }}\{ j \mid  \left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  \left( D\right) \left( j\right)  = Q\left( {{h}_{\eta }\left( \bar{x}\right) }\right) \} }\right| 
$$

$$
 = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{\substack{{\eta  \in  \operatorname{t-Hom}\left( {Q,D}\right)  : } \\  {{h}_{\eta }\left( \bar{x}\right)  = \bar{a}} }}\left| {\{ j \mid  \left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  \left( D\right) \left( j\right)  = Q\left( {{h}_{\eta }\left( \bar{x}\right) }\right) \} }\right| 
$$

$$
 = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{\substack{{\eta  \in  \operatorname{t-Hom}\left( {Q,D}\right)  : } \\  {{h}_{\eta }\left( \bar{x}\right)  = \bar{a}} }}{\operatorname{mult}}_{Q,D}\left( {h}_{\eta }\right) 
$$

As was stated before, there is a correspondence between t-homomorphisms and homomorphism, meaning:

如前所述，t-同态与同态之间存在对应关系，即：

$$
{\operatorname{mult}}_{\lceil Q\rrbracket \left( D\right) }\left( {Q\left( \bar{a}\right) }\right)  = \mathop{\sum }\limits_{\substack{{h \in  \operatorname{Hom}\left( {Q,D}\right)  : } \\  {h\left( \bar{x}\right)  = \bar{a}} }}{\operatorname{mult}}_{Q,D}\left( h\right) 
$$

$$
 = {\operatorname{mult}}_{\llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right) }\left( {Q\left( \bar{a}\right) }\right) 
$$

Given that ${\operatorname{mult}}_{\lceil Q\parallel \left( D\right) }\left( {Q\left( \bar{a}\right) }\right)  = {\operatorname{mult}}_{\lceil Q\rceil \left( D\right) }\left( {Q\left( \bar{a}\right) }\right)$ for every $Q\left( \bar{a}\right)$ in $I\left( {\lceil Q\parallel \left( D\right) }\right)$ ,this implies that ${\operatorname{mult}}_{\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  \left( D\right) }\left( {Q\left( \bar{a}\right) }\right)  = {\operatorname{mult}}_{\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  \left( D\right) }\left( {Q\left( \bar{a}\right) }\right)$ and therefore,for every $\mathrm{{CQ}}Q$ and database $D$ it holds that $\llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right)  = \llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right)$ .

已知对于$I\left( {\lceil Q\parallel \left( D\right) }\right)$中的每个$Q\left( \bar{a}\right)$都有${\operatorname{mult}}_{\lceil Q\parallel \left( D\right) }\left( {Q\left( \bar{a}\right) }\right)  = {\operatorname{mult}}_{\lceil Q\rceil \left( D\right) }\left( {Q\left( \bar{a}\right) }\right)$，这意味着${\operatorname{mult}}_{\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  \left( D\right) }\left( {Q\left( \bar{a}\right) }\right)  = {\operatorname{mult}}_{\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  \left( D\right) }\left( {Q\left( \bar{a}\right) }\right)$，因此对于每个$\mathrm{{CQ}}Q$和数据库$D$都满足$\llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right)  = \llbracket Q\rrbracket \left( D\right)$。

## Proof of Theorem 4.1

## 定理4.1的证明

Proof. Fix a schema $\sigma$ and fix a HCQ $Q$ over $\sigma$ of the form:

证明。固定模式$\sigma$，并固定$\sigma$上形式如下的HCQ$Q$：

$$
Q\left( \bar{x}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{0}\left( {\bar{x}}_{0}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{m - 1}\left( {\bar{x}}_{m - 1}\right) .
$$

Without loss of generality,we assume that $Q$ has at least two atoms,i.e., $m \geq  2$ . If not,it is straightforward to construct a PCEA for $Q$ . Further,for the sake of simplification,we assume that $Q$ does not have data values (i.e., constants); all the constructions below work with data values in the atoms with the additional cost of differentiating the variables from the data values in the set $\left\{  {\bar{x}}_{i}\right\}$ . Given that $Q$ is full,this means that $\{ \bar{x}\}  = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i = 0}}\left\{  {\bar{x}}_{i}\right\}$ . For this reason,we will use $\{ \bar{x}\}$ to refer to the set of all variables in $Q$ . Recall that we usually consider $Q$ as bag of atoms,namely, $Q = \left\{  \left\{  {{R}_{0}\left( {\bar{x}}_{0}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{m - 1}\left( {\bar{x}}_{m - 1}\right) }\right\}  \right\}$ . Then $I\left( Q\right)$ is the set of all the identifiers $\{ 0,\ldots ,m - 1\}$ and $U\left( Q\right)$ the set of all different atoms in $Q$ . We say that $Q$ is connected iff there exists a variable $x \in  \{ \bar{x}\}$ such that $x \in  \left\{  {\bar{x}}_{i}\right\}$ for every $i < m$ .

不失一般性，我们假设$Q$至少包含两个原子，即$m \geq  2$。若不满足此条件，可直接为$Q$构建PCEA。此外，为简化起见，我们假设$Q$不含数据值（即常量）；以下所有构造均可处理含数据值的原子，仅需额外区分集合$\left\{  {\bar{x}}_{i}\right\}$中的变量与数据值。鉴于$Q$是完备的，这意味着$\{ \bar{x}\}  = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i = 0}}\left\{  {\bar{x}}_{i}\right\}$。因此，我们将用$\{ \bar{x}\}$指代$Q$中所有变量的集合。请注意，我们通常将$Q$视为原子包，即$Q = \left\{  \left\{  {{R}_{0}\left( {\bar{x}}_{0}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{m - 1}\left( {\bar{x}}_{m - 1}\right) }\right\}  \right\}$。那么$I\left( Q\right)$是所有标识符$\{ 0,\ldots ,m - 1\}$的集合，而$U\left( Q\right)$是$Q$中所有不同原子的集合。当且仅当存在变量$x \in  \{ \bar{x}\}$使得对于每个$i < m$都有$x \in  \left\{  {\bar{x}}_{i}\right\}$时，我们称$Q$是连通的。
