For the sake of readability we represent each node $\bar{u}$ by the variable/atom in the underlying set of $Q$ that labels the node, $Q\left( {{\tau }_{Q}\left( \bar{u}\right) }\right)$ . Since we have no self joins in ${Q}_{1}$ ,we omit the variables when representing the atoms.

图3：${Q}_{1}$和${Q}_{2}$的有效q树示例。为提升可读性，我们用$Q$基础集中标记节点的变量/原子$Q\left( {{\tau }_{Q}\left( \bar{u}\right) }\right)$来表示每个节点$\bar{u}$。由于${Q}_{1}$中不存在自连接，表示原子时我们省略了变量。

<!-- Media -->

In the following, we first give the proof of the theorem for connected HCQ without self joins, then move to the case of connected HCQ with self joins, and end with the general case.

下文将首先给出无自连接的连通HCQ定理证明，继而讨论含自连接的连通HCQ情形，最后处理一般情况。

Connected HCQ without self joins. A $q$ -tree for $Q$ is a labeled tree, ${\tau }_{Q} : t \rightarrow  I\left( Q\right)  \cup  \{ \bar{x}\}$ ,where for every $x \in  \{ \bar{x}\}$ there is a unique inner node $\bar{u} \in  t$ such that ${\tau }_{Q}\left( \bar{u}\right)  = x$ ,and for every $i \in  I\left( Q\right)$ there is a unique leaf node $\bar{v} \in  t$ such that ${\tau }_{Q}\left( \bar{v}\right)  = i$ and,if ${\bar{u}}_{1},\ldots ,{\bar{u}}_{k}$ are the inner nodes of the path from the root until $\bar{v}$ ,then $\left\{  {\bar{x}}_{i}\right\}   = \left\{  {{\tau }_{Q}\left( {\bar{u}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{\tau }_{Q}\left( {\bar{u}}_{k}\right) }\right\}$ ,i.e., $\left\{  {\bar{x}}_{i}\right\}   = \left\{  {{\tau }_{Q}\left( \bar{u}\right)  \mid  \bar{u} \in  {\operatorname{ancst}}_{{\tau }_{Q}}\left( \bar{v}\right)  \land  \bar{u} \neq  \bar{v}}\right\}$ . As an example,in Figure 3 we can see a q-tree associated with ${Q}_{1}\left( {x,y,z,v,w}\right)  \leftarrow  \{ \{ R\left( {x,y,z}\right) ,S\left( {x,y,v}\right) ,T\left( {x,w}\right) ,U\left( {x,y}\right) \} \}$ and two valid q-trees for the query ${Q}_{2}\left( {x,y,z,v}\right)  \leftarrow  \{ \{ R\left( {x,y,z}\right) ,R\left( {x,y,v}\right) ,U\left( {x,y}\right) \} \}$ . Note that we use the identifiers of the atoms (instead of the atom itself),so if an atom appears $n$ times in the query,there will be $n$ different leaf nodes for the same atom.

无自连接的连通HCQ。对于$Q$的$q$树是一棵带标签的树${\tau }_{Q} : t \rightarrow  I\left( Q\right)  \cup  \{ \bar{x}\}$，其中每个$x \in  \{ \bar{x}\}$都存在唯一内部节点$\bar{u} \in  t$满足${\tau }_{Q}\left( \bar{u}\right)  = x$，且每个$i \in  I\left( Q\right)$都存在唯一叶节点$\bar{v} \in  t$满足${\tau }_{Q}\left( \bar{v}\right)  = i$。若${\bar{u}}_{1},\ldots ,{\bar{u}}_{k}$是从根节点到$\bar{v}$路径上的内部节点，则$\left\{  {\bar{x}}_{i}\right\}   = \left\{  {{\tau }_{Q}\left( {\bar{u}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{\tau }_{Q}\left( {\bar{u}}_{k}\right) }\right\}$，即$\left\{  {\bar{x}}_{i}\right\}   = \left\{  {{\tau }_{Q}\left( \bar{u}\right)  \mid  \bar{u} \in  {\operatorname{ancst}}_{{\tau }_{Q}}\left( \bar{v}\right)  \land  \bar{u} \neq  \bar{v}}\right\}$。例如图3展示了与${Q}_{1}\left( {x,y,z,v,w}\right)  \leftarrow  \{ \{ R\left( {x,y,z}\right) ,S\left( {x,y,v}\right) ,T\left( {x,w}\right) ,U\left( {x,y}\right) \} \}$关联的q-树，以及查询${Q}_{2}\left( {x,y,z,v}\right)  \leftarrow  \{ \{ R\left( {x,y,z}\right) ,R\left( {x,y,v}\right) ,U\left( {x,y}\right) \} \}$对应的两棵有效q-树。注意我们使用原子标识符（而非原子本身），因此若某原子在查询中出现$n$次，同一原子将对应$n$个不同的叶节点。

Theorem B.1 ([5). $Q$ is hierarchical and connected iff there exists a $q$ -tree for $Q$ .

定理B.1（[5）。当且仅当存在$Q$的$q$树时，$Q$是层次化且连通的。

In the following,we fix a q-tree for $Q$ that we denote by ${\tau }_{Q}$ . Furthermore,given that there is a one-to-one correspondence between nodes of ${\tau }_{Q}$ and the set $I\left( Q\right)  \cup  \{ \bar{x}\}$ ,by some abuse of notation,we will usually use variables and identifiers as nodes in ${\tau }_{Q}$ (e.g.,des ${\mathrm{c}}_{{\tau }_{Q}}\left( x\right)$ or ${\operatorname{ancst}}_{{\tau }_{Q}}\left( i\right)$ ).

下文我们将固定用${\tau }_{Q}$表示$Q$的q-树。鉴于${\tau }_{Q}$的节点与集合$I\left( Q\right)  \cup  \{ \bar{x}\}$存在一一对应关系，在不引起歧义的情况下，我们通常直接使用变量和标识符作为${\tau }_{Q}$中的节点（例如描述${\mathrm{c}}_{{\tau }_{Q}}\left( x\right)$或${\operatorname{ancst}}_{{\tau }_{Q}}\left( i\right)$）。

We define the compact $q$ -tree of an HCQ $Q$ as the result of taking the original $\mathrm{q}$ -tree of the query and removing all inner nodes with a single child. In other words,to generate the compact q-tree ${\tau }_{Q}^{c}$ from ${\tau }_{Q}$ , we copy ${\tau }_{Q}$ ,then for each node $\bar{u} \in  {\tau }_{Q}^{c}$ such that children ${\tau }_{Q}^{c}\left( \bar{u}\right)  = \{ \bar{v}\}$ ,we remove $\bar{v}$ and ${\tau }_{Q}^{c}\left( \bar{v}\right)$ from ${\tau }_{Q}^{c}$ , and we redefine children ${\tau }_{O}^{c}\left( \bar{u}\right)  = {\operatorname{children}}_{{\tau }_{O}^{c}}\left( \bar{v}\right)$ . We can see in Figure 4 the compact $q$ -trees associated with each of the q-trees of Figure 3,Note that for every q-tree ${\tau }_{Q}$ ,the root of ${\tau }_{Q}^{c}$ is always variable and has at least two children since we assume that $Q$ has at least two atoms (and then ${\tau }_{Q}$ has at least two leaves). For simplification,from now on,we assume that ${\tau }_{Q}$ is compact. If not,all the constructions below hold verbatim by replacing ${\tau }_{Q}$ by ${\tau }_{Q}^{c}$ and restricting to the variables that appear in ${\tau }_{Q}^{c}$ .

我们将HCQ $Q$的紧凑$q$树定义为：获取查询的原始$\mathrm{q}$树后，移除所有仅含单个子节点的内部节点。换言之，要从${\tau }_{Q}$生成紧凑q树${\tau }_{Q}^{c}$，我们先复制${\tau }_{Q}$，然后对于每个满足子节点${\tau }_{Q}^{c}\left( \bar{u}\right)  = \{ \bar{v}\}$的节点$\bar{u} \in  {\tau }_{Q}^{c}$，从${\tau }_{Q}^{c}$中移除$\bar{v}$和${\tau }_{Q}^{c}\left( \bar{v}\right)$，并重新定义子节点${\tau }_{O}^{c}\left( \bar{u}\right)  = {\operatorname{children}}_{{\tau }_{O}^{c}}\left( \bar{v}\right)$。图4展示了与图3中各个q树对应的紧凑$q$树。注意对于任何q树${\tau }_{Q}$，由于我们假设$Q$至少包含两个原子（因此${\tau }_{Q}$至少有两个叶节点），${\tau }_{Q}^{c}$的根节点始终是变量且至少有两个子节点。为简化起见，下文默认${\tau }_{Q}$已是紧凑形式。若非如此，只需将${\tau }_{Q}$替换为${\tau }_{Q}^{c}$并限定于出现在${\tau }_{Q}^{c}$中的变量，以下所有构造均保持原样成立。

We can now proceed with the proof. From $Q$ we construct the PCEA:

现在我们可以继续证明。根据$Q$构造PCEA如下：

$$
{\mathcal{P}}_{Q} = \left( {I\left( Q\right) \cup \{ \bar{x}\} ,{\mathbf{U}}_{\text{lin }},{\mathbf{B}}_{\text{eq }},I\left( Q\right) ,\Delta ,\left\{  {{\tau }_{Q}\left( \varepsilon \right) }\right\}  }\right) 
$$

where the states of the automaton are the nodes of ${\tau }_{Q}$ ,the labeling set is the set off atom identifiers of the query $Q$ ,and the only final state is the variable at root of ${\tau }_{Q}$ (i.e., ${\tau }_{Q}\left( \varepsilon \right)  = {\tau }_{Q}\left( {\operatorname{root}\left( {\tau }_{Q}\right) }\right)$ ). For defining the transition relation $\Delta$ ,we need additional definitions regarding predicates and some further constructs over ${\tau }_{Q}$ .

其中自动机的状态是${\tau }_{Q}$的节点，标记集是查询$Q$的原子标识符集合，唯一终态是${\tau }_{Q}$根部的变量节点（即${\tau }_{Q}\left( \varepsilon \right)  = {\tau }_{Q}\left( {\operatorname{root}\left( {\tau }_{Q}\right) }\right)$）。为定义转移关系$\Delta$，我们需要关于谓词的附加定义以及${\tau }_{Q}$上的一些进一步构造。

---

${}^{1}$ Given that $Q$ is hierarchical,this definition is equivalent to the notion of connected CQ,i.e.,that the Gaifman graph of $Q$ is connected.

${}^{1}$ 鉴于$Q$是层次化的，该定义等价于连通CQ的概念，即$Q$的盖夫曼图是连通的。

Figure 4: Example of compact q-trees for ${\tau }_{{Q}_{1}},{\tau }_{{Q}_{2}}$ and ${\tau }_{{Q}_{2}}^{\prime }$ . Since we have no self joins in ${Q}_{1}$ ,we omit the variables when representing the atoms.

图4：${\tau }_{{Q}_{1}},{\tau }_{{Q}_{2}}$和${\tau }_{{Q}_{2}}^{\prime }$的紧凑q树示例。由于${Q}_{1}$中不存在自连接，表示原子时我们省略了变量。

<!-- Media -->

Below,we use $h : \{ \bar{x}\}  \cup  \mathbf{D} \rightarrow  \mathbf{D}$ to denote a homomorphism and Hom to denote the set of all homomorphisms. Let $x \in  \{ \bar{x}\}$ be a variable and let $S\left( \bar{y}\right) ,T\left( \bar{z}\right)  \in  U\left( Q\right)$ be atoms of $Q$ . We define the unary predicate of $S\left( \bar{y}\right)$ as:

下文用$h : \{ \bar{x}\}  \cup  \mathbf{D} \rightarrow  \mathbf{D}$表示同态，用Hom表示所有同态的集合。设$x \in  \{ \bar{x}\}$为变量，$S\left( \bar{y}\right) ,T\left( \bar{z}\right)  \in  U\left( Q\right)$为$Q$的原子。我们将$S\left( \bar{y}\right)$的一元谓词定义为：

$$
{U}_{S\left( \bar{y}\right) } \mathrel{\text{:=}} \{ S\left( \bar{b}\right)  \in  \operatorname{Tuples}\left\lbrack  \sigma \right\rbrack   \mid  \exists h \in  \operatorname{Hom}.h\left( {S\left( \bar{y}\right) }\right)  = S\left( \bar{b}\right) \} 
$$

and the binary predicate of $S\left( \bar{y}\right)$ and $T\left( \bar{z}\right)$ as:

并将$S\left( \bar{y}\right)$与$T\left( \bar{z}\right)$的二元谓词定义为：

$$
{B}_{S\left( \bar{y}\right) ,T\left( \bar{z}\right) } \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\{  {\left( {S\left( \bar{b}\right) ,T\left( \bar{c}\right) }\right) \mid \exists h \in  \operatorname{Hom}.h\left( {S\left( \bar{y}\right) }\right)  = S\left( \bar{b}\right)  \land  h\left( {T\left( \bar{z}\right) }\right)  = T\left( \bar{c}\right) }\right\}  .
$$

One can easily check that ${U}_{S\left( \bar{y}\right) } \in  {\mathbf{U}}_{\text{lin }}$ and ${B}_{S\left( \bar{y}\right) ,T\left( \bar{z}\right) }$ is an equality predicate for every pair of atoms $S\left( \bar{y}\right)$ and $T\left( \bar{z}\right)$ .

容易验证，对于任意原子对$S\left( \bar{y}\right)$和$T\left( \bar{z}\right)$，${U}_{S\left( \bar{y}\right) } \in  {\mathbf{U}}_{\text{lin }}$与${B}_{S\left( \bar{y}\right) ,T\left( \bar{z}\right) }$构成等价谓词。

We extend the definition of ${B}_{S\left( \bar{y}\right) ,T\left( \bar{z}\right) }$ to a pair variable-atom as follows. Take again the atom $S\left( \bar{y}\right)$ and let $x$ be a variable such that $x \notin  \{ \bar{y}\}$ . Note that this implies that $\{ \bar{y}\}  \cap  \left\{  {\bar{x}}_{i}\right\}   = \{ \bar{y}\}  \cap  \left\{  {\bar{x}}_{j}\right\}$ for every pair of distinct atoms $i,j \in  {\operatorname{desc}}_{{\tau }_{Q}}\left( x\right)$ . In other words,all atoms (i.e.,identifiers) in the q-tree below $x$ share the same variables with $S\left( \bar{y}\right)$ . Define the binary predicate of $x$ and $S\left( \bar{y}\right)$ as:

我们将${B}_{S\left( \bar{y}\right) ,T\left( \bar{z}\right) }$的定义扩展为变量-原子对，具体如下。再次取原子$S\left( \bar{y}\right)$，并设$x$为一个变量，使得$x \notin  \{ \bar{y}\}$。注意这意味着对于任意两个不同的原子$i,j \in  {\operatorname{desc}}_{{\tau }_{Q}}\left( x\right)$，都有$\{ \bar{y}\}  \cap  \left\{  {\bar{x}}_{i}\right\}   = \{ \bar{y}\}  \cap  \left\{  {\bar{x}}_{j}\right\}$。换言之，在$x$下方的q-树中，所有原子（即标识符）都与$S\left( \bar{y}\right)$共享相同的变量。将$x$与$S\left( \bar{y}\right)$的二元谓词定义为：

$$
{B}_{x,S\left( \bar{y}\right) } = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i \in  {\operatorname{desc}}_{{\tau }_{Q}}\left( x\right) }}{B}_{{R}_{i}\left( {\bar{x}}_{i}\right) ,S\left( \bar{y}\right) }.
$$

Given the previous discussion and given that $Q$ does not have self joins (all atoms are different),one can easily check that ${B}_{x,S\left( \bar{y}\right) }$ is an equality predicate in ${\mathbf{B}}_{\mathrm{{eq}}}$ .

基于前述讨论，并考虑到$Q$不存在自连接（所有原子均不相同），可以轻松验证${B}_{x,S\left( \bar{y}\right) }$是${\mathbf{B}}_{\mathrm{{eq}}}$中的等价谓词。

For our last definition,let $i \in  I\left( Q\right)$ be an identifier of $Q$ and $x \in  \left\{  {\bar{x}}_{i}\right\}$ . Define the set of incomplete states of $x$ given $i$ as:

对于最后一个定义，令$i \in  I\left( Q\right)$作为$Q$和$x \in  \left\{  {\bar{x}}_{i}\right\}$的标识符。给定$i$时，将$x$的不完全状态集合定义为：

$$
{C}_{x,i} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\{  {\ell  \in  I\left( Q\right)  \cup  \{ \bar{x}\}  \mid  {\operatorname{parent}}_{{\tau }_{Q}}\left( \ell \right)  \in  \left( {{\operatorname{desc}}_{{\tau }_{Q}}\left( x\right)  \cap  \left\{  {\bar{x}}_{i}\right\}  }\right) }\right\}   \smallsetminus  \left( {\{ i\}  \cup  \left\{  {\bar{x}}_{i}\right\}  }\right) 
$$

In other words, ${C}_{x,i}$ is the set of all variables or atom identifiers that hang from a variable of ${\bar{x}}_{i}$ that is a descendant of $x$ ,except for variables in ${\bar{x}}_{i}$ or the atom $i$ . For example,in ${\tau }_{{Q}_{1}}$ of Figure 4 one can check that ${C}_{y,U} = \{ R,S,T\}$ and ${C}_{x,T} = \{ y\}$ .

换言之，${C}_{x,i}$是所有悬挂于${\bar{x}}_{i}$变量上的变量或原子标识符的集合，这些变量是$x$的后代，但不包括${\bar{x}}_{i}$中的变量或原子$i$。例如，在图4的${\tau }_{{Q}_{1}}$中可验证${C}_{y,U} = \{ R,S,T\}$和${C}_{x,T} = \{ y\}$。

With these definitions,we define the transition relation $\Delta$ as follows:

基于这些定义，我们将转移关系$\Delta$定义如下：

$$
\Delta  \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\{  {\left( {\varnothing ,{U}_{{R}_{i}\left( {\bar{x}}_{i}\right) },\varnothing ,\{ i\} ,i}\right)  \mid  i \in  I\left( Q\right) }\right\}  
$$

$$
 \cup  \left\{  {\left( {{C}_{x,i},{U}_{{R}_{i}\left( {\bar{x}}_{i}\right) },{\mathcal{B}}_{x,i},\{ i\} ,x}\right)  \mid  i \in  I\left( Q\right)  \land  x \in  \left\{  {\bar{x}}_{i}\right\}  }\right\}  
$$

such that ${\mathcal{B}}_{x,i}$ is the predicate function associated with ${C}_{x,i}$ defined as follows: ${\mathcal{B}}_{x,i}\left( y\right)  = {B}_{y,{R}_{i}\left( {\bar{x}}_{i}\right) }$ for every variable $y \in  {C}_{x,i}$ and ${\mathcal{B}}_{x,i}\left( j\right)  = {B}_{{R}_{j}\left( {\bar{x}}_{j}\right) ,{R}_{i}\left( {\bar{x}}_{i}\right) }$ for every identifier $j \in  {C}_{x,i}$ . Intuitively, $\Delta$ can be interpreted as the traversal and completion of the compact q-tree, with the first set of transitions representing the independent leaf nodes being completed by their respective atom, while the second set represents the completion of a variable, meaning the last tuple in its descendants was found.

其中${\mathcal{B}}_{x,i}$是与${C}_{x,i}$关联的谓词函数，其定义为：对每个变量$y \in  {C}_{x,i}$有${\mathcal{B}}_{x,i}\left( y\right)  = {B}_{y,{R}_{i}\left( {\bar{x}}_{i}\right) }$，对每个标识符$j \in  {C}_{x,i}$有${\mathcal{B}}_{x,i}\left( j\right)  = {B}_{{R}_{j}\left( {\bar{x}}_{j}\right) ,{R}_{i}\left( {\bar{x}}_{i}\right) }$。直观上，$\Delta$可解释为紧凑q树的遍历与完成过程——第一组转移表示独立叶节点由其对应原子完成，第二组转移表示变量的完成，即其派生节点中最后一个元组已被找到。

From the previous construction,one can easily check by inspection that ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$ is of quadratic size with respect to $Q$ . In the sequel,we prove that ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$ is unambiguous and ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q} \equiv  Q$ . To prove both of these conditions, we will use the following lemma:

由前述构造可直观验证，${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$相对于$Q$具有二次规模。后续我们将证明${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$是明确的且满足${\mathcal{P}}_{Q} \equiv  Q$。为证明这两个条件，需使用以下引理：

Lemma B.2. For every accepting run $\rho$ of ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$ and for every atom $i \in  I\left( Q\right)$ ,there is exactly one node $\bar{u} \in  \rho$ such that $\rho \left( \bar{u}\right)  = \left( {p,j,\{ i\} }\right)$ . Proof. Let ${i}_{R} \in  I\left( Q\right)$ be an atom with $Q\left( {i}_{R}\right)  = R\left( \bar{x}\right)$ . Since $\rho$ is an accepting run of ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$ ,its root must be labeled by the root of the compact q-tree, $\rho \left( {\operatorname{root}\left( \rho \right) }\right)  = \left( {r,j,\left\{  {i}_{R}\right\}  }\right)$ with $r = \operatorname{root}\left( {\tau }_{Q}\right)$ and ${i}_{S} \in  I\left( Q\right)$ such that $Q\left( {i}_{S}\right)  = S\left( \bar{y}\right)$ . Assuming a non trivial case,i.e. ${i}_{R} \neq  {i}_{S}$ ,there must be a transition $\left( {{C}_{r,{i}_{S}},{U}_{S\left( \bar{y}\right) },{\mathcal{B}}_{r,{i}_{S}},\left\{  {i}_{S}\right\}  ,r}\right)  \in  \Delta$ ,which leaves us with two recursive cases.

引理B.2。对于${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$的每个接受运行$\rho$及每个原子$i \in  I\left( Q\right)$，存在唯一节点$\bar{u} \in  \rho$使得$\rho \left( \bar{u}\right)  = \left( {p,j,\{ i\} }\right)$。证明：设${i}_{R} \in  I\left( Q\right)$是满足$Q\left( {i}_{R}\right)  = R\left( \bar{x}\right)$的原子。由于$\rho$是${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$的接受运行，其根节点必须标记为紧凑q树的根$\rho \left( {\operatorname{root}\left( \rho \right) }\right)  = \left( {r,j,\left\{  {i}_{R}\right\}  }\right)$，其中$r = \operatorname{root}\left( {\tau }_{Q}\right)$和${i}_{S} \in  I\left( Q\right)$满足$Q\left( {i}_{S}\right)  = S\left( \bar{y}\right)$。在非平凡情况下（即${i}_{R} \neq  {i}_{S}$），必存在转移$\left( {{C}_{r,{i}_{S}},{U}_{S\left( \bar{y}\right) },{\mathcal{B}}_{r,{i}_{S}},\left\{  {i}_{S}\right\}  ,r}\right)  \in  \Delta$，这将导致两种递归情形。

First,the case where none of the ancestors of ${i}_{r}$ are present in ${C}_{r,{i}_{S}}$ ,which will be the base case. Looking at the definition of ${C}_{r,{i}_{S}}$ and considering that ${i}_{S} \neq  {i}_{R}$ ,this means that ${i}_{R}$ must be present in ${C}_{r,{i}_{S}}$ and therefore,there must be a node labeled by $\left( {{i}_{R},k,\left\{  {i}_{R}\right\}  }\right)$ .

首先，当${i}_{r}$的祖先节点均不存在于${C}_{r,{i}_{S}}$时的情况，这将作为基础情形。根据${C}_{r,{i}_{S}}$的定义并考虑到${i}_{S} \neq  {i}_{R}$，这意味着${i}_{R}$必须存在于${C}_{r,{i}_{S}}$中，因此必然存在一个标记为$\left( {{i}_{R},k,\left\{  {i}_{R}\right\}  }\right)$的节点。

On the other hand,if one of the ancestors of ${i}_{R}$ is present in ${C}_{r,{i}_{S}}$ the case will be recursive. Let this ancestor be $s \in  {\operatorname{ancst}}_{{\tau }_{Q}}\left( {i}_{R}\right)  \cap  {C}_{r,{i}_{S}}$ and since it is present in ${C}_{r,{i}_{S}}$ ,there must be a transition $\left( {{C}_{s,{i}_{T}},{U}_{{i}_{T}},{P}_{{i}_{T},C},\left\{  {i}_{T}\right\}  ,s}\right)  \in  \Delta$ ,leaving us with the same three cases as before; the trivial case where ${i}_{T} = {i}_{R}$ ,the base case where there are no ancestors of ${i}_{R}$ in ${C}_{s,{i}_{T}}$ and the current recursive case. If we repeat the recursive case,we will eventually visit every ancestor of ${i}_{R}$ ,leaving us with either the trivial or the base case,meaning there exists a node $\bar{u} \in  \rho$ such that $\rho \left( \bar{u}\right)  = \left( {p,j,\left\{  {i}_{R}\right\}  }\right)$ .

另一方面，若${i}_{R}$的某个祖先节点存在于${C}_{r,{i}_{S}}$中，则属于递归情形。设该祖先节点为$s \in  {\operatorname{ancst}}_{{\tau }_{Q}}\left( {i}_{R}\right)  \cap  {C}_{r,{i}_{S}}$，由于它存在于${C}_{r,{i}_{S}}$中，必然存在转移$\left( {{C}_{s,{i}_{T}},{U}_{{i}_{T}},{P}_{{i}_{T},C},\left\{  {i}_{T}\right\}  ,s}\right)  \in  \Delta$，这将使我们面临与之前相同的三种情形：${i}_{T} = {i}_{R}$的平凡情形、${C}_{s,{i}_{T}}$中不存在${i}_{R}$祖先节点的基础情形，以及当前的递归情形。若重复递归情形，最终会遍历${i}_{R}$的所有祖先节点，最终归结为平凡情形或基础情形，这意味着存在满足$\rho \left( \bar{u}\right)  = \left( {p,j,\left\{  {i}_{R}\right\}  }\right)$的节点$\bar{u} \in  \rho$。

We have proven that for every atom ${i}_{R} \in  I\left( Q\right)$ ,there is a node ${\bar{u}}_{R} \in  \rho$ such that $\rho \left( {\bar{u}}_{R}\right)  = \left( {{p}_{R},{j}_{R},\left\{  {i}_{R}\right\}  }\right)$ and $Q\left( {i}_{R}\right)  = R\left( {\bar{x}}_{R}\right)$ . Following the definition $\Delta$ ,one can easily see that if ${\bar{u}}_{R}$ is an inner node, ${i}_{R} \in$ ${\operatorname{desc}}_{{\tau }_{Q}}\left( {p}_{R}\right)$ and if ${\bar{u}}_{R}$ is a leaf node, ${p}_{R} = {i}_{R}$ .

我们已证明对于每个原子${i}_{R} \in  I\left( Q\right)$，都存在满足$\rho \left( {\bar{u}}_{R}\right)  = \left( {{p}_{R},{j}_{R},\left\{  {i}_{R}\right\}  }\right)$且$Q\left( {i}_{R}\right)  = R\left( {\bar{x}}_{R}\right)$的节点${\bar{u}}_{R} \in  \rho$。根据定义$\Delta$可明显看出：若${\bar{u}}_{R}$是内部节点，则${i}_{R} \in$${\operatorname{desc}}_{{\tau }_{Q}}\left( {p}_{R}\right)$；若${\bar{u}}_{R}$是叶节点，则${p}_{R} = {i}_{R}$。

On the other hand for each inner node, there must exist a transition of the form:

另一方面，对于每个内部节点，必然存在以下形式的转移：

$$
\left( {{C}_{{p}_{R},{i}_{R}},{U}_{R\left( {\bar{x}}_{R}\right) },{\mathcal{B}}_{{p}_{R},{i}_{R}},\left\{  {i}_{R}\right\}  ,{p}_{R}}\right)  \in  \Delta 
$$

and from the definition of ${C}_{{p}_{R},{i}_{R}}$ it follows that for each $S\left( {\bar{x}}_{S}\right)  \in  {\operatorname{desc}}_{{\tau }_{O}}\left( {p}_{R}\right)$ ,with $S\left( {\bar{x}}_{S}\right)  \neq  R\left( {\bar{x}}_{R}\right)$ and $Q\left( {i}_{S}\right)  = S\left( {\bar{x}}_{S}\right)$ ,there is exactly one node ${\bar{u}}_{S} \in  \rho$ such that $\rho \left( {\bar{u}}_{S}\right)  = \left( {{i}_{S},{j}_{S},\left\{  {i}_{S}\right\}  }\right)$ or $\rho \left( {\bar{u}}_{S}\right)  = \left( {{p}_{S},{j}_{S},\{ i\} }\right)$ where if $i \neq  {i}_{S},{i}_{S} \in  {\operatorname{desc}}_{{\tau }_{Q}}\left( {p}_{S}\right)$ . Since each transition of $\Delta$ has exactly one atom as its label,then there will be exactly one node in the run marked by each atom.

根据${C}_{{p}_{R},{i}_{R}}$的定义可推知，对于每个$S\left( {\bar{x}}_{S}\right)  \in  {\operatorname{desc}}_{{\tau }_{O}}\left( {p}_{R}\right)$（其中$S\left( {\bar{x}}_{S}\right)  \neq  R\left( {\bar{x}}_{R}\right)$和$Q\left( {i}_{S}\right)  = S\left( {\bar{x}}_{S}\right)$），存在唯一节点${\bar{u}}_{S} \in  \rho$使得$\rho \left( {\bar{u}}_{S}\right)  = \left( {{i}_{S},{j}_{S},\left\{  {i}_{S}\right\}  }\right)$或$\rho \left( {\bar{u}}_{S}\right)  = \left( {{p}_{S},{j}_{S},\{ i\} }\right)$成立，此处若$i \neq  {i}_{S},{i}_{S} \in  {\operatorname{desc}}_{{\tau }_{Q}}\left( {p}_{S}\right)$。由于$\Delta$的每个转移都仅有一个原子作为标记，因此在运行过程中每个原子将精确标记一个节点。

The automaton ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$ will be unambiguous iff every accepting run of ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$ is simple and for every stream $\mathcal{S}$ and a valuation $\nu$ ,there is at most one run $\rho$ of ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$ over $\mathcal{S}$ such that ${\nu }_{\rho } = \nu$ . Every transition of ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$ uses a single label $i \in  I\left( Q\right)$ ,which combined with the correspondence between atoms and nodes in each run of directly indicates that every accepting run of ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$ is simple.

当且仅当满足以下条件时，自动机${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$才是无歧义的：${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$的每个接受运行都是简单的，且对于每个流$\mathcal{S}$和赋值$\nu$，至多存在一个运行$\rho$使得${\nu }_{\rho } = \nu$成立。由于${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$的每个转移都使用单一标签$i \in  I\left( Q\right)$，结合原子与运行节点间的对应关系，可直接证明${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$的所有接受运行都是简单的。

On the other hand,it is easy to see that given a tuple $R\left( \bar{a}\right)  \in  \mathcal{S}$ ,there will only be a single initial transition $\left( {\varnothing ,{U}_{{R}_{i}\left( {\bar{x}}_{i}\right) },\varnothing ,\{ i\} ,i}\right)  \in  \Delta$ such that $R\left( \bar{a}\right)  \in  {U}_{{R}_{i}\left( {\bar{x}}_{i}\right) }$ and given a set of possible states for a run of ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q},C$ there will be at most one transition,due to the definition of ${C}_{x,i}$ ,that can be taken by ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$ , meaning there will be at most one accepting run for each corresponding output and therefore ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$ will be unambiguous.

另一方面，显然对于给定元组$R\left( \bar{a}\right)  \in  \mathcal{S}$，仅存在唯一的初始转移$\left( {\varnothing ,{U}_{{R}_{i}\left( {\bar{x}}_{i}\right) },\varnothing ,\{ i\} ,i}\right)  \in  \Delta$满足$R\left( \bar{a}\right)  \in  {U}_{{R}_{i}\left( {\bar{x}}_{i}\right) }$；且根据${C}_{x,i}$的定义，对于${\mathcal{P}}_{Q},C$运行的任何可能状态集，${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$至多只能执行一个转移。这意味着每个对应输出至多存在一个接受运行，因此${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$具有无歧义性。

Now proving that ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q} \equiv  Q$ ,this will hold iff ${\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{P}}_{Q}\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  = {\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$ for every position $n \in  \mathbb{N}$ and every stream $\mathcal{S}$ ,which means we need to prove the following two conditions:

现证明${\mathcal{P}}_{Q} \equiv  Q$成立当且仅当：对于每个位置$n \in  \mathbb{N}$和每个流$\mathcal{S}$都满足${\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{P}}_{Q}\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  = {\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$，这意味着需要证明以下两个条件：

- ${\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{P}}_{Q}\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  \subseteq  {\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$ . The output of the automaton ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$ over the stream $\mathcal{S} = {t}_{0}{t}_{1}\ldots$ at position $n$ is defined as the set of valuations:

- ${\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{P}}_{Q}\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  \subseteq  {\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$。自动机${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$在流$\mathcal{S} = {t}_{0}{t}_{1}\ldots$上位置$n$处的输出定义为赋值集合：

$$
{\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{P}}_{Q}\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  = \left\{  {{\nu }_{\rho } \mid  \rho \text{ is an accepting run of }{\mathcal{P}}_{Q}\text{ over }S\text{ at position }n}\right\}  
$$

meaning that for every valuation ${\nu }_{\rho } \in  {\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{P}}_{Q}\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$ there is an associated accepting run tree $\rho  : t \rightarrow$ $\left( {{2}^{\Omega }\smallsetminus \{ \varnothing \} }\right)$ .

这意味着对于每个估值${\nu }_{\rho } \in  {\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{P}}_{Q}\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$，都存在一个关联的接受运行树$\rho  : t \rightarrow$$\left( {{2}^{\Omega }\smallsetminus \{ \varnothing \} }\right)$。

Because of Lemma B.2,we know that for every atom ${i}_{R} \in  I\left( Q\right)$ ,with $Q\left( {i}_{R}\right)  = R\left( {\bar{x}}_{R}\right)$ ,there is a node ${\bar{u}}_{R} \in  \rho$ such that $\rho \left( {\bar{u}}_{R}\right)  = \left( {{p}_{R},{j}_{R},\left\{  {i}_{R}\right\}  }\right)$ . Using this,we can define $\eta  : I\left( Q\right)  \rightarrow  I\left( {{D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  }\right)$ and ${h}_{\eta } : X \cup  {D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack   \rightarrow  {D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack$ such that for every ${i}_{R} \in  I\left( Q\right) ,\eta \left( {i}_{R}\right)  = {j}_{R}$ and:

根据引理B.2，我们可知对于每个原子${i}_{R} \in  I\left( Q\right)$（其中$Q\left( {i}_{R}\right)  = R\left( {\bar{x}}_{R}\right)$），存在节点${\bar{u}}_{R} \in  \rho$使得$\rho \left( {\bar{u}}_{R}\right)  = \left( {{p}_{R},{j}_{R},\left\{  {i}_{R}\right\}  }\right)$。由此可定义$\eta  : I\left( Q\right)  \rightarrow  I\left( {{D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  }\right)$和${h}_{\eta } : X \cup  {D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack   \rightarrow  {D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack$，使得对于每个${i}_{R} \in  I\left( Q\right) ,\eta \left( {i}_{R}\right)  = {j}_{R}$满足：

$$
{h}_{\eta }\left( x\right)  = \left\{  \begin{array}{ll} x & x \in  {D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \\  {D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{R}\right) & x \in  \mathbf{X} \end{array}\right. 
$$

Since ${h}_{\eta }\left( a\right)  = a$ for every $a \in  {D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack$ ,it is a homomorphism,so if ${h}_{\eta }$ is well-defined it will be a homomorphism from $Q$ to ${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack$ and $\eta$ will be a t-homomorphism from $Q$ to ${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack$ with ${h}_{\eta }$ as its corresponding homomorphism.

由于对每个$a \in  {D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack$都有${h}_{\eta }\left( a\right)  = a$，这是一个同态映射。因此若${h}_{\eta }$定义良好，则其将成为从$Q$到${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack$的同态映射，且$\eta$将成为以${h}_{\eta }$为对应同态映射的、从$Q$到${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack$的t-同态映射。

Consider the atoms ${i}_{R},{i}_{T} \in  I\left( Q\right)$ and the nodes of the run tree ${\bar{u}}_{R},{\bar{u}}_{T} \in  \rho$ such that $\rho \left( {\bar{u}}_{R}\right)  =$ $\left( {{p}_{R},{j}_{R},\left\{  {i}_{R}\right\}  }\right) ,\rho \left( {\bar{u}}_{T}\right)  = \left( {{p}_{T},{j}_{T},\left\{  {i}_{T}\right\}  }\right) ,Q\left( {i}_{R}\right)  = R\left( {\bar{x}}_{R}\right)$ and $Q\left( {i}_{T}\right)  = T\left( {\bar{x}}_{T}\right)$ . We need to prove that for every $x \in  \mathbf{X},{h}_{\eta }\left( x\right)$ has a single value; in other words,if $z \in  \left\{  {{\bar{x}}_{R} \cap  {\bar{x}}_{T}}\right\}$ ,then ${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{R}\right) .z =$ ${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{T}\right) .z$ . Given the structure of the q-tree,the path from the root to every atom corresponds to the variables of the atom,so $z \in  {\operatorname{ancst}}_{{\tau }_{Q}}\left( {R\left( {\bar{x}}_{R}\right) }\right)  \cap  {\operatorname{ancst}}_{{\tau }_{Q}}\left( {T\left( {\bar{x}}_{T}\right) }\right)$ .

考虑原子${i}_{R},{i}_{T} \in  I\left( Q\right)$与运行树节点${\bar{u}}_{R},{\bar{u}}_{T} \in  \rho$，使得$\rho \left( {\bar{u}}_{R}\right)  =$$\left( {{p}_{R},{j}_{R},\left\{  {i}_{R}\right\}  }\right) ,\rho \left( {\bar{u}}_{T}\right)  = \left( {{p}_{T},{j}_{T},\left\{  {i}_{T}\right\}  }\right) ,Q\left( {i}_{R}\right)  = R\left( {\bar{x}}_{R}\right)$且$Q\left( {i}_{T}\right)  = T\left( {\bar{x}}_{T}\right)$。我们需要证明对于每个$x \in  \mathbf{X},{h}_{\eta }\left( x\right)$都具有单一值；换言之，若$z \in  \left\{  {{\bar{x}}_{R} \cap  {\bar{x}}_{T}}\right\}$，则${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{R}\right) .z =$${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{T}\right) .z$。根据q树的结构，从根节点到每个原子的路径对应着该原子的变量，因此$z \in  {\operatorname{ancst}}_{{\tau }_{Q}}\left( {R\left( {\bar{x}}_{R}\right) }\right)  \cap  {\operatorname{ancst}}_{{\tau }_{Q}}\left( {T\left( {\bar{x}}_{T}\right) }\right)$。

For the purpose of simplification we will start by assuming that one of the states of the run is marked by $z$ ,and then explain the general case. Let ${i}_{S} \in  I\left( Q\right)$ be an atom and ${\bar{u}}_{S} \in  \rho$ be a node of the run tree such that $\rho \left( {\bar{u}}_{S}\right)  = \left( {z,{j}_{S},\left\{  {i}_{S}\right\}  }\right)$ and $Q\left( {i}_{S}\right)  = S\left( {\bar{x}}_{S}\right)$ ,which means there must be a transition of the form, $\left( {{C}_{z,{i}_{S}},{U}_{{S}_{i}\left( {\bar{x}}_{S}\right) },{\mathcal{B}}_{z,{i}_{S}},\left\{  {i}_{S}\right\}  ,z}\right)$ in $\Delta$ . Given $z \in  {\operatorname{ancst}}_{{\tau }_{Q}}\left( {R\left( {\bar{x}}_{R}\right) }\right)$ ,we have two possible cases.

为简化分析，我们首先假设运行状态的某个标记为$z$，再解释普遍情况。设${i}_{S} \in  I\left( Q\right)$为原子，${\bar{u}}_{S} \in  \rho$为运行树节点，满足$\rho \left( {\bar{u}}_{S}\right)  = \left( {z,{j}_{S},\left\{  {i}_{S}\right\}  }\right)$且$Q\left( {i}_{S}\right)  = S\left( {\bar{x}}_{S}\right)$，这意味着在$\Delta$中必然存在形式为$\left( {{C}_{z,{i}_{S}},{U}_{{S}_{i}\left( {\bar{x}}_{S}\right) },{\mathcal{B}}_{z,{i}_{S}},\left\{  {i}_{S}\right\}  ,z}\right)$的转移。给定$z \in  {\operatorname{ancst}}_{{\tau }_{Q}}\left( {R\left( {\bar{x}}_{R}\right) }\right)$，我们面临两种可能情形。

Case (1) corresponds to ${p}_{R} \in  {C}_{z,{i}_{S}}$ . This implies that the tuples associated with ${i}_{R}$ and ${i}_{S}$ must satisfy the binary predicate given by the transition,i.e. $\left( {{D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{R}\right) ,{D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{S}\right) }\right)  \in  {\mathcal{B}}_{z,{i}_{S}}\left( {i}_{R}\right)$ ), and therefore,by the definition of the binary predicate of $R\left( {\bar{x}}_{R}\right)$ and $S\left( {\bar{x}}_{S}\right) ,{D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{R}\right) .z =$ ${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{S}\right)  \cdot  z$ .

情形(1)对应于${p}_{R} \in  {C}_{z,{i}_{S}}$。这意味着与${i}_{R}$和${i}_{S}$关联的元组必须满足该转换给出的二元谓词，即$\left( {{D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{R}\right) ,{D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{S}\right) }\right)  \in  {\mathcal{B}}_{z,{i}_{S}}\left( {i}_{R}\right)$），因此根据$R\left( {\bar{x}}_{R}\right)$和$S\left( {\bar{x}}_{S}\right) ,{D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{R}\right) .z =$的二元谓词定义${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{S}\right)  \cdot  z$。

On the other hand,for case $\left( 2\right) ,{p}_{R} \notin  {C}_{z,{i}_{S}}$ . Since there is a node labeled by $z$ in the run tree,the variable must have been completed during the run, meaning there must be a sequence of nodes ${\bar{u}}_{1},\ldots ,{\bar{u}}_{m} \in  \rho$ ,with ${\bar{u}}_{1} = {\bar{u}}_{S},{\bar{u}}_{m} = {\bar{u}}_{R}$ ,such that for each node ${\bar{u}}_{k},\rho \left( {\bar{u}}_{k}\right)  = \left( {{p}_{k},{j}_{k},\left\{  {i}_{k}\right\}  }\right)$ and ${\bar{u}}_{k} = {\operatorname{parent}}_{\rho }\left( {\bar{u}}_{k + 1}\right)$ . This means that ${p}_{k + 1} \in  {C}_{{p}_{k},{i}_{k}}$ and ${p}_{k + 1} \in  {\operatorname{desc}}_{{\tau }_{Q}}\left( {p}_{k}\right)$ for every node ${\bar{u}}_{k}$ , which indicates that if ${p}_{k} \neq  z$ ,then ${p}_{k} \in  {\operatorname{desc}}_{{\tau }_{Q}}\left( z\right)$ and $z$ is a variable of $Q\left( {i}_{k}\right)$ . Following the same steps of (1),it is easy to see that for every ${\bar{u}}_{k},\left( {{D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{k + 1}\right) ,{D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{k}\right) }\right)  \in  {\mathcal{B}}_{{p}_{k},{i}_{k}}\left( {i}_{k + 1}\right)$ and ${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{k}\right) .z = {D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{k + 1}\right) .z$ ,therefore ${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{R}\right) .z = {D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{S}\right) .z$ . Note that we purposefully omitted the case ${i}_{S} = {i}_{R}$ in which the condition holds trivially.

另一方面，对于情形$\left( 2\right) ,{p}_{R} \notin  {C}_{z,{i}_{S}}$。由于运行树中存在标记为$z$的节点，该变量必定已在运行过程中完成，这意味着必须存在节点序列${\bar{u}}_{1},\ldots ,{\bar{u}}_{m} \in  \rho$，其中${\bar{u}}_{1} = {\bar{u}}_{S},{\bar{u}}_{m} = {\bar{u}}_{R}$，使得对于每个节点${\bar{u}}_{k},\rho \left( {\bar{u}}_{k}\right)  = \left( {{p}_{k},{j}_{k},\left\{  {i}_{k}\right\}  }\right)$和${\bar{u}}_{k} = {\operatorname{parent}}_{\rho }\left( {\bar{u}}_{k + 1}\right)$。这表明对于每个节点${\bar{u}}_{k}$都有${p}_{k + 1} \in  {C}_{{p}_{k},{i}_{k}}$和${p}_{k + 1} \in  {\operatorname{desc}}_{{\tau }_{Q}}\left( {p}_{k}\right)$，这意味着若${p}_{k} \neq  z$，则${p}_{k} \in  {\operatorname{desc}}_{{\tau }_{Q}}\left( z\right)$且$z$是$Q\left( {i}_{k}\right)$的变量。遵循(1)的相同步骤可推知，对于每个${\bar{u}}_{k},\left( {{D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{k + 1}\right) ,{D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{k}\right) }\right)  \in  {\mathcal{B}}_{{p}_{k},{i}_{k}}\left( {i}_{k + 1}\right)$和${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{k}\right) .z = {D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{k + 1}\right) .z$都有${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{R}\right) .z = {D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{S}\right) .z$。注意我们刻意省略了${i}_{S} = {i}_{R}$这种条件自然成立的平凡情形。

It is clear that the same arguments as in (1) and (2) are valid for the atom ${i}_{T}$ ,so if the run tree has a node labeled by $z$ ,it holds that ${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{T}\right) .z = {D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{S}\right) .z = {D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{R}\right) .z$ . If there is no such node in the run, we can start from the root of the run and its corresponding transition, where $\rho \left( {\operatorname{root}\left( \rho \right) }\right)  = \left( {r,{j}_{S},\left\{  {i}_{S}\right\}  }\right)$ with $r = \operatorname{root}\left( {\tau }_{Q}\right)$ and $\left( {{C}_{r,{i}_{S}},{U}_{S\left( {\bar{x}}_{S}\right) },{\mathcal{B}}_{r,{i}_{S}},\left\{  {i}_{S}\right\}  ,r}\right)  \in  \Delta$ with $Q\left( {i}_{S}\right)  = S\left( {\bar{x}}_{S}\right)$ . It is easy to see that if there are no nodes labeled by $z$ ,then $z$ must be a variable of $S\left( {\bar{x}}_{S}\right)$ ,so we can use the exact same arguments that we used in (1) and (2) to prove that ${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{R}\right) .z = {D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{S}\right) .z$ and ${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{T}\right) .z = {D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{S}\right) .z$ ,therefore in any case it holds that ${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{T}\right) .z = {D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{R}\right) .z$ and ${h}_{\eta }$ is well-defined and a homomorphism from $Q$ to ${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack$ .

显然，(1)和(2)中的相同论证对原子${i}_{T}$同样适用，因此若运行树中存在标记为$z$的节点，则${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{T}\right) .z = {D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{S}\right) .z = {D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{R}\right) .z$成立。若运行中无此类节点，可从运行的根节点及其对应转换开始，其中$\rho \left( {\operatorname{root}\left( \rho \right) }\right)  = \left( {r,{j}_{S},\left\{  {i}_{S}\right\}  }\right)$对应$r = \operatorname{root}\left( {\tau }_{Q}\right)$，$\left( {{C}_{r,{i}_{S}},{U}_{S\left( {\bar{x}}_{S}\right) },{\mathcal{B}}_{r,{i}_{S}},\left\{  {i}_{S}\right\}  ,r}\right)  \in  \Delta$对应$Q\left( {i}_{S}\right)  = S\left( {\bar{x}}_{S}\right)$。易见若无标记为$z$的节点，则$z$必为$S\left( {\bar{x}}_{S}\right)$的变量，故可直接运用(1)和(2)中的论证证明${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{R}\right) .z = {D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{S}\right) .z$与${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{T}\right) .z = {D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{S}\right) .z$，因此无论如何${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{T}\right) .z = {D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {j}_{R}\right) .z$均成立，且${h}_{\eta }$是良定义的、从$Q$到${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack$的同态映射。

Finally,since we have a t-homomorphism from $Q$ to ${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack$ for every run tree $\rho$ of ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$ ,and ${\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  = \left\{  {\widehat{\eta } \mid  \eta }\right.$ is a t-homomorphism from $\left. {Q\text{to}{D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  }\right\}$ ,then ${\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{P}}_{Q}\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  \subseteq  {\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$ .

最终，由于对于${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$的每个运行树$\rho$都存在从$Q$到${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack$的t-同态，且${\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  = \left\{  {\widehat{\eta } \mid  \eta }\right.$是从$\left. {Q\text{to}{D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  }\right\}$出发的t-同态，故${\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{P}}_{Q}\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  \subseteq  {\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$成立。

- ${\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  \subseteq  {\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{P}}_{Q}\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$ . Let ${\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  = \{ \widehat{\eta } \mid  \eta$ is a t-homomorphism from $Q$ to ${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \}$ be the set of outputs of $Q$ over the stream $\mathcal{S}$ .

- ${\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  \subseteq  {\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{P}}_{Q}\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$。设${\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  = \{ \widehat{\eta } \mid  \eta$为从$Q$到${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \}$的t-同态映射（t-homomorphism），表示$Q$在流$\mathcal{S}$上的输出集合。

Let $\widehat{\eta } \in  \llbracket Q{\rrbracket }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$ be a valuation with its associated t-homomorphism $\eta$ and homomorphism from ${h}_{\eta }$ . We can represent the tuples of $\widehat{\eta }$ as the sequence ${t}_{\eta } = \left\{  \left\{  {{i}_{1},\ldots {i}_{m}}\right\}  \right\}$ ,where for all $k \in  {t}_{\eta }$ , ${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {i}_{k}\right)  = {R}_{k}\left( {\bar{a}}_{k}\right)$ and ${i}_{k} < {i}_{k + 1}$ .

设$\widehat{\eta } \in  \llbracket Q{\rrbracket }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$为带有其关联t-同态映射$\eta$及从${h}_{\eta }$出发的同态映射的赋值。我们可以将$\widehat{\eta }$的元组表示为序列${t}_{\eta } = \left\{  \left\{  {{i}_{1},\ldots {i}_{m}}\right\}  \right\}$，其中对于所有$k \in  {t}_{\eta }$，${D}_{n}\left\lbrack  \mathcal{S}\right\rbrack  \left( {i}_{k}\right)  = {R}_{k}\left( {\bar{a}}_{k}\right)$且${i}_{k} < {i}_{k + 1}$。

Using the valuation and the sequence ${t}_{\eta }$ ,we can build a run tree of ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q},\rho  : t \rightarrow  \left( {Q \times  \mathbb{N} \times  \left( {{2}^{\Omega }\smallsetminus \{ \varnothing \} }\right) }\right)$ . We starting with a single leaf node $\varepsilon$ ,with $\rho \left( \varepsilon \right)  = \left( {{R}_{1}\left( {\bar{x}}_{1}\right) ,{t}_{\eta }\left( 1\right) ,\left\{  {{R}_{1}\left( {\bar{x}}_{1}\right) }\right\}  }\right)$ . After this,for every tuple $k \in  {t}_{\eta }$ ,we must check if there is a transition $\left( {P,U,B,L,q}\right)  \in  \Delta$ such that for each $p \in  P$ ,there is a node $\bar{u} \in  \rho$ with $\rho \left( \bar{u}\right)  = \left( {p,j,L}\right)$ and $\bar{u} \notin  {\operatorname{children}}_{\rho }\left( \bar{v}\right)$ for every $\bar{v} \in  \rho$ ,i.e. we check if there are nodes with no parent that are labeled by each state needed for a transition. If we find a transition that satisfies the previous condition,we add a new node $\bar{v}$ labeled by $\left( {q,{t}_{\eta }\left( k\right) ,L}\right)$ ; if there are no transitions that satisfy the condition,we add a new leaf node labeled by $\left( {{R}_{k}\left( {\bar{x}}_{k}\right) ,{t}_{\eta }\left( k\right) ,\left\{  {{R}_{k}\left( {\bar{x}}_{k}\right) }\right\}  }\right)$ .

利用该赋值与序列${t}_{\eta }$，我们可以构建${\mathcal{P}}_{Q},\rho  : t \rightarrow  \left( {Q \times  \mathbb{N} \times  \left( {{2}^{\Omega }\smallsetminus \{ \varnothing \} }\right) }\right)$的运行树。初始时创建单个叶节点$\varepsilon$，其满足$\rho \left( \varepsilon \right)  = \left( {{R}_{1}\left( {\bar{x}}_{1}\right) ,{t}_{\eta }\left( 1\right) ,\left\{  {{R}_{1}\left( {\bar{x}}_{1}\right) }\right\}  }\right)$。此后，对于每个元组$k \in  {t}_{\eta }$，需检查是否存在转移$\left( {P,U,B,L,q}\right)  \in  \Delta$使得对每个$p \in  P$都存在节点$\bar{u} \in  \rho$满足$\rho \left( \bar{u}\right)  = \left( {p,j,L}\right)$，且对每个$\bar{v} \in  \rho$有$\bar{u} \notin  {\operatorname{children}}_{\rho }\left( \bar{v}\right)$——即验证是否存在无父节点、且被转移所需各状态标记的节点。若找到满足条件的转移，则添加由$\left( {q,{t}_{\eta }\left( k\right) ,L}\right)$标记的新节点$\bar{v}$；若无满足条件的转移，则添加由$\left( {{R}_{k}\left( {\bar{x}}_{k}\right) ,{t}_{\eta }\left( k\right) ,\left\{  {{R}_{k}\left( {\bar{x}}_{k}\right) }\right\}  }\right)$标记的新叶节点。

Since we get each tuple from the values given by the homomorphism ${h}_{\eta }$ ,we know that they will satisfy the unary and binary predicates of each transition, and since there is exactly one tuple for each atom of $Q$ ,we know that there will be a one to one correspondence between the tuples and the labels in the tree $\rho$ ,meaning it is an accepting run and therefore $\llbracket Q{\rrbracket }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  \subseteq  \llbracket {\mathcal{P}}_{Q}{\rrbracket }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$ .

由于我们从同态${h}_{\eta }$给定的值中获取每个元组，因此它们将满足每个转移的一元和二元谓词；又因为对$Q$的每个原子恰好存在一个元组，故元组与树$\rho$中的标签将形成一一对应关系，这意味着这是一个接受运行，因此$\llbracket Q{\rrbracket }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  \subseteq  \llbracket {\mathcal{P}}_{Q}{\rrbracket }_{n}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$。

Connected HCQ with self joins. For a non-empty set $A \subseteq  I\left( Q\right)$ we say that $A$ is a self join (of $Q$ ) iff ${R}_{i} = {R}_{j}$ for every $i,j \in  A$ . That is,if all relations of the identifiers are the same. We denote by ${\mathrm{{SJ}}}_{Q}$ the set of all self joins $A$ of $Q$ . Note that $\mathop{\bigcap }\limits_{{i \in  A}}\left\{  {\bar{x}}_{i}\right\}   \neq  \varnothing$ for every $A \in  {\mathrm{{SJ}}}_{Q}$ since ${\tau }_{Q}\left( \varepsilon \right)  \in  \mathop{\bigcap }\limits_{{i \in  A}}\left\{  {\bar{x}}_{i}\right\}$ (i.e., $Q$ is connected).

带自连接的连通HCQ。对于非空集合$A \subseteq  I\left( Q\right)$，当且仅当对于每个$i,j \in  A$都有${R}_{i} = {R}_{j}$时，我们称$A$是$Q$的自连接。即所有标识符的关系都相同。我们用${\mathrm{{SJ}}}_{Q}$表示$Q$的所有自连接$A$的集合。注意由于${\tau }_{Q}\left( \varepsilon \right)  \in  \mathop{\bigcap }\limits_{{i \in  A}}\left\{  {\bar{x}}_{i}\right\}$（即$Q$是连通的），对每个$A \in  {\mathrm{{SJ}}}_{Q}$都有$\mathop{\bigcap }\limits_{{i \in  A}}\left\{  {\bar{x}}_{i}\right\}   \neq  \varnothing$。

To deal with self joins, we must modify the previous construction by keeping track of the last atom or self join that was read. This modification is necessary to use equality predicates. Furthermore, the construction has an inherently exponential blow-up in the number of transitions, given that we need to annotate tuples with self joins. This blow-up seems unavoidable for the model since,if $Q$ has only atoms with the same relational symbol, then the last transitions is forced to annotate with sets of atoms, which are an exponential number of them.

处理自连接时，必须通过记录最后读取的原子或自连接来修改先前的构造。这种修改对于使用相等谓词是必要的。此外，由于需要为元组标注自连接，该构造在转移数量上存在固有的指数级膨胀。这种膨胀对于模型似乎是不可避免的，因为如果$Q$仅包含具有相同关系符号的原子，则最后的转移被迫用原子集合进行标注，其数量呈指数级增长。

Let ${\mathrm{{xSJ}}}_{Q} = \left\{  {\left( {x,A}\right)  \mid  A \in  {\mathrm{{SJ}}}_{Q} \land  x \in  \mathop{\bigcap }\limits_{{i \in  A}}\left\{  {\bar{x}}_{i}\right\}  }\right\}$ . We define the PCEA:

设${\mathrm{{xSJ}}}_{Q} = \left\{  {\left( {x,A}\right)  \mid  A \in  {\mathrm{{SJ}}}_{Q} \land  x \in  \mathop{\bigcap }\limits_{{i \in  A}}\left\{  {\bar{x}}_{i}\right\}  }\right\}$。我们定义PCEA如下：

$$
{\mathcal{P}}_{Q} = \left( {I\left( Q\right)  \cup  {\mathrm{{xSJ}}}_{Q},{\mathbf{U}}_{\text{lin }},{\mathbf{B}}_{\mathrm{{eq}}},I\left( Q\right) ,\Delta ,\left\{  {{\tau }_{Q}\left( \varepsilon \right) }\right\}   \times  {\mathrm{{SJ}}}_{Q}}\right) .
$$

Note that $\left\{  {{\tau }_{Q}\left( \varepsilon \right) }\right\}   \times  {\mathrm{{SJ}}}_{Q} \subseteq  {\mathrm{{xSJ}}}_{Q}$ . The main change is that the variable states of the automaton are pairs formed by a variable and a self join that brought ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$ to that variable. We dedicate the rest of this subsection to introduce the notation that we need to define $\Delta$ .

注意$\left\{  {{\tau }_{Q}\left( \varepsilon \right) }\right\}   \times  {\mathrm{{SJ}}}_{Q} \subseteq  {\mathrm{{xSJ}}}_{Q}$。主要变化在于自动机的变量状态是由变量和将该变量${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$带入当前状态的自连接组成的二元组。本小节剩余部分将专门介绍定义$\Delta$所需的符号表示法。

To account for a single tuple satisfying a self join of $Q$ ,we need to define a new unary predicate associated with a self join $A \in  {\mathrm{{SJ}}}_{Q}$ . For this,the following lemma will be relevant.

为说明单个元组满足$Q$的自连接，需要定义与自连接$A \in  {\mathrm{{SJ}}}_{Q}$关联的新一元谓词。为此，以下引理具有重要意义。

Lemma B.3. Let $A \in  {\mathrm{{SJ}}}_{Q}$ . There exists an atom ${t}_{A}$ (not necessarily in $Q$ ) such that,for every $t \in$ Tuples $\left\lbrack  \sigma \right\rbrack$ ,the following statements are equivalent: (1) there exists $h \in$ Hom such that $h\left( {{R}_{i}\left( {\bar{x}}_{i}\right) }\right)  = t$ for every $i \in  A$ ; (2) there exists ${h}^{\prime } \in$ Hom such that ${h}^{\prime }\left( {t}_{A}\right)  = t$ .

引理B.3。设$A \in  {\mathrm{{SJ}}}_{Q}$。存在一个原子${t}_{A}$（不一定在$Q$中），使得对于每个$t \in$元组$\left\lbrack  \sigma \right\rbrack$，以下陈述等价：(1) 存在$h \in$同态Hom使得对每个$i \in  A$都有$h\left( {{R}_{i}\left( {\bar{x}}_{i}\right) }\right)  = t$；(2) 存在${h}^{\prime } \in$同态Hom使得${h}^{\prime }\left( {t}_{A}\right)  = t$成立。

Proof. Let $\left\lbrack  {1,n}\right\rbrack   = \{ 1,\ldots ,n\}$ and define the relation $H \subseteq  {\left\lbrack  1,n\right\rbrack  }^{2}$ such that:

证明。设$\left\lbrack  {1,n}\right\rbrack   = \{ 1,\ldots ,n\}$并定义关系$H \subseteq  {\left\lbrack  1,n\right\rbrack  }^{2}$满足：

$$
H = \left\{  \left( {{k}_{1},{k}_{2} \in  {\left\lbrack  1,n\right\rbrack  }^{2}\mid \exists i,j.{\bar{x}}_{i}\left\lbrack  {k}_{1}\right\rbrack   = {\bar{x}}_{j}\left\lbrack  {k}_{2}\right\rbrack  }\right) \right\}  
$$

Note that $H$ is reflexive and symmetric,but not necessarily transitive.

注意$H$具有自反性和对称性，但不一定具有传递性。

Let ${H}^{T}$ be the transitive closure of $H$ . Now ${H}^{T}$ is an equivalence relation. Given $k \in  \left\lbrack  {1,\ldots ,n}\right\rbrack$ ,let $\left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ be the equivalence class of $k$ with respect to ${H}^{T}$ . Define $R\left( \bar{x}\right)  = R\left( {\left\lbrack  1\right\rbrack  ,\ldots ,\left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }\right)$ . Next we prove that the atom ${t}_{A} = R\left( \bar{x}\right)$ satisfies the theorem.

设${H}^{T}$为$H$的传递闭包。此时${H}^{T}$构成等价关系。给定$k \in  \left\lbrack  {1,\ldots ,n}\right\rbrack$，令$\left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$表示$k$关于${H}^{T}$的等价类。定义$R\left( \bar{x}\right)  = R\left( {\left\lbrack  1\right\rbrack  ,\ldots ,\left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }\right)$。接下来我们证明原子${t}_{A} = R\left( \bar{x}\right)$满足该定理。

- $\left( 1\right)  \rightarrow  \left( 2\right)$ . Suppose that $h$ is a homomorphism such that $h\left( {R\left( \bar{x}\right) }\right)  = R\left( \bar{a}\right)$ . Define the homomorphism ${h}^{ * }\left( x\right)  = \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ ,with ${\bar{x}}_{i}\left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack   = x$ for some $\mathrm{i}$ .

- $\left( 1\right)  \rightarrow  \left( 2\right)$。假设$h$是同态映射且满足$h\left( {R\left( \bar{x}\right) }\right)  = R\left( \bar{a}\right)$。定义同态映射${h}^{ * }\left( x\right)  = \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$，其中对某个$\mathrm{i}$有${\bar{x}}_{i}\left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack   = x$。

One can prove that (1) ${h}^{ * }$ is well-defined,i.e.,it does not depend on $k$ and (2) ${h}^{ * }\left( {R\left( {\bar{x}}_{i}\right) }\right)  = R\left( \bar{x}\right)$ for all $i \leq  n$ .

可证明：(1)${h}^{ * }$是良定义的，即不依赖于$k$；(2)对所有$i \leq  n$都有${h}^{ * }\left( {R\left( {\bar{x}}_{i}\right) }\right)  = R\left( \bar{x}\right)$。

Define ${h}^{\prime } = {h}^{ * } \circ  h$ ,then ${h}^{\prime }\left( {R\left( {\bar{x}}_{i}\right) }\right)  = h\left( {{h}^{ * }\left( {R\left( {\bar{x}}_{i}\right) }\right) }\right)  = h\left( {R\left( \bar{x}\right) }\right)  = R\left( \bar{a}\right)$ .

定义${h}^{\prime } = {h}^{ * } \circ  h$，则${h}^{\prime }\left( {R\left( {\bar{x}}_{i}\right) }\right)  = h\left( {{h}^{ * }\left( {R\left( {\bar{x}}_{i}\right) }\right) }\right)  = h\left( {R\left( \bar{x}\right) }\right)  = R\left( \bar{a}\right)$。

- $\left( 2\right)  \rightarrow  \left( 1\right)$ . Suppose that ${h}^{\prime } \in$ hom satisfies ${h}^{\prime }\left( {R\left( {\bar{x}}_{i}\right) }\right)  = R\left( \bar{a}\right)$ for every $i$ .

- $\left( 2\right)  \rightarrow  \left( 1\right)$。假设${h}^{\prime } \in$同态满足：对每个$i$都有${h}^{\prime }\left( {R\left( {\bar{x}}_{i}\right) }\right)  = R\left( \bar{a}\right)$。

Define the homomorphism $h$ such that $h\left( \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  \right)  = {h}^{\prime }\left( {{\bar{x}}_{1}\left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }\right)$ . It is easy to see that $h$ is well-defined, namely if $\left\lbrack  {k}_{1}\right\rbrack   = \left\lbrack  {k}_{2}\right\rbrack$ ,then ${h}^{\prime }\left( {{\bar{x}}_{1}\left\lbrack  {k}_{1}\right\rbrack  }\right)  = {h}^{\prime }\left( {{\bar{x}}_{1}\left\lbrack  {k}_{2}\right\rbrack  }\right)$ .

定义同态映射$h$使得$h\left( \left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  \right)  = {h}^{\prime }\left( {{\bar{x}}_{1}\left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack  }\right)$。易见$h$是良定义的，即若$\left\lbrack  {k}_{1}\right\rbrack   = \left\lbrack  {k}_{2}\right\rbrack$则${h}^{\prime }\left( {{\bar{x}}_{1}\left\lbrack  {k}_{1}\right\rbrack  }\right)  = {h}^{\prime }\left( {{\bar{x}}_{1}\left\lbrack  {k}_{2}\right\rbrack  }\right)$。

Since $h$ is well-defined,then $h$ is a homomorphism and:

由于$h$是良定义的，故$h$构成同态映射且满足：

$$
h\left( {R\left( \bar{x}\right) }\right)  = R\left( {h\left( \left\lbrack  1\right\rbrack  \right) ,\ldots ,h\left( \left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  \right) }\right) 
$$

$$
 = R\left( {{h}^{\prime }\left( {{\bar{x}}_{1}\left\lbrack  1\right\rbrack  }\right) ,\ldots ,{h}^{\prime }\left( {{\bar{x}}_{1}\left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }\right) }\right) 
$$

$$
 = {h}^{\prime }\left( {R\left( {\bar{x}}_{1}\right) }\right) 
$$

$$
 = R\left( \bar{a}\right) 
$$

Note that the homomorphism $h$ implicitly forces that ${t}_{A}$ has the same relational symbol as every ${R}_{i}$ with $i \in  A$ ,otherwise there would be no tuple that satisfies the single homomorphism condition. It also motivates the following unary predicate for a self join $A \in  {\mathrm{{SJ}}}_{Q}$ :

注意该同态映射$h$隐含要求${t}_{A}$与每个满足$i \in  A$的${R}_{i}$具有相同关系符号，否则将不存在满足单一同态条件的元组。这也引出了以下针对自连接$A \in  {\mathrm{{SJ}}}_{Q}$的一元谓词：

$$
{U}_{A} = \left\{  {t \in  \operatorname{Tuples}\left\lbrack  \sigma \right\rbrack  \mid \exists h \in  \operatorname{Hom}.h\left( {t}_{A}\right)  = t}\right\}  .
$$

One can check that ${U}_{A} \in  {\mathbf{U}}_{\text{lin }}$ given that one can compute ${t}_{A}$ from $A$ in advance,and then check that $t$ is homomorphic to ${t}_{A}$ in linear time over $t$ for every $t \in  \operatorname{Tuples}\left\lbrack  \sigma \right\rbrack$ .

若可预先从$A$计算出${t}_{A}$，则能验证${U}_{A} \in  {\mathbf{U}}_{\text{lin }}$，进而对每个$t \in  \operatorname{Tuples}\left\lbrack  \sigma \right\rbrack$在$t$上以线性时间验证$t$与${t}_{A}$同态。

Similar to unary predicates, we can derive a lemma for a pair of self joins.

类似于一元谓词，我们可以推导出一对自连接引理。

Lemma B.4. Let ${A}_{1},{A}_{2} \in  {\mathrm{{SJ}}}_{Q}$ . There exist atoms ${\bar{t}}_{{A}_{1},{A}_{2}}$ and ${\bar{t}}_{{A}_{1},{A}_{2}}$ (not necessarily in $Q$ ) such that, for every pair $\left( {{t}_{1},{t}_{2}}\right)  \in  \operatorname{Tuples}{\left\lbrack  \sigma \right\rbrack  }^{2}$ ,the following statements are equivalent: (1) there exists $h \in  \mathrm{{Hom}}$ such that $h\left( {{R}_{i}\left( {\bar{x}}_{i}\right) }\right)  = {t}_{j}$ for every $j \in  \{ 1,2\}$ and every $i \in  {A}_{j}$ ; (2) there exists ${h}^{\prime } \in$ Hom such that ${h}^{\prime }\left( {\bar{t}}_{{A}_{1},{A}_{2}}\right)  = {t}_{1}$ and ${h}^{\prime }\left( {\overrightarrow{t}}_{{A}_{1},{A}_{2}}\right)  = {t}_{2}.$ Proof. Let ${A}_{1} = {R}_{1}\left( {{\bar{x}}_{1},\ldots ,{R}_{1}\left( {\bar{x}}_{m}\right) }\right) ,{A}_{2} = {R}_{2}\left( {{\bar{y}}_{1},\ldots ,{R}_{2}\left( {\bar{y}}_{{m}^{\prime }}\right) }\right) ,\left\lbrack  {1,n}\right\rbrack   = \{ 1,\ldots ,n\}$ and $\left\lbrack  {{1}^{\prime },{n}^{\prime }}\right\rbrack   = \left\{  {{1}^{\prime },\ldots ,{n}^{\prime }}\right\}$ . Note that $\left\lbrack  {1,n}\right\rbrack   \cap  \left\lbrack  {{1}^{\prime },{n}^{\prime }}\right\rbrack   = \varnothing$ . Define the relation $H \subseteq  {\left( \left\lbrack  1,n\right\rbrack   \cup  \left\lbrack  {1}^{\prime },{n}^{\prime }\right\rbrack  \right) }^{2}$ such that:

引理B.4. 设${A}_{1},{A}_{2} \in  {\mathrm{{SJ}}}_{Q}$。存在原子${\bar{t}}_{{A}_{1},{A}_{2}}$和${\bar{t}}_{{A}_{1},{A}_{2}}$（未必在$Q$中），使得对于每对$\left( {{t}_{1},{t}_{2}}\right)  \in  \operatorname{Tuples}{\left\lbrack  \sigma \right\rbrack  }^{2}$，下列陈述等价：(1) 存在$h \in  \mathrm{{Hom}}$使得对每个$j \in  \{ 1,2\}$和每个$i \in  {A}_{j}$都有$h\left( {{R}_{i}\left( {\bar{x}}_{i}\right) }\right)  = {t}_{j}$；(2) 存在Hom中的${h}^{\prime } \in$使得${h}^{\prime }\left( {\bar{t}}_{{A}_{1},{A}_{2}}\right)  = {t}_{1}$且${h}^{\prime }\left( {\overrightarrow{t}}_{{A}_{1},{A}_{2}}\right)  = {t}_{2}.$。证明：设${A}_{1} = {R}_{1}\left( {{\bar{x}}_{1},\ldots ,{R}_{1}\left( {\bar{x}}_{m}\right) }\right) ,{A}_{2} = {R}_{2}\left( {{\bar{y}}_{1},\ldots ,{R}_{2}\left( {\bar{y}}_{{m}^{\prime }}\right) }\right) ,\left\lbrack  {1,n}\right\rbrack   = \{ 1,\ldots ,n\}$和$\left\lbrack  {{1}^{\prime },{n}^{\prime }}\right\rbrack   = \left\{  {{1}^{\prime },\ldots ,{n}^{\prime }}\right\}$。注意到$\left\lbrack  {1,n}\right\rbrack   \cap  \left\lbrack  {{1}^{\prime },{n}^{\prime }}\right\rbrack   = \varnothing$。定义关系$H \subseteq  {\left( \left\lbrack  1,n\right\rbrack   \cup  \left\lbrack  {1}^{\prime },{n}^{\prime }\right\rbrack  \right) }^{2}$满足：

$$
H = \left\{  \left( {{k}_{1},{k}_{2}\mid \exists i,j.{\bar{x}}_{i}\left\lbrack  {k}_{1}\right\rbrack   = {\bar{x}}_{j}\left\lbrack  {k}_{2}\right\rbrack  }\right) \right\}  
$$

$$
 = \left\{  \left( {{k}_{1}^{\prime },{k}_{2}^{\prime }\mid \exists i,j.{\bar{y}}_{i}\left\lbrack  {k}_{1}\right\rbrack   = {\bar{y}}_{j}\left\lbrack  {k}_{2}\right\rbrack  }\right) \right\}  
$$

$$
 = \left\{  \left( {{k}_{1},{k}_{2}^{\prime }\mid \exists i,j.{\bar{x}}_{i}\left\lbrack  {k}_{1}\right\rbrack   = {\bar{y}}_{j}\left\lbrack  {k}_{2}\right\rbrack  }\right) \right\}  
$$

$$
 = \left\{  \left( {{k}_{1}^{\prime },{k}_{2}\mid \exists i,j.{\bar{y}}_{i}\left\lbrack  {k}_{1}\right\rbrack   = {\bar{x}}_{j}\left\lbrack  {k}_{2}\right\rbrack  }\right) \right\}  
$$

Just like for Lemma B.3,take ${H}^{T}$ the transitive closure of $H$ ,with ${H}^{T}$ an equivalence relation. Given $k \in  \left\lbrack  {1,\ldots ,n}\right\rbrack   \cup  \left\lbrack  {{1}^{\prime },{n}^{\prime }}\right\rbrack$ ,let $\left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$ be the equivalence class of $k$ in ${H}^{T}$ . Define ${R}_{1}\left( \bar{x}\right)  = {R}_{1}\left( {\left\lbrack  1\right\rbrack  ,\ldots ,\left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }\right)$ and ${R}_{2}\left( \bar{y}\right)  = {R}_{2}\left( {\left\lbrack  {1}^{\prime }\right\rbrack  ,\ldots ,\left\lbrack  {n}^{\prime }\right\rbrack  }\right)$ . The proof that ${\widetilde{t}}_{{A}_{1},{A}_{2}}$ and ${\widetilde{t}}_{{A}_{1},{A}_{2}}$ is completely analogous to the one in Lemma B.3

如同引理B.3的情形，取${H}^{T}$为$H$的传递闭包，其中${H}^{T}$构成等价关系。给定$k \in  \left\lbrack  {1,\ldots ,n}\right\rbrack   \cup  \left\lbrack  {{1}^{\prime },{n}^{\prime }}\right\rbrack$，令$\left\lbrack  k\right\rbrack$表示$k$在${H}^{T}$中的等价类。定义${R}_{1}\left( \bar{x}\right)  = {R}_{1}\left( {\left\lbrack  1\right\rbrack  ,\ldots ,\left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }\right)$和${R}_{2}\left( \bar{y}\right)  = {R}_{2}\left( {\left\lbrack  {1}^{\prime }\right\rbrack  ,\ldots ,\left\lbrack  {n}^{\prime }\right\rbrack  }\right)$。关于${\widetilde{t}}_{{A}_{1},{A}_{2}}$及${\widetilde{t}}_{{A}_{1},{A}_{2}}$的证明过程与引理B.3完全类似

Analog to the unary predicates, this lemma motivates the following binary predicate for every pair ${A}_{1},{A}_{2} \in  {\mathrm{{SJ}}}_{Q}$ :

与一元谓词相仿，该引理为每一对${A}_{1},{A}_{2} \in  {\mathrm{{SJ}}}_{Q}$推导出如下二元谓词：

$$
{B}_{{A}_{1},{A}_{2}} = \left\{  {\left( {{t}_{1},{t}_{2}}\right)  \in  \operatorname{Tuples}{\left\lbrack  \sigma \right\rbrack  }^{2}\mid \exists h \in  \operatorname{Hom}.h\left( {\bar{t}}_{{A}_{1},{A}_{2}}\right)  = {t}_{1} \land  h\left( {\bar{t}}_{{A}_{1},{A}_{2}}\right)  = {t}_{2}}\right\}  .
$$

Note that ${B}_{{A}_{1},{A}_{2}} \in  {\mathbf{B}}_{\mathrm{{eq}}}$ . Indeed, ${B}_{{A}_{1},{A}_{2}} = {B}_{S\left( \bar{y}\right) ,T\left( \bar{z}\right) }$ with $S\left( \bar{y}\right)  = {\bar{t}}_{{A}_{1},{A}_{2}}$ and $T\left( \bar{z}\right)  = {\bar{t}}_{{A}_{1},{A}_{2}}$ .

注意${B}_{{A}_{1},{A}_{2}} \in  {\mathbf{B}}_{\mathrm{{eq}}}$。实际上，${B}_{{A}_{1},{A}_{2}} = {B}_{S\left( \bar{y}\right) ,T\left( \bar{z}\right) }$成立，其中$S\left( \bar{y}\right)  = {\bar{t}}_{{A}_{1},{A}_{2}}$且$T\left( \bar{z}\right)  = {\bar{t}}_{{A}_{1},{A}_{2}}$。

Now,we move to the definitions for constructing the transition relation $\Delta$ . Let $A \in  {\mathrm{{SJ}}}_{Q}$ . For a variable $x \in  \mathop{\bigcap }\limits_{{i \in  A}}\left\{  {\bar{x}}_{i}\right\}$ we define the set of incomplete states of $x$ given $A$ as:

接下来转向构建转移关系$\Delta$的定义。设$A \in  {\mathrm{{SJ}}}_{Q}$。对于变量$x \in  \mathop{\bigcap }\limits_{{i \in  A}}\left\{  {\bar{x}}_{i}\right\}$，我们定义给定$A$时$x$的不完全状态集合为：

$$
{C}_{x,A} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\{  {\ell  \in  I\left( Q\right)  \cup  \{ \bar{x}\}  \mid  {\operatorname{parent}}_{{\tau }_{Q}}\left( \ell \right)  \in  \left( {{\operatorname{desc}}_{{\tau }_{Q}}\left( x\right)  \cap  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i \in  A}}\left\{  {\bar{x}}_{i}\right\}  }\right) }\right\}   \smallsetminus  \left( {A \cup  \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i \in  A}}\left\{  {\bar{x}}_{i}\right\}  }\right) .
$$

${C}_{x,A}$ is the generalization of the set ${C}_{x,i}$ . Indeed,one can check that ${C}_{x,i} = {C}_{x,\{ i\} }$ . Then,the intuition behind ${C}_{x,A}$ is similar: in ${C}_{x,A}$ we are collecting all variables or atoms identifiers in ${\tau }_{Q}$ that directly hangs from a variable that it is a descendant of $x$ and it is in an atom of $A$ ,except for the same variables and atoms in $A$ .

${C}_{x,A}$是集合${C}_{x,i}$的泛化形式。事实上可验证${C}_{x,i} = {C}_{x,\{ i\} }$。因此${C}_{x,A}$的核心思想与之相似：在${C}_{x,A}$中我们汇集${\tau }_{Q}$内所有直接悬挂于$x$后代变量且位于$A$原子中的变量或原子标识符，但排除$A$中相同的变量和原子。

Let ${C}_{x,A}$ be the incomplete states of $x$ given $A$ . We say that a subset of states $C \subseteq  I\left( Q\right)  \cup  {\mathrm{{xSJ}}}_{Q}$ encodes ${C}_{x,A}$ in ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$ iff $C \cap  I\left( Q\right)  = {C}_{x,A} \cap  I\left( Q\right)$ and for every variable $y \in  {C}_{x,A}$ there exists a unique pair $\left( {y,{A}^{\prime }}\right)  \in  C$ . Intuitively, $C$ will be the analog of ${C}_{x,i}$ that we use as a set of states in the case without self joins. Note that ${C}_{x,A}$ can be empty,and then $\varnothing$ is the only set representing ${C}_{x,A}$ . We denote by ${\bar{C}}_{x,A}$ the set of all encodings of ${C}_{x,A}$ in ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$ .

设${C}_{x,A}$为给定$A$时$x$的不完全状态集。当且仅当$C \cap  I\left( Q\right)  = {C}_{x,A} \cap  I\left( Q\right)$且对于每个变量$y \in  {C}_{x,A}$存在唯一配对$\left( {y,{A}^{\prime }}\right)  \in  C$时，我们称状态子集$C \subseteq  I\left( Q\right)  \cup  {\mathrm{{xSJ}}}_{Q}$在${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$中对${C}_{x,A}$进行编码。直观而言，$C$将作为${C}_{x,i}$的类比物，用于无自连接情况下的状态集合。注意${C}_{x,A}$可为空集，此时$\varnothing$是唯一表示${C}_{x,A}$的集合。我们用${\bar{C}}_{x,A}$表示${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$中所有${C}_{x,A}$编码的集合。

With this machinery,we construct the new transition relation $\Delta$ as follows:

基于此机制，我们按如下方式构建新的转移关系$\Delta$：

$$
\Delta  = \left\{  {\left( {\varnothing ,{U}_{{R}_{i}\left( {\bar{x}}_{i}\right) },\varnothing ,\{ i\} ,i}\right)  \mid  i \in  I\left( Q\right) }\right\}  
$$

$$
 \cup  \left\{  {\left( {C,{U}_{A},{\mathcal{B}}_{C,A},A,\left( {x,A}\right) }\right)  \mid  A \in  {\mathrm{{SJ}}}_{Q} \land  x \in  \mathop{\bigcap }\limits_{{i \in  A}}\left\{  {\bar{x}}_{i}\right\}   \land  C \in  {\bar{C}}_{x,A}}\right\}  
$$

such that ${\mathcal{B}}_{C,A} : C \rightarrow  {\mathbf{B}}_{\mathrm{{eq}}}$ is the predicate function associated with $C$ and $A$ defined as follows: ${\mathcal{B}}_{C,A}\left( j\right)  =$ ${B}_{\{ j\} ,A}$ for every identifier $j \in  C$ ,and ${F}_{x,C}\left( \left( {y,{A}^{\prime }}\right) \right)  = {B}_{{A}^{\prime },A}$ for every $\left( {y,{A}^{\prime }}\right)  \in  C$ . Note that all the binary predicates of ${F}_{C,A}$ are equality predicates as defined above.

其中${\mathcal{B}}_{C,A} : C \rightarrow  {\mathbf{B}}_{\mathrm{{eq}}}$是与$C$及$A$关联的谓词函数，定义如下：对每个标识符$j \in  C$有${\mathcal{B}}_{C,A}\left( j\right)  =$${B}_{\{ j\} ,A}$，对每个$\left( {y,{A}^{\prime }}\right)  \in  C$有${F}_{x,C}\left( \left( {y,{A}^{\prime }}\right) \right)  = {B}_{{A}^{\prime },A}$。注意${F}_{C,A}$的所有二元谓词均为前述定义的等值谓词。

This ends the definition of $\Delta$ and ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$ for a connected HCQ $Q$ with self joins. Next,we prove that ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$ is unambiguous and ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q} \equiv  Q$ .

至此完成了带自连接的连通HCQ$Q$的$\Delta$与${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$定义。接下来我们将证明${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$具有明确性且${\mathcal{P}}_{Q} \equiv  Q$。

Thanks to Lemma B. 3 and Lemma B. 4, this proof is analogous the the one without self joins.

根据引理B.3和引理B.4，该证明与无自连接情况类似。

The general case. We end this proof by showing the case when $Q$ is disconnected. If this is the case, let ${x}^{ * }$ be a fresh variable and redefine $Q$ as:

一般情况。通过展示$Q$非连通的情形来结束证明。若为此情况，设${x}^{ * }$为新变量，并将$Q$重新定义为：

$$
{Q}^{ * }\left( {{x}^{ * },\bar{x}}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{0}\left( {{x}^{ * },{\bar{x}}_{0}}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{m - 1}\left( {{x}^{ * },{\bar{x}}_{m - 1}}\right) 
$$

where ${Q}^{ * }$ is defined over a new schema ${\sigma }^{ * }$ where the arity of each relational symbol is increased by one. ${Q}^{ * }$ is hierarchical and now is connected. By the previous case,we can construct a PCEA ${\mathcal{P}}_{{Q}^{ * }}$ such that ${\mathcal{P}}_{{Q}^{ * }} \equiv  {Q}^{ * }$ . Now,take ${\mathcal{P}}_{{Q}^{ * }}$ and define ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$ by removing the fresh variable ${x}^{ * }$ from ${\mathcal{P}}_{{Q}^{ * }}$ ,namely,remove it from the unary and binary predicates in the transitions. One can easily check that ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q} \equiv  Q$ .

其中${Q}^{ * }$定义于新模式${\sigma }^{ * }$上，各关系符号的元数增加一。${Q}^{ * }$现在是分层的且连通的。根据前例可构造PCEA${\mathcal{P}}_{{Q}^{ * }}$使得${\mathcal{P}}_{{Q}^{ * }} \equiv  {Q}^{ * }$。现取${\mathcal{P}}_{{Q}^{ * }}$并通过从转移关系的一元及二元谓词中移除新变量${x}^{ * }$来定义${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$。易验证${\mathcal{P}}_{Q} \equiv  Q$。

## Proof of Theorem 4.2

## 定理4.2证明

Fix a schema $\sigma$ ,a set of data values $\mathbf{D} = \mathbb{N}$ and an acyclic $\mathrm{{CQ}}Q$ over $\sigma$ of the form:

给定模式$\sigma$、数据值集合$\mathbf{D} = \mathbb{N}$及形如下的$\sigma$上非循环$\mathrm{{CQ}}Q$：

$$
Q\left( \bar{x}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{0}\left( {\bar{x}}_{0}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{m - 1}\left( {\bar{x}}_{m - 1}\right) 
$$

that is not a HCQ,meaning there is a pair of variables $y,z \in  \mathbf{X}$ with atoms $\left( y\right)  \notin$ atoms(z),atoms $\left( z\right)  \notin$ atoms(y)and atoms $\left( y\right)  \cap$ atoms $\left( z\right)  \neq  \varnothing$ . Without loss of generality,assume that $y \in  \left\{  {\bar{x}}_{0}\right\}   \cap  \left\{  {\bar{x}}_{1}\right\}$ , $y \notin  \left\{  \overline{{x}_{2}}\right\}$ and $z \in  \left\{  \overline{{x}_{0}}\right\}   \cap  \left\{  \overline{{x}_{2}}\right\}$ and $z \notin  \left\{  \overline{{x}_{1}}\right\}$ ; moreover, ${R}_{0}\left( {{x}_{0}^{0},\ldots {x}_{0}^{{n}_{0}}}\right) ,{R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1}^{0},\ldots {x}_{1}^{{n}_{1}}}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2}^{0},\ldots {x}_{2}^{{n}_{2}}}\right)$ with ${x}_{0}^{0} = {x}_{1}^{0} = y$ and ${x}_{0}^{1} = {x}_{2}^{0} = z.$

这不是一个HCQ（Hard Constraint Query，硬约束查询），意味着存在一对变量$y,z \in  \mathbf{X}$，其原子$\left( y\right)  \notin$为atoms(z)，原子$\left( z\right)  \notin$为atoms(y)，且原子$\left( y\right)  \cap$为atoms$\left( z\right)  \neq  \varnothing$。不失一般性，假设$y \in  \left\{  {\bar{x}}_{0}\right\}   \cap  \left\{  {\bar{x}}_{1}\right\}$、$y \notin  \left\{  \overline{{x}_{2}}\right\}$、$z \in  \left\{  \overline{{x}_{0}}\right\}   \cap  \left\{  \overline{{x}_{2}}\right\}$和$z \notin  \left\{  \overline{{x}_{1}}\right\}$；此外，${R}_{0}\left( {{x}_{0}^{0},\ldots {x}_{0}^{{n}_{0}}}\right) ,{R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1}^{0},\ldots {x}_{1}^{{n}_{1}}}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2}^{0},\ldots {x}_{2}^{{n}_{2}}}\right)$与${x}_{0}^{0} = {x}_{1}^{0} = y$及${x}_{0}^{1} = {x}_{2}^{0} = z.$相关联

Let ${\mathcal{S}}_{k} = \left\{  \left\{  {{R}_{0}\left( {\bar{a}}_{0}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{m - 1}\left( {\bar{a}}_{m - 1}\right) ,\ldots }\right\}  \right\}$ be a family of streams where for every tuple ${R}_{i}\left( {{a}_{i}^{0},\ldots {a}_{i}^{{n}_{i}}}\right)$ , ${a}_{i}^{j} = k$ ,with $k \in  \mathbb{N}$ if ${x}_{i}^{j} = y$ or ${x}_{i}^{j} = z$ and ${a}_{i}^{j} = 0$ if ${x}_{i}^{j} \neq  y$ and ${x}_{i}^{j} \neq  z$ . In other words,every variable of every tuple is mapped to 0,except for $y$ and $z$ ,which are mapped to $k$ . Recalling our previous definition for $\mathrm{{CQ}}$ over streams,i.e. considering $\Omega  = I\left( Q\right)$ ,it is clear that the valuation $\nu  = \{ \{ 0,1,2,\ldots ,m - 1\} \}  \in  {\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{k}\right)$ for every $k \in  \mathbb{N}$ .

设${\mathcal{S}}_{k} = \left\{  \left\{  {{R}_{0}\left( {\bar{a}}_{0}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{m - 1}\left( {\bar{a}}_{m - 1}\right) ,\ldots }\right\}  \right\}$为一个流族，其中对于每个元组${R}_{i}\left( {{a}_{i}^{0},\ldots {a}_{i}^{{n}_{i}}}\right)$、${a}_{i}^{j} = k$，当${x}_{i}^{j} = y$或${x}_{i}^{j} = z$时满足$k \in  \mathbb{N}$，而当${x}_{i}^{j} \neq  y$且${x}_{i}^{j} \neq  z$时满足${a}_{i}^{j} = 0$。换言之，每个元组的每个变量均映射为0，除了$y$和$z$被映射为$k$。回顾我们先前对流上$\mathrm{{CQ}}$的定义（即考虑$\Omega  = I\left( Q\right)$），显然对于每个$k \in  \mathbb{N}$的赋值$\nu  = \{ \{ 0,1,2,\ldots ,m - 1\} \}  \in  {\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{k}\right)$成立

Assume there is a Parallelized-CEA ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q} = \left( {{\mathcal{P}}_{Q},\mathbf{U},\mathbf{B},\Omega ,\Delta ,F}\right)$ such that ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q} \equiv  Q$ ,meaning that for every stream and,in particular,for every stream ${\mathcal{S}}_{k}$ it holds that $\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{P}}_{Q}\right\rbrack   = \left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$ ,which means $\nu  \in  {\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{P}}_{Q}\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{k}\right)$ for every $k \in  \mathbb{N}$ . Since ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$ has a finite number of states,there must exist $j,k \in  \mathbb{N}$ such that their accepting runs for $\nu ,{\rho }_{j}$ and ${\rho }_{k}$ respectively,are isomorphic,meaning their runs go through the exact same states. For the sake of simplification,we assume that $\operatorname{dom}\left( {\rho }_{j}\right)  = \operatorname{dom}\left( {\rho }_{k}\right)$ .

假设存在一个并行化-CEA（Parallelized-CEA）${\mathcal{P}}_{Q} = \left( {{\mathcal{P}}_{Q},\mathbf{U},\mathbf{B},\Omega ,\Delta ,F}\right)$使得${\mathcal{P}}_{Q} \equiv  Q$成立，这意味着对于每个数据流（stream），特别是对于每个数据流${\mathcal{S}}_{k}$，都有$\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{P}}_{Q}\right\rbrack   = \left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack$成立，即对于每个$k \in  \mathbb{N}$都有$\nu  \in  {\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{P}}_{Q}\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{k}\right)$。由于${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$具有有限状态数，必然存在$j,k \in  \mathbb{N}$使得它们分别对$\nu ,{\rho }_{j}$和${\rho }_{k}$的接受运行（accepting runs）是同构的，即它们的运行轨迹经过完全相同的状态。为简化起见，我们假设$\operatorname{dom}\left( {\rho }_{j}\right)  = \operatorname{dom}\left( {\rho }_{k}\right)$。

Let ${\mathcal{S}}_{j \leftarrow  k} = \left\{  \left\{  {{R}_{0}\left( {\bar{a}}_{0}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{m - 1}\left( {\bar{a}}_{m - 1}\right) ,\ldots }\right\}  \right\}$ where for every tuple ${R}_{i}\left( {{a}_{i}^{0},\ldots {a}_{i}^{{n}_{i}}}\right) ,{a}_{i}^{\ell } = j$ if ${x}_{i}^{\ell } = y,{a}_{i}^{\ell } = k$ if ${x}_{i}^{\ell } = z$ and ${a}_{i}^{\ell } = 0$ if ${x}_{i}^{\ell } \neq  y$ and ${x}_{i}^{\ell } \neq  z$ . It is clear that this time, $\nu  = \{ \{ 0,1,2,\ldots ,m - 1\} \}  \notin  {\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{j \leftarrow  k}\right)$ .

设${\mathcal{S}}_{j \leftarrow  k} = \left\{  \left\{  {{R}_{0}\left( {\bar{a}}_{0}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{m - 1}\left( {\bar{a}}_{m - 1}\right) ,\ldots }\right\}  \right\}$，其中对于每个元组${R}_{i}\left( {{a}_{i}^{0},\ldots {a}_{i}^{{n}_{i}}}\right) ,{a}_{i}^{\ell } = j$，若${x}_{i}^{\ell } = y,{a}_{i}^{\ell } = k$则当${x}_{i}^{\ell } = z$且${a}_{i}^{\ell } = 0$时，若${x}_{i}^{\ell } \neq  y$且${x}_{i}^{\ell } \neq  z$。显然此时$\nu  = \{ \{ 0,1,2,\ldots ,m - 1\} \}  \notin  {\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{j \leftarrow  k}\right)$成立。

Since ${\rho }_{j}$ and ${\rho }_{k}$ are runs associated with $\nu$ ,there are nodes ${\bar{u}}_{0},{\bar{u}}_{1},{\bar{u}}_{2} \in  I\left( {\rho }_{j}\right)$ such that ${\rho }_{j}\left( {\bar{u}}_{i}\right)  =$ $\left( {{p}_{i},i,{L}_{i}}\right)$ and ${\rho }_{k}\left( {\bar{u}}_{i}\right)  = \left( {{p}_{i},i,{L}_{i}}\right)$ ,with ${R}_{i}\left( {\bar{x}}_{i}\right)  \in  {L}_{i}$ for each $i \in  \{ 0,1,2\}$ . Since this nodes exist in ${\rho }_{j}$ and ${\rho }_{k}$ ,for every $i \in  \{ 0,1,2\}$ there must transitions $\left( {{P}_{i},{U}_{i},{B}_{i},{L}_{i},{p}_{i}}\right)$ and $\left( {{P}_{i},{U}_{i}^{\prime },{B}_{i}^{\prime },{L}_{i},{p}_{i}}\right)$ such that ${\mathcal{S}}_{j}\left( i\right)$ satisfies the unary and binary predicates ${U}_{i},{B}_{i}$ and ${\mathcal{S}}_{k}\left( i\right)$ satisfies the unary and binary predicates ${U}_{i}^{\prime },{B}_{i}^{\prime }$ .

由于${\rho }_{j}$和${\rho }_{k}$是与$\nu$关联的运行，存在节点${\bar{u}}_{0},{\bar{u}}_{1},{\bar{u}}_{2} \in  I\left( {\rho }_{j}\right)$使得${\rho }_{j}\left( {\bar{u}}_{i}\right)  =$$\left( {{p}_{i},i,{L}_{i}}\right)$和${\rho }_{k}\left( {\bar{u}}_{i}\right)  = \left( {{p}_{i},i,{L}_{i}}\right)$，且对于每个$i \in  \{ 0,1,2\}$都有${R}_{i}\left( {\bar{x}}_{i}\right)  \in  {L}_{i}$。由于这些节点存在于${\rho }_{j}$和${\rho }_{k}$中，对于每个$i \in  \{ 0,1,2\}$必然存在转移$\left( {{P}_{i},{U}_{i},{B}_{i},{L}_{i},{p}_{i}}\right)$和$\left( {{P}_{i},{U}_{i}^{\prime },{B}_{i}^{\prime },{L}_{i},{p}_{i}}\right)$，使得${\mathcal{S}}_{j}\left( i\right)$满足一元和二元谓词${U}_{i},{B}_{i}$，且${\mathcal{S}}_{k}\left( i\right)$满足一元和二元谓词${U}_{i}^{\prime },{B}_{i}^{\prime }$。

Using these transitions,we can define ${\rho }_{j \leftarrow  k} : I\left( {\rho }_{j}\right)  \rightarrow  \left( {{\mathcal{P}}_{Q},\mathbb{N},\left( {{2}^{\Omega } \smallsetminus  \varnothing }\right) }\right)$ with ${\rho }_{j \leftarrow  k}\left( \bar{u}\right)  = {\rho }_{j}\left( \bar{u}\right)$ for every $\bar{u} \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( {\rho }_{j \leftarrow  k}\right)$ . One can easily check that ${\rho }_{j \leftarrow  k}$ is an accepting run tree of ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$ over ${\mathcal{S}}_{j \leftarrow  k}$ ,since it can follow $\left( {{P}_{i},{U}_{i}^{\prime },{B}_{i}^{\prime },{L}_{i},{p}_{i}}\right)$ for ${R}_{0}\left( {\bar{a}}_{0}\right) ,{R}_{1}\left( {\bar{a}}_{1}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {\bar{a}}_{2}\right)$ and follow the exact same transitions as ${\rho }_{j}$ for every other tuple.

利用这些转移，我们可以为每个$\bar{u} \in  \operatorname{dom}\left( {\rho }_{j \leftarrow  k}\right)$定义具有${\rho }_{j \leftarrow  k}\left( \bar{u}\right)  = {\rho }_{j}\left( \bar{u}\right)$的${\rho }_{j \leftarrow  k} : I\left( {\rho }_{j}\right)  \rightarrow  \left( {{\mathcal{P}}_{Q},\mathbb{N},\left( {{2}^{\Omega } \smallsetminus  \varnothing }\right) }\right)$。可以轻易验证${\rho }_{j \leftarrow  k}$是${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$在${\mathcal{S}}_{j \leftarrow  k}$上的接受运行树，因为它能针对${R}_{0}\left( {\bar{a}}_{0}\right) ,{R}_{1}\left( {\bar{a}}_{1}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {\bar{a}}_{2}\right)$遵循$\left( {{P}_{i},{U}_{i}^{\prime },{B}_{i}^{\prime },{L}_{i},{p}_{i}}\right)$，并对所有其他元组遵循与${\rho }_{j}$完全相同的转移。

Since for the valuation $\nu  = \{ \{ 0,1,2,\ldots ,m - 1\} \}$ it holds that $\nu  \notin  {\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{j \leftarrow  k}\right)$ and $\nu  \in  {\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{P}}_{Q}\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{j \leftarrow  k}\right)$ , then ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q} ≢ Q$ and therefore if $Q$ is and acyclic CQ that is not hierarchical,then ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q} ≢ Q$ for all PFA ${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$ .

由于对于赋值$\nu  = \{ \{ 0,1,2,\ldots ,m - 1\} \}$，$\nu  \notin  {\left\lbrack  Q\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{j \leftarrow  k}\right)$和$\nu  \in  {\left\lbrack  {\mathcal{P}}_{Q}\right\rbrack  }_{n}\left( {\mathcal{S}}_{j \leftarrow  k}\right)$成立，则${\mathcal{P}}_{Q} ≢ Q$，因此如果$Q$是一个非层次化的无环CQ（acyclic CQ），那么对于所有PFA（Probabilistic Finite Automaton）${\mathcal{P}}_{Q}$都有${\mathcal{P}}_{Q} ≢ Q$。

## C Proofs of Section 5

## 附录C 第5节证明

## Proof of Theorem 5.2

## 定理5.2证明

Proof. Let $w \in  \mathbb{N}$ be a window size, ${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$ be a simple data structure and $\mathrm{n} \in  \operatorname{Nodes}\left( {\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}\right)$ be a node of the data structure. The valuations in ${\left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}$ are defined as:

证明。设$w \in  \mathbb{N}$为窗口大小，${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$为简单数据结构，$\mathrm{n} \in  \operatorname{Nodes}\left( {\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}\right)$为该数据结构的节点。${\left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}$中的赋值定义为：

$$
{\left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \{ \{ \nu  \in  \left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack  \left| \right| i - \min \left( \nu \right)  \mid   \leq  w\} \} .
$$

with

其中

$$
{\left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }_{\text{prod }} \mathrel{\text{:=}} \left\{  \left\{  {\nu }_{L\left( n\right) ,i\left( n\right) }\right\}  \right\}   \oplus  \mathop{\bigoplus }\limits_{{{n}^{\prime } \in  \operatorname{prod}\left( n\right) }}\left\lbrack  {n}^{\prime }\right\rbrack  \;\left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack   \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }_{\text{prod }} \cup  \left\lbrack  {\operatorname{uleft}\left( n\right) }\right\rbrack   \cup  \left\lbrack  {\operatorname{uright}\left( n\right) }\right\rbrack  .
$$

Following the definitions used in [22],we will say that the algorithm enumerates the results $\nu  \in  \llbracket \mathrm{n}\rrbracket {}_{i}^{w}$ by writing $\# {\nu }_{1}\# {\nu }_{2}\# \ldots \# {\nu }_{m}\#$ to the output registers,where $\#  \notin  \Omega$ is a separator symbol. Let time(i) be the time in the enumeration when the algorithm writes the $i$ -th symbol $\#$ ,we define the delay $\left( i\right)  =$ $\operatorname{time}\left( {i + 1}\right)  - \operatorname{time}\left( i\right)$ for each $i \leq  m$ . We say that the enumeration has output-linear delay if there is a constant $k$ such that for every $i \leq  m$ it holds that delay $\left( i\right)  \leq  k \cdot  \left| {\nu }_{i}\right|$ .

根据[22]中采用的定义，当算法将$\# {\nu }_{1}\# {\nu }_{2}\# \ldots \# {\nu }_{m}\#$写入输出寄存器时（其中$\#  \notin  \Omega$为分隔符），我们称该算法枚举了结果$\nu  \in  \llbracket \mathrm{n}\rrbracket {}_{i}^{w}$。设time(i)表示算法写入第$i$个符号$\#$时的枚举时间，我们对每个$i \leq  m$定义延迟$\left( i\right)  =$$\operatorname{time}\left( {i + 1}\right)  - \operatorname{time}\left( i\right)$。若存在常数$k$使得对于所有$i \leq  m$都满足delay$\left( i\right)  \leq  k \cdot  \left| {\nu }_{i}\right|$，则称该枚举具有输出线性延迟。

To output the first valuation of ${\left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}$ we need to (1) determine if ${\left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w} = \varnothing$ and (2) build the valuation by calculating the products in $\left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack$ . We can know that ${\left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w} \neq  \varnothing$ iff $\left| {i - \operatorname{max-start}\left( \mathrm{n}\right) }\right|  \leq  w$ ,and since the value of $\max  - \operatorname{start}\left( \mathrm{n}\right)  = \max \left\{  {\min \left( \nu \right)  \mid  \nu  \in  {\left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack  }_{\text{prod }}}\right\}$ is stored in every node $\mathrm{n}$ and we are doing a simple calculation with constants, we can check (1) in constant time. Note that it is not necessary to recursively check the max-start of the rest of the nodes in ${\left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }_{\text{prod }}$ since they are considered in the definition.

要输出${\left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}$的首个赋值，需要：(1)判断${\left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w} = \varnothing$是否成立；(2)通过计算$\left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack$中的乘积构建赋值。已知当且仅当$\left| {i - \operatorname{max-start}\left( \mathrm{n}\right) }\right|  \leq  w$时${\left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w} \neq  \varnothing$成立，且由于$\max  - \operatorname{start}\left( \mathrm{n}\right)  = \max \left\{  {\min \left( \nu \right)  \mid  \nu  \in  {\left\lbrack  \mathrm{n}\right\rbrack  }_{\text{prod }}}\right\}$的值存储在每个节点$\mathrm{n}$中，我们只需进行常量值的简单计算，故可在常数时间内完成(1)的校验。注意无需递归检查${\left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }_{\text{prod }}$中其余节点的max-start，因其已在定义中予以考虑。

On the other hand,the product of two bags of valuations $V,{V}^{\prime }$ is defined as the bag $V \oplus  {V}^{\prime } =$ $\left\{  \left\{  {\nu  \oplus  {\nu }^{\prime } \mid  \nu  \in  V,{\nu }^{\prime } \in  {V}^{\prime }}\right\}  \right\}$ ,where $\nu  \oplus  {\nu }^{\prime }$ is the product of two valuations,defined as a valuation such that $\left\lbrack  {\nu  \oplus  {\nu }^{\prime }}\right\rbrack  \left( \ell \right)  = \nu \left( \ell \right)  \cup  {\nu }^{\prime }\left( \ell \right)$ for every $\ell  \in  \Omega$ . With these definitions,we can enumerate a single valuation $\nu  \in  {\left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }_{\text{prod }}$ by calculating the union between a valuation ${\nu }_{n} \in  U\left( \left\{  \left\{  {\nu }_{L\left( n\right) ,i\left( n\right) }\right\}  \right\}  \right)$ and ${\nu }_{{n}^{\prime }} \in  \operatorname{prod}\left( {n}^{\prime }\right)$ for each ${n}^{\prime } \in  \operatorname{prod}\left( {n}^{\prime }\right)$ . It is easy to see that we can complete (2) by both calculating and writing this valuation in linear time. It is worth noting that we can make sure that we find valuations inside of the time window in constant time by traversing every bag in reverse order (starting from the valuations with a higher to lower min $\{ \nu \} )$ .

另一方面，两个估值包$V,{V}^{\prime }$的乘积被定义为包$V \oplus  {V}^{\prime } =$$\left\{  \left\{  {\nu  \oplus  {\nu }^{\prime } \mid  \nu  \in  V,{\nu }^{\prime } \in  {V}^{\prime }}\right\}  \right\}$，其中$\nu  \oplus  {\nu }^{\prime }$是两个估值的乘积，定义为一个满足以下条件的估值：对于每个$\ell  \in  \Omega$都有$\left\lbrack  {\nu  \oplus  {\nu }^{\prime }}\right\rbrack  \left( \ell \right)  = \nu \left( \ell \right)  \cup  {\nu }^{\prime }\left( \ell \right)$。根据这些定义，我们可以通过计算估值${\nu }_{n} \in  U\left( \left\{  \left\{  {\nu }_{L\left( n\right) ,i\left( n\right) }\right\}  \right\}  \right)$与${\nu }_{{n}^{\prime }} \in  \operatorname{prod}\left( {n}^{\prime }\right)$的并集来枚举单一估值$\nu  \in  {\left\lbrack  n\right\rbrack  }_{\text{prod }}$（针对每个${n}^{\prime } \in  \operatorname{prod}\left( {n}^{\prime }\right)$）。显然，我们能在线性时间内完成该估值的计算和写入，从而完善(2)式。值得注意的是，通过按逆序遍历每个包（从具有较高到较低min$\{ \nu \} )$的估值开始），我们能在常数时间内确保找到时间窗口内的估值。

After enumerating the first output, we can continue traversing the bags of valuations, checking in constant time if $\left| {i - \min \left\{  {\nu }_{{\mathbf{n}}^{\prime }}\right\}   \leq  w}\right|$ .
