In the worst case,which will occur right after writing the last valuation in the output,we will have to check that $\left| {i - \min \left\{  {\nu }_{{n}^{\prime }}\right\}   \leq  w}\right|$ for every node ${n}^{\prime } \in  \operatorname{prod}\left( n\right)$ ,but since each check takes constant time and there is one node for each valuation we are adding to the output, this step can also be done in linear time with respect to $\left| \nu \right|$ . Finally,after enumerating every output of prod( $n$ ) inside the time window,we can recursively start the enumeration for uleft(n) and uright(n) in constant time, which will maintain an output-linear delay.

枚举首个输出后，我们可以持续遍历估值包，并在常数时间内检查$\left| {i - \min \left\{  {\nu }_{{\mathbf{n}}^{\prime }}\right\}   \leq  w}\right|$。最坏情况发生在写入输出中最后一个估值后，此时需要为每个节点${n}^{\prime } \in  \operatorname{prod}\left( n\right)$检查$\left| {i - \min \left\{  {\nu }_{{n}^{\prime }}\right\}   \leq  w}\right|$。但由于每次检查耗时恒定，且每个输出估值对应一个节点，此步骤相对于$\left| \nu \right|$仍保持线性时间复杂度。最终，在枚举时间窗口内prod($n$)的所有输出后，我们能在常数时间内递归启动uleft(n)和uright(n)的枚举，从而维持输出线性延迟。

## Proof of Proposition 5.3

## 命题5.3的证明

Proof. Fix $k,w \in  \mathbb{N}$ and assume that one performs union $\left( {{\mathrm{n}}_{1},{\mathrm{n}}_{2}}\right)$ over ${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$ with the same position $i = i\left( {\mathrm{n}}_{2}\right)$ at most $k$ times. In the following,we first prove the proposition with an implementation of the union operation that is not fully persistent and then show how to modify the implementation to maintain this property.

证明。固定$k,w \in  \mathbb{N}$并假设对具有相同位置$i = i\left( {\mathrm{n}}_{2}\right)$的${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$执行最多$k$次联合操作$\left( {{\mathrm{n}}_{1},{\mathrm{n}}_{2}}\right)$。下文将首先通过非完全持久化的联合操作实现来证明该命题，随后说明如何修改实现以维持此性质。

Let ${n}_{1},{n}_{2} \in  \operatorname{Nodes}\left( {\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}\right)$ be two nodes such that $\max \left( {n}_{1}\right)  \leq  i\left( {n}_{2}\right)$ and $\operatorname{uleft}\left( {n}_{2}\right)  = \operatorname{uright}\left( {n}_{2}\right)  =  \bot$ . We say that ${n}_{1} \leq  {n}_{2}$ iff (1) max-start $\left( {n}_{1}\right)  \leq  \operatorname{max-start}\left( {n}_{2}\right)$ and (2) if max-start $\left( {n}_{1}\right)  = \operatorname{max-start}\left( {n}_{2}\right)$ then $i\left( {\mathrm{n}}_{1}\right)  \leq  i\left( {\mathrm{n}}_{2}\right)$ .

设${n}_{1},{n}_{2} \in  \operatorname{Nodes}\left( {\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}\right)$为两个节点，满足$\max \left( {n}_{1}\right)  \leq  i\left( {n}_{2}\right)$且$\operatorname{uleft}\left( {n}_{2}\right)  = \operatorname{uright}\left( {n}_{2}\right)  =  \bot$。当且仅当满足以下条件时我们称${n}_{1} \leq  {n}_{2}$：(1) 最大起始时间$\left( {n}_{1}\right)  \leq  \operatorname{max-start}\left( {n}_{2}\right)$；(2) 若最大起始时间$\left( {n}_{1}\right)  = \operatorname{max-start}\left( {n}_{2}\right)$，则$i\left( {\mathrm{n}}_{1}\right)  \leq  i\left( {\mathrm{n}}_{2}\right)$。

Recall that this operation requires inserting ${n}_{2}$ into ${n}_{1}$ and it outputs a fresh node ${n}_{u}$ such that ${\left\lbrack  {\mathrm{n}}_{u}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w} \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\left\lbrack  {\mathrm{n}}_{1}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w} \cup  {\left\lbrack  {\mathrm{n}}_{2}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}.$

需注意该操作要求将${n}_{2}$插入${n}_{1}$，并输出一个新节点${n}_{u}$使得${\left\lbrack  {\mathrm{n}}_{u}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w} \mathrel{\text{:=}} {\left\lbrack  {\mathrm{n}}_{1}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w} \cup  {\left\lbrack  {\mathrm{n}}_{2}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}.$

If $\left| {\max  - \operatorname{start}\left( {{n}_{1} - i\left( {n}_{2}\right) }\right) }\right|  > w$ then all of the outputs from ${n}_{1}$ are now out of the time window,so ${\left\lbrack  {n}_{1}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w} \cup  {\left\lbrack  {n}_{2}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w} = {\left\lbrack  {n}_{2}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}$ and therefore union $\left( {{n}_{1},{n}_{2}}\right)  = {n}_{2}$ . The time it will take to do this operation will be the time necessary to insert ${\mathrm{n}}_{2}$ in ${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$ ,which we will analyze later.

若$\left| {\max  - \operatorname{start}\left( {{n}_{1} - i\left( {n}_{2}\right) }\right) }\right|  > w$，则${n}_{1}$的所有输出均已超出时间窗口，故${\left\lbrack  {n}_{1}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w} \cup  {\left\lbrack  {n}_{2}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w} = {\left\lbrack  {n}_{2}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}$，因此合并$\left( {{n}_{1},{n}_{2}}\right)  = {n}_{2}$。此操作所需时间即为将${\mathrm{n}}_{2}$插入${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$所需时间，我们将在后文分析。

On the other hand,if $\left| {\max  - \operatorname{start}\left( {{n}_{1} - i\left( {n}_{2}\right) }\right) }\right|  \leq  w$ ,we have to consider the outputs of both nodes, ${n}_{2}$ and ${n}_{1}$ . First check how ${n}_{1}$ compares with ${n}_{2}$ . If ${n}_{1} \leq  {n}_{2}$ ,then we have to create the new node $n = {n}_{2}$ such that $\operatorname{uleft}\left( \mathrm{n}\right)  = \operatorname{union}\left( {\operatorname{uleft}\left( {\mathrm{n}}_{2}\right) ,{\mathrm{n}}_{1}}\right)$ and,similarly,if ${\mathrm{n}}_{1} > {\mathrm{n}}_{2}$ ,we need to create the new node $\mathrm{n} = {\mathrm{n}}_{1}$ such that $\operatorname{uleft}\left( \mathbf{n}\right)  = \operatorname{union}\left( {\operatorname{uleft}\left( {\mathbf{n}}_{1}\right) ,{\mathbf{n}}_{2}}\right)$ . In both cases,we are only creating one node and switching or adding pointers between nodes a constant number of times. Although it might seem like a recursive operation at first glance,we know beforehand that $\operatorname{max-start}\left( n\right)  \geq  \operatorname{max-start}\left( {\operatorname{uleft}\left( n\right) }\right)$ ,so there will be at most one other union process generated. Once again since we can do this part of the operation in constant time and the bulk of the operation will be the time necessary for the insertion of the new node.

另一方面，若$\left| {\max  - \operatorname{start}\left( {{n}_{1} - i\left( {n}_{2}\right) }\right) }\right|  \leq  w$，则需同时考虑节点${n}_{2}$和${n}_{1}$的输出。首先比较${n}_{1}$与${n}_{2}$的关系。若${n}_{1} \leq  {n}_{2}$，则需创建新节点$n = {n}_{2}$使得$\operatorname{uleft}\left( \mathrm{n}\right)  = \operatorname{union}\left( {\operatorname{uleft}\left( {\mathrm{n}}_{2}\right) ,{\mathrm{n}}_{1}}\right)$；同理，若${\mathrm{n}}_{1} > {\mathrm{n}}_{2}$，则需创建新节点$\mathrm{n} = {\mathrm{n}}_{1}$使得$\operatorname{uleft}\left( \mathbf{n}\right)  = \operatorname{union}\left( {\operatorname{uleft}\left( {\mathbf{n}}_{1}\right) ,{\mathbf{n}}_{2}}\right)$。这两种情况都仅需创建单个节点，并以恒定次数调整节点间指针。虽然初看像是递归操作，但我们预先知道$\operatorname{max-start}\left( n\right)  \geq  \operatorname{max-start}\left( {\operatorname{uleft}\left( n\right) }\right)$，因此最多只会产生一次额外的合并过程。由于该操作部分可在常数时间内完成，其主要耗时仍在于新节点的插入。

We know that for every position $i = i\left( {\mathrm{n}}_{2}\right)$ we will perform a union operation at most $k$ times. Starting with an empty data structure,there will be at most $k \cdot  w$ nodes in ${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$ given a time window $w$ . Assuming ${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$ is a perfectly balanced binary tree,this means that the tree has a depth of ${\log }_{2}\left( {k \cdot  w}\right)$ .

已知每个位置$i = i\left( {\mathrm{n}}_{2}\right)$最多执行$k$次合并操作。从空数据结构开始，在时间窗口$w$内，${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$中的节点数最多为$k \cdot  w$。假设${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$是完全平衡二叉树，则其深度为${\log }_{2}\left( {k \cdot  w}\right)$。

To ensure that the tree will always be balanced, we can add one bit of information to every node, which we will call the direction bit, that indicates which of the children of the node we need to visit for the insertion. If $\operatorname{bit}\left( \mathrm{n}\right)  = 0$ ,we must go to its left child and we must go to the right one otherwise. After each insertion, we need to change the value of the direction bit of every node in the path from the root to the newly inserted one, to avoid repeating the same path on the next insertion. This operation can be done in constant time for each node, so the time it will take to update all of the direction bits for each insertion will be exactly the depth of the tree.

为确保树始终保持平衡，可为每个节点添加方向位信息，指示插入时需要访问的子节点方向。若$\operatorname{bit}\left( \mathrm{n}\right)  = 0$则进入左子树，否则进入右子树。每次插入后，需翻转从根节点到新插入节点路径上所有方向位的值，以避免下次插入时重复相同路径。每个节点的方向位更新可在常数时间内完成，因此每次插入的更新总时间精确等于树的深度。

Since one performs union $\left( {{\mathrm{n}}_{1},{\mathrm{n}}_{2}}\right)$ over ${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$ with the same position $i = i\left( {\mathrm{n}}_{2}\right)$ at most $k$ times,if we start with an empty data structure,it will have at most $k \times  w$ nodes after reading $w$ tuples from the stream. To insert the next node ${\mathrm{n}}^{\prime }$ ,by following the direction bits,we will end up in the oldest node of the tree $n$ ,but it is clear that $i\left( n\right)  \leq  i\left( {n}^{\prime }\right)  + w$ ,meaning that $\operatorname{max-start}\left( n\right)  - i\left( n\right)  \leq  w$ which indicates that all of the outputs of $n$ are outside of the time window and therefore we can safely remove $n$ from the tree and replace it with ${\mathrm{n}}^{\prime }$ without losing outputs. Given that the depth of ${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$ is at most $k \cdot  w$ ,and all of our previous operations take time proportional to the depth of the tree, we can conclude that the running time of the union operation is in $\mathcal{O}\left( {\log \left( {k \cdot  w}\right) }\right)$ for each call.

由于对同一位置$i = i\left( {\mathrm{n}}_{2}\right)$最多执行$k$次联合操作$\left( {{\mathrm{n}}_{1},{\mathrm{n}}_{2}}\right)$，若从空数据结构开始，在读取$w$个元组后，该结构最多包含$k \times  w$个节点。插入新节点${\mathrm{n}}^{\prime }$时，沿方向位引导将抵达树中最旧节点$n$，但显然$i\left( n\right)  \leq  i\left( {n}^{\prime }\right)  + w$意味着$\operatorname{max-start}\left( n\right)  - i\left( n\right)  \leq  w$，这表明$n$的所有输出均超出时间窗口范围，因此可安全移除$n$并用${\mathrm{n}}^{\prime }$替换而不丢失输出。鉴于${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$的深度不超过$k \cdot  w$，且所有先前操作耗时与树深成正比，可得出每次联合操作的运行时间属于$\mathcal{O}\left( {\log \left( {k \cdot  w}\right) }\right)$。

As we stated before,although the method we just discussed works and has a running time in $\mathcal{O}(\log (k$ $w))$ for each call,it is not a fully persistent implementation,since we are removing nodes from the leaves when they are not producing an output and we are also modifying the direction bits of the nodes. To solve this problem, we can use the path copying method. With this method, whenever we need to modify a node, we create a copy with the modifications applied instead.

如前所述，尽管刚讨论的方法有效且每次调用运行时间为$\mathcal{O}(\log (k$$w))$，但并非完全持久化实现——因我们会移除无输出的叶节点并修改节点的方向位。为解决此问题，可采用路径复制法：需要修改节点时，创建包含修改的新副本替代原节点。

In our case, for every insertion we will create a copy of the entire path from the root to the new node, since we will modify the direction bit of each of these nodes, setting the modified copy of the root as the new root of the data structure. It is easy to see that with clever use of pointers, the copying of a node can be done in constant time, so the usage of this method does not increase the overall running time of the union operation.

本方案中，每次插入都将复制从根节点到新节点的完整路径（因需修改路径上各节点的方向位），并将修改后的根节点副本设为数据结构新根。显然通过智能指针运用，节点复制可恒定时间内完成，故该方法不会增加联合操作的整体运行时间。

## Proof of Proposition 5.4

## 命题5.4证明

Proof. Fix a time window size $w \in  \mathbb{N}$ a stream $\mathcal{S}$ ,a position $i \in  \mathbb{N}$ and an PCEA with equality predicates $\mathcal{P} = \left( {Q,{\mathbf{U}}_{\text{lin }},{\mathbf{B}}_{\text{eq }},\Omega ,\Delta ,F}\right)$ . The output of the automaton $\mathcal{P}$ over $\mathcal{S}$ at position $i$ with time window $w$ is defined as the set of valuations:

证明。固定时间窗口大小$w \in  \mathbb{N}$、流$\mathcal{S}$、位置$i \in  \mathbb{N}$及带相等谓词$\mathcal{P} = \left( {Q,{\mathbf{U}}_{\text{lin }},{\mathbf{B}}_{\text{eq }},\Omega ,\Delta ,F}\right)$的PCEA。自动机$\mathcal{P}$在位置$i$处关于流$\mathcal{S}$和时间窗口$w$的输出定义为赋值集合：

$$
{\left\lbrack  \mathcal{P}\right\rbrack  }_{i}^{w}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)  = \left\{  {{\nu }_{\rho } \mid  \rho \text{ is an accepting run of }\mathcal{P}\text{ over }S\text{ at position }i \land  \left| {i - \min \left( \nu \right) }\right|  \leq  w}\right\}  
$$

We need to prove that Algorithm 1 enumerates every valuation ${\nu }_{\rho }$ without repetitions. One way to do this is showing that at any position in the stream the indices in $H$ contain the information of every single run of $\mathcal{P}$ so far showing that the outputs for each of these runs can be enumerated.

需证明算法1能无重复枚举每个赋值${\nu }_{\rho }$。可通过证明流中任意位置处，$H$中的索引均包含$\mathcal{P}$至今所有运行轨迹信息，从而说明这些轨迹的输出均可被枚举。

- Let $i = 0$ and suppose that $\mathcal{S} = \{ \{ R\left( \bar{x}\right) ,\ldots \} \} .H$ trivially contains the information of all the runs up to this point, so we need to show that this condition still holds after the first tuple.

- 设$i = 0$并假设$\mathcal{S} = \{ \{ R\left( \bar{x}\right) ,\ldots \} \} .H$显然包含至此所有运行轨迹信息，故需证明处理首个元组后该条件仍成立。

Looking at the algorithm,after the RESET call,we start with $i = 0,{\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w} = \varnothing ,{N}_{p} = \varnothing$ for every $p \in  Q$ . Calling FIRETRANSITIONS $\left( {R\left( \bar{x}\right) ,0}\right)$ we check each transitions satisfied by $R\left( \bar{x}\right)$ and we register them in nodes for ${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$ . Since $\mathcal{P}$ is unambiguous,there is only one transition that can lead to an accepting state, ${e}_{f} = \left( {\varnothing ,U,\varnothing ,{L}_{f},{p}_{f}}\right)  \in  \Delta$ with ${p}_{f} \in  F$ and for ${e}_{f}$ we have $N = \{ \}$ and ${N}_{{p}_{f}} = \operatorname{extend}\left( {{L}_{f},0,\{ \} }\right)$ . In addition, $\mathcal{P}$ can take (several)transitions that do not lead to a final state; these would be transitions of the form $e = \left( {\varnothing ,U,\varnothing ,L,p}\right)  \in  \Delta$ with ${N}_{p} = \operatorname{extend}\left( {L,0,\{ \} }\right)$ .

观察该算法，在执行RESET调用后，我们为每个$p \in  Q$初始化$i = 0,{\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w} = \varnothing ,{N}_{p} = \varnothing$。调用FIRETRANSITIONS$\left( {R\left( \bar{x}\right) ,0}\right)$时，会检查所有被$R\left( \bar{x}\right)$满足的转移条件，并将其注册到${\mathrm{{DS}}}_{w}$的节点中。由于$\mathcal{P}$具有唯一性，仅存在一个能到达接受状态的转移——即带有${p}_{f} \in  F$的${e}_{f} = \left( {\varnothing ,U,\varnothing ,{L}_{f},{p}_{f}}\right)  \in  \Delta$，而对于${e}_{f}$则存在$N = \{ \}$和${N}_{{p}_{f}} = \operatorname{extend}\left( {{L}_{f},0,\{ \} }\right)$。此外，$\mathcal{P}$可能执行（若干）无法到达终态的转移，这类转移形式为带有${N}_{p} = \operatorname{extend}\left( {L,0,\{ \} }\right)$的$e = \left( {\varnothing ,U,\varnothing ,L,p}\right)  \in  \Delta$。

On the other hand,UPDATEINDICES $\left( {R\left( \bar{x}\right) }\right)$ uses the nodes created in FIRETRANSITIONS and assigns them to every possible transition that could be satisfied by them. In particular, for every reached state $p$ ,we add each node in ${\mathrm{N}}_{p}$ to the data structure $H\left\lbrack  {e,p,{\overline{\mathcal{B}}}_{p}\left( t\right) }\right\rbrack$ ,registering every incomplete run of $\mathcal{P}$ .

另一方面，UPDATEINDICES$\left( {R\left( \bar{x}\right) }\right)$会利用FIRETRANSITIONS创建的节点，将它们分配给所有可能被满足的转移条件。具体而言，对于每个到达状态$p$，我们会将${\mathrm{N}}_{p}$中的节点加入数据结构$H\left\lbrack  {e,p,{\overline{\mathcal{B}}}_{p}\left( t\right) }\right\rbrack$，以此记录$\mathcal{P}$的所有不完整运行路径。

Finally,we enumerate the outputs of each run that reached a final state. Since $\mathcal{P}$ is unambiguous, this enumeration will not have duplicates. It is easy to see that enumerate will output our only valuation since ${p}_{f} \in  F$ .

最后，我们枚举所有到达终态的运行路径输出。由于$\mathcal{P}$具有唯一性，该枚举结果不会出现重复项。显然，枚举过程将输出我们唯一的赋值方案，因为${p}_{f} \in  F$。

- Suppose that $H$ contains the information of every single run of $\mathcal{P}$ up until position $i - 1$ and that $\mathcal{S}\left\lbrack  i\right\rbrack   = S\left( \bar{y}\right)$ and that we can enumerate every valuation in $\llbracket \mathcal{P}{\rrbracket }_{i - 1}^{w}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$ . We want to prove that after calling FIRETRANSITIONS $\left( {S\left( \bar{y}\right) ,i}\right)$ and UPDATEINDICES $\left( {S\left( \bar{y}\right) }\right) ,H$ will also contain the information of the runs up until $i$ .

- 假设$H$包含$\mathcal{P}$在位置$i - 1$之前所有运行路径的信息，且$\mathcal{S}\left\lbrack  i\right\rbrack   = S\left( \bar{y}\right)$成立，同时我们能够枚举$\llbracket \mathcal{P}{\rrbracket }_{i - 1}^{w}\left( \mathcal{S}\right)$中的每个赋值。需要证明的是：在调用FIRETRANSITIONS$\left( {S\left( \bar{y}\right) ,i}\right)$和UPDATEINDICES$\left( {S\left( \bar{y}\right) }\right) ,H$后，该数据结构也将包含截至$i$位置的运行路径信息。

Once again we start with ${\mathrm{N}}_{p} = \varnothing$ for each $p \in  Q$ ,but this time $H\left\lbrack  {e,p,{\overline{\mathcal{B}}}_{p}\left( t\right) }\right\rbrack$ is not empty. Similar to the previous case,upon calling FIRETRANSITIONS $\left( {S\left( \bar{y}\right) ,i}\right)$ ,we create a new node for every new state reached by any of the runs and it is clear by the definition of $\Delta$ and ${\overline{\mathcal{B}}}_{p}\left( t\right)$ that $S\left( \bar{y}\right)  \in  U$ and $\mathop{\bigwedge }\limits_{{p \in  P}}\mathrm{H}\left\lbrack  {e,p,{\overrightarrow{\mathcal{B}}}_{p}\left( t\right) }\right\rbrack   \neq  \varnothing$ for a transition $e = \left( {P,U,\mathcal{B},L,q}\right)  \in  \Delta$ iff there is a run tree $\rho$ and a node $\bar{u}$ such that $\rho \left( \bar{u}\right)  = \left( {q,i,L}\right)$ .

我们再次从每个$p \in  Q$的${\mathrm{N}}_{p} = \varnothing$开始，但这次$H\left\lbrack  {e,p,{\overline{\mathcal{B}}}_{p}\left( t\right) }\right\rbrack$非空。与之前情况类似，当调用FIRETRANSITIONS$\left( {S\left( \bar{y}\right) ,i}\right)$时，我们会为每个运行到达的新状态创建新节点。根据$\Delta$和${\overline{\mathcal{B}}}_{p}\left( t\right)$的定义可明确：当且仅当存在运行树$\rho$和节点$\bar{u}$使得$\rho \left( \bar{u}\right)  = \left( {q,i,L}\right)$时，转换$e = \left( {P,U,\mathcal{B},L,q}\right)  \in  \Delta$的$S\left( \bar{y}\right)  \in  U$和$\mathop{\bigwedge }\limits_{{p \in  P}}\mathrm{H}\left\lbrack  {e,p,{\overrightarrow{\mathcal{B}}}_{p}\left( t\right) }\right\rbrack   \neq  \varnothing$成立。

In the same fashion,UPDATEINDICES $\left( {S\left( \bar{y}\right) }\right)$ will thoroughly calculate for each transition and each state in those transitions the left projection of the binary predicate for $S\left( \bar{y}\right)$ ,maintaining the data structure in $H$ updated with the runs od $\mathcal{P}$ .

同理，UPDATEINDICES $\left( {S\left( \bar{y}\right) }\right)$将为每个转移及其中的每个状态彻底计算二元谓词对$S\left( \bar{y}\right)$的左投影，并通过$\mathcal{P}$的运行轨迹保持$H$中数据结构的更新。

Finally, the algorithm was already capable of enumerating every valuation that ends in a position $j < i$ ,and we get from FIRETRANSITIONS $\left( {S\left( \bar{y}\right) ,i}\right)$ that every new accepting run will have its associated nodes.

最终，该算法已能枚举所有终止于位置$j < i$的估值，且从FIRETRANSITIONS $\left( {S\left( \bar{y}\right) ,i}\right)$可知，每个新接受的运行轨迹都将获得其关联节点。
